# Homilies IV–VIII (Omelie IV-VIII)

**Author**: Sévère Ibn-al-Moqaffa`  
**Series**: PO Tome 57  
**Columns**: pp. 130–336  

[p. 130] {#po.57.p130}

### HOMILY IV
ድርሳን ፡ራብዕ።
Anamnesis of the previous homily
ድርሳን ፡፣ ራብዕ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ከሚተ፤ ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ፍስሕ ፡ ወበግዕ ፡ ወእፎ ፡ ይከውን ፡፡ኅብስተ ፡ወወይነ፡ ፡ሥጋሁ ፡ወደሙ ፣ለክርስቶስ ። ናሁ ፡ከሠትኩ ፣ ለከ፣ ኦፍቁር ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ያብርህ ፡ አዕይንተ ፡ ልበከ' ፡ በብርሃነ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ናዛዚ ፡ከመ ፡ትለቡ ፡ ምሥጢራተ! ፡መለኮቱ፡ ፡ውስተ ፡መጽሐፍ ፡ዘእምቅድመ፤ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኩሎ ፣ ትርጓሜ ፡ ቃላት ፡ ብዙኅ" ፡ በኦሪት"! ፡ ወኢያሱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ነዌ ። ወአጠየቁ ፡ ለከ ፣ ከመ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ኮኑ ፣ አምሳለ ፣ ወአርአያ ፣ ለም ግባራተ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወኢከሠትኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ፍሥሕ ፣፤ ወበግዕ ፡ወተካየድኩከ ፡ከመ ፡እተርጉም ፡ለከ ። ለቡ ፡ ይእዜ፤! ፡ ዘአነ ፡ እከሥት ፡ ለከ ፡ እምዝንቱ ። ወውእቱኬ ፣ እስመ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ሶበ ፡ኮኑ' በምድረ ፡ግብጽ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ግብርናተ ፡ፈርያዖን፣ ወሠራዊቱ ። ወፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያግዕዞሙ ፡ እምዝክቱ ፡ ፈነዎ፣ ለሙሴ ፡ ወዘበጦ ፡ ለፈርዖን ፡ ወሠራዊቱ" ፡ በ8 ፣፡ መቅሠፍታት ፣ ዘከሠትኩ ፡ ለከ፣ በውስተ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ዘእም ፡ ቅድመ ፣ ዝንቱ ። ወኢፈቀደ ፣ ፈርያዖን፣ ከመ ፣ ይፈንዎሙ ። ወይቤሎ” ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ አሐተ! ፡ ቅሥፈተ ፣ ዝብጥ"" ፣ ባቲ ፣ ለፈርዖን ፣ ወሠራዊቱ ። ወውእቱ ፣ ይፌንዎሙ ። ወአዝዞሙ ፣ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሣየጡ ፡ ለለ8፳ ፡ ቤት ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ በግዓ፣ ፍጹመ ፡ዘእንበለ ፡ነውር ።
1 Fourth Homily: concerning the exposition of the hermeneutics of the Passover and the lamb, and how the bread and wine become the body and blood of Christ. I have therefore manifested to you, O beloved, that the Lord may illuminate the eyes of your mind with the light of the Holy Spirit the Comforter, in the book that precedes this, all the hermeneutics of the copious words in the Torah and in Joshua son of Nun, so that you may understand the mysteries of His divinity. I have also confirmed to you that all these were types and likenesses of the actions of our Lord Jesus Christ. I did not disclose to you the hermeneutics of the Passover and the lamb, but I promised you that I would explain it to you. Prolepsis to the subject of the fourth homily. The Paschal lamb 2 Now, pay attention to what I will manifest to you of this, namely, that when the children of Israel lived in the land of Egypt under the slavery of Pharaoh and his army, and God willed to liberate them from that, He sent Moses who struck Pharaoh and his army with nine punishments that I manifested to you in the book that precedes this. But Pharaoh did not want to release them (Ex 11:10). And the Lord said to Moses: “Inflict a blow upon Pharaoh and his army, and he will release them. Order the children of Israel to acquire for each of their houses a perfect lamb, without blemish.


[p. 132] {#po.57.p132}
ለእመ ፡ኮኑ፤ ፣ ሰብአ ፡ ቤት ፡ ኢይብልዑ፡ ፡ ብግዓ ፡ አላ ፡ ይሳተፉ ፡ ምስለ፣ ካልእ ፣ ቤት ፡ ቦቱ ። ወይሣየጥዎ ፣ ለዝንቱ ፡ በግዕ ፡ እምዐፉሩ፣ መዐልት፤፣ ወይዕቀብዎ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ ፅለተ ፡ ዐሥሩ ፡ ወረቡዑ ። ይጥብሕዎ ፡ በዕርበተ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ በምሴተ" ፡ ዐሥሩ ፡ ወረቡዑ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይበውእ ፡ ሌሊት" ፡ ዐሥሙሩ፣ ወኅሙሱ ። ይቅብዑ ፡ ደሞ ፣ ዲበ ፡ መድረካት ፣ አናቅጸ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ፡ ዘመ ልዕልት ፣ወዘመትሕት ። እስመ ፣ መንፈቀ ፡ሌሊት ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት" ፡ እፌኑ፤!፣ መልአከ ፡፣ ሙስና" ፡ ቅትሎሙ ፣ ለበኩራተ" ፣ ፈርዖን ፣ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ይኔጽር ፡ ደመ ፡ በዲበ" ፡ አናቅጸ ፡ አብያተ ፣ ደቂቀ” ፡ እስራኤል፤' ፣ ወያእም ሮሙ" ፣ቦቱ ። ወኢይባእ” ኀቤሆሙ ፣ወኢያጥፍእ ፡በኩሮሙ ፡ አላ ፡ያጠፍኦሙ ፡ ለአብያተ ፣ ግብጻውያን ፡ ወሠራዊተ ፡ ፈርዖን” ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ደም ፡ ይከውኖ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ወሊተኒ ፡ አነ ፡ ካዕበ፡! ። እስመ” ፣ አነ” ፡ ሶበ ፡ እሬእዮ” ፡
3 “If the family members do not eat a lamb, let them participate in it with another family. Let them acquire this lamb from the tenth day and keep it with them until the fourteenth day. Let them slaughter it at the setting of the sun, on the evening of the fourteenth day, when the night of the fifteenth day enters. Let them anoint (with) its blood the upper and lower doorposts of their houses, because at midnight of that night, I will send the angel of destruction so that he may kill the firstborn of Pharaoh and his army. When he sees the blood on the doors of the children of Israel, he will recognize them through it, so as not to enter among them and not to destroy their firstborn, but he will destroy the houses of the Egyptians and the army of Pharaoh. That blood will in fact be a sign for him and also for me, since I, when I see the blood on the doors of their houses, will hide them and will not let the destroyer enter among them.
ለደም ፡ዲበ ፡ አናቅጸ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ፡ እሴውሮሙ” ፣ ወኢያኅድጎ” ፡ ለዘያማስን ፡ ይባእ” ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይጠብሕዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ በግፅ ፡ ኢይብልዑ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ዘብሱል ፣ በማይ ፡ አላ ፣ ጥብሶ ፡ በእሳት ፡ ወይበልፅዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ቅኑት” ፣፣ ሐቄሆሙ ፣ ወአብትሪሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ወአሣእኒሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እገሪሆሙ ። ወይብልፅዎ ፡፣ ፍጡነ ፡ ወኢያትርፉ ፣ እምኔሁ ፡ 'እስከ ፡ ሳኒታ ፡ ወኢይስብሩ ፣ ዐፅሞ” ፡ ወኢምንተኒ"! ፣ ወኢያፍልስዎ ፣ እምቤት ፡ እስከ ፡ ቤት ። ወይበልዑ ፣ ርእሶ ፣ ወከርሦ ፣ ወእገሪሁ ። ወዘተርፈ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡፣ ዘኢይትከሀል ፡ ይብልዕዎ ፣ ያውዕፅይዎ ፡በእሳት ። እስመ ፡በይእቲ ፣ሌሊት” ፡አግዓዞሙ” ፣እምድረ ፡ግብጽ፡፣ እምግብርናተ ፡ ፈርዖን ፡ይግበሩ ፡ዘንተ” ፡ግብረ ፡በይእቲ ፡ዕለት” ፡ለለዓመት ፡
4 “When they slaughter this lamb, let them not eat its meat boiled in water, but the meat roasted with fire; they shall eat it with their loins girded, and with their staffs in their hands, and their sandals on their feet. Let them eat it quickly and let them not leave any remainder of it until the following day and let them not break its bone. Let them not transfer it from one house to another. They shall eat its head, its entrails, and its feet. What is left over from it that they were not able to consume, let them burn with fire.
ከመ ፡ይዘከሩ” ፡ እፎ ፡ አውፃእክዎሙ ፡በእድ ፡ጽንዕት ፡ወበመዝራዕት ፡፡ኀይልት ፡ ወልዕልት” ። እስመ ፡ይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ፅለተ ፡ፍሥሐ ፡እግዚአብሔር ። "ግበርዎ ፡እስከ” ፡ ለትውልደ ፣ ትውልድክሙ ። ወይእቲ ፡ሌሊት ፡ሌሊተ ፡ወርኀ” ፡ እግዚአብሔር ።
5 “In that night, in fact, I will liberate them from the land of Egypt, from the slavery of Pharaoh. Let them perform this action on this day, every year, so that they may remember how I brought them out with a strong hand and with a powerful and outstretched arm.


[p. 134] {#po.57.p134}
ወሶበ ፡ይወፅኡ፤ ፡እምድረ ፡ግብጽ ፡ ኢይብልዑ ፡ብሑአ ። አላ ፡ይብልዑ?፤ ፡ናእተ ፡ ሰብዐተ ፡ መዓልተ ። ወኩሉ!፤ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ዘትበልዕ ፣ ብሑአ ፡ በእማንቱ ፡ መዓልት ፣ አው ፡ይትረከብ ፡በኀቤሃ ፡ብሑአ‹ ፡ትትፈለጥ" ፡ እምሕዝባ ። ዘንተ ፡ይቤሎሙ ፣ ወእሙንቱ ፡ገብሩ ፡ዘአዘዞሙ ፡ቦቱ።
Indeed this day is the day of the Passover of the Lord. Perform it from generation to generation. This night is a night of the month of the Lord. When they go out from the land of Egypt, let them not eat leaven, but let them eat unleavened bread for seven days. Every soul that eats leaven in those days, or that leaven is found with it, let it be cut off from its people.” This He said to them, and they fulfilled what He had commanded them.

### Hermeneutics of the Paschal lamb: general indications
ወውእቱ ፡ "ኩሉ ፡ምሳሌ ።ፖ ለአድኅኖቶሙ "። ወበከመ፤ ፡ ተርጐምክዎ ፡ለከ ፡ በመጽሐፍ ፡ዘእምቅድመ ፡ ዝንቱ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ ከመ ፡ደቂቀ ፡ አዳም ፡ሶበ ፡ኮኑ ፣ ታሕተ ፡ግብርናተ ፣ዲያብሎስ ፡ወሠራዊቱ ፡ውስተ ፡ዓለም ፡ወውስተ"'የ ፡ሲኦል ። ፈነወ፤! ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወልዶ ፡ከመ ፣ይረስዮሙ፤ ፡ አግዓዝያነ ፡ እምግብርናቱ ፡ ወይሜይጦሙ”፤ ፡ ለገነት ፡ ወለመንግሥተ” ፡ ሰማያት ። ወዘበጦ ፡ ለዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ" ፡ በመቅሠፍታት ፡ ዘነገርኩከ ፡ በመጽሐፈ ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ወስቅለቱ ። ኀብአ ፡ ፣ርእሶ ፣ እምኔሁ" ፡ በምግባራተ'' ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘከሠትኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ እስከ ፣ ኀለየ ፣ከመ ፡-ብእሲ ፡ ድኩም ። ወአድኃኖ ፡ ለአዳም ፡እምኔሁ ፡በክዕወተ ፡ ደሙ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በግዑ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ነውር ፣ ባሕቲቱ።
6 All this is a type of their salvation. As I interpreted for you in the book that precedes this, namely, that when the children of Adam were under the slavery of the devil and his army, in the world and in Sheol, the Lord sent His Son to render them free from his slavery and to bring them back to Paradise and to the Kingdom of Heaven. He struck the devil and his army with the punishments that I exposed to you in the book of the explanation of His incarnation and crucifixion. He hid Himself from him through human actions that I illustrated to you, so much so that (the devil) thought that (the Son) was a weak man. He saved Adam from him (i.e., from the devil) with the shedding of His blood, because He is the lamb of God, the only perfect one, without blemish (1 Pt 1:19). Immolations of lambs for sin
እስመ” ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ይሠዑ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ኃጣውኢሆምጮ ፣ በግዓ ፣ ንጹሐ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ነውር ። ወሶበ ፡ አበሰ ፡ አሐዱ ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡፣ ወየአምር”" ፣ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ የርኩስ ፣ ወደለዎ፤ ፡ እምኀበ ፡ እግዚ
7 The Lord ordered the children of Israel to sacrifice for their sins the immaculate lamb, without blemish. “If any of them sinned and was aware of being impure and was deserving of death from God,

let him take a pure lamb, without blemish, and let him (or them) enter before God, he and the lamb, being deserving of death because of his impurity” (Lv 5:5). As for the lamb, however, it does not deserve death because it is purer than him. Let him slaughter his lamb in place of his death sentence which he deserves. Thus did our Lord Jesus Christ with all the children of Adam. For Adam sinned and became a slave of the devil and deserved death and condemnation together with the devil for eternity.

### Jesus the sacrificial lamb immolated for Adam and for his offspring
ውእቱ ፡ ወኩሉ ፣ ዘርኡ ፡ እለ ፡ ተወልዱ ፡ እምፍትወቱ ፡ ዘአበሱ፤ ፡ ወዘኢ አበሱ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አግብርት ፡ ለዲያብሎስ ፡ እስመ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ እምፍትወተ ፡ አዳም ፡ ገብሩ ። እስመ ፡ ዘአጥረየ ፡ ገብረ ፡ አጥረየ ፣ ውሉዶ ። ወመጽአ ፡ እግዚ እነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ በግዑ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ተሠገወ" ፡ በሥጋ" ፣ እመን ፈስ ፡ቅዱስ ፡ ወእማርያም ፡ቅድስት'! ፡ ድንግል ፡ ወአኮ ፡ እምፍትወተ ፡ አዳም" ። ወኢደለዎ ፡ ሞት ፣ ወኢኩነኔ ፡ ወአኮ ፣ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ለዲያብሎስ ፡ ኩነኔ" ፣ እስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ኢተወልደ ፡እምፍትወተ ፡አዳም ፡ገብሩ ። ወኢገብረ ፡ ኃጢአተ ፣ከመ፣ ይደልዎ ፣፡ ግብርናት ፣ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኃጢአት ፣ ወዘእንበለ” ፡ነውር ፡ዘይደልዎ" ፡ ሕይወት ፡ዘለዓለም ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ለዓለም" ። ወኢይደልዎ ፡ኩነኔ ፡ወኢሞት"'' ። ወኩሎሙ” ፡ደቂቀ ፡አዳም ፡፡፣ኃጥአን ፡ ርኩሳን ፡ ወቦሙ” ፣ ነውረ” ፣ ወይደልዎሙ”" ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወሞት ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወበእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወጾረ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ህየንቴሆሙ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዕፀ፣ መስቀል ፡ ወቤዘዎሙ” ፣ በነፍሱ” ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ወኢይደልዎ” ፡ ሞት ፡ጾረ ፡በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ሞቶሙ ። በከመ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ሶበ፣ ይኤብሱ ፣ ወይደልዎሙ” ፣ ሞት ፣ ይጠብሑ” ፣ አባግዒሆሙ ፡ ህየንቴሆሙ ፣ ይጸውሩ” ፣ ሞቶሙ ፣ ወክርስቶስ ፡ በግዑ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘውእቱ”፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ነውር ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ጾረ”' ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወሞት?! ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኃጣውአ ፣ ዓለም ።
8 He (Adam) and all his lineage that is born from his lust, those who have sinned and those who have not sinned (Rm 5:14), are slaves of the devil, because they have acted according to the lust of Adam. In fact, he who has acquired a slave has also acquired his children. Our Lord Jesus Christ came, the Lamb of God (Jn 1:29), He was incarnated in flesh from the Holy Spirit and from the Holy Virgin Mary and not from the lust of Adam. He was not deserving of death nor of condemnation, and over Him the devil had no dominion, because He was not born from the lust of Adam his servant. He did not commit sin so that He might deserve slavery, because He is the only pure one, without sin, without blemish, to whom belongs eternal life, forever. 9 Condemnation does not belong to Him, nor death. Instead, all the children of Adam are sinners, impure, and have blemish; they deserve condemnation and death for eternity. For them Christ died and in their stead He endured the condemnation on the wood of the cross and redeemed them with His soul. For He died while not deserving death, (yet) He endured in their stead their death, just as the children of Israel when they used to sin becoming deserving of death, in their stead they slaughtered their lambs (which) took upon themselves their death (Lv 16). Christ, lamb of God who is without blemish, pure, perfect, has taken upon Himself the condemnation and the death for the sins of the world (Is 53:4-5, 7; Jn 1:29).

10 When the devil dared against Him, he killed Him without it being necessary for Him (to be killed); He, however, snatched from him Adam and all his lineage without injustice, in place of His killing in justice. Christ is the lamb of God, perfect, pure, without blemish. He ransomed us from the devil and his army which is the powerful Pharaoh, with the shedding of His blood (1 Cor 5:5-7; 1 Pt 1:18-19). He saved us on the 15th day of the month which is the day of the Passover of the Jews, on which in the morning the lamb was slaughtered through which they were saved from Pharaoh and his army. Observe all that I have manifested to you from the Word of God, concerning the lamb, that it was the type of Christ, the lamb of God. For God says concerning the month, that it is a new month, and the beginning of months for you (Ex 12:2), since in it the children of Adam were renewed from the perdition in which they lived anciently.

11 They were new in the Paradise of delight, and returned to it a second time. And it is a new month, the beginning of the months of their salvation, the beginning of their eternal life. As for what He said: “Acquire a lamb ten days before, and keep it until the fourteenth day and slaughter it at the setting of the sun” (Ex 12:6), in the holy Gospel it says that the year in which Christ was crucified, the beginning of the month of the year of the Jews in which the Passover fell, was Nisan, the first day of Friday.

### The entry of Jesus into Jerusalem
ወበዕለተ ፡ እሑድ!! ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ዐሥሩ" ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ቦአ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየ ሱስ ፣ክርስቶስ ፡ውስተ ፡ሀገረ'፤ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም" ፡እንዘ ፡ ይጹዓን ፡በከመ ፡አምሳለ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወተወክፍዎ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ የደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል" ፡ ወአምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ከመ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ንጉሦሙ ፡ ዘይጸንሕዎ ፡ ለአድኅኖቶሙ ። ወያዱ" ፡ ኩሎሙ ፣ በቅድሜሁ ፡በልሳኖሙ ፡እንዘ ፡ይብሉ ፡ሆሳዕና ፡ለወልደ ፡ዳዊት ፡ዘትርጓሜሃ' ፣ አድኅነነ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዳዊት" ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ንጉሥ ፡ዘይመጽእ ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሆሳዕና ፣ በአርያም ። ተፈጸመ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘአዘዞሙ”" ፣ ከመ ፡ ይሣ የጥዎ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ተወክፍዎ”" ፡ ወአምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወተአ መነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘይጸንሕዎ ፡ እምዘርአ ፡ ዳዊት ። ወሰአልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ያድኅኖሙ፣ ወያስተፍሥሖሙ ፡በምድር ፡ወበአርያም ።
12 And on the day of Sunday which is the tenth (day) of it (following), our Lord Jesus Christ entered into the city of Jerusalem, riding in the image of a king. All the children of Israel welcomed Him and believed in Him, that He was Christ their king whom they awaited so that He might save them. All went before Him saying in their language: “Hosanna to the son of David,” the translation of which is: “Save us, O Son of David, blessed is the king who comes in the name of the Lord, hosanna in the heights” (Mt 21:1-9//). The word of God was fulfilled which ordered them to “acquire it on that day” (Ex 12:21) because they welcomed Him and believed in Him and confessed that He was the Christ from the lineage of David whom they awaited. They besought Him so that He might save them and gladden them on earth and in the heights.


[p. 142] {#po.57.p142}

### The Last Supper
ወነበረ ፡ኀቤሆሙ፤ ፡እምዕለተ ፡እሑድ ፡ዝውእቱ ፡ዐሥሩ ፡ለወርኅ ፡እስከ ፡ ፅለተ ፡ ሐሙስ ፣ ዘውእቱ ፣ ዐሥሩ ፣ ወረቡዑ ፣ እምኔሆሙ፤። በጽሐ ፡ ምስለ፣ አርዳኢሁ ፡ በምሴተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዕለት" ፡በጊዜ ፡ጥብሐተ ፡ በግዕ ፡ "ኀበ ፡ ዕርበተ" ፡ ፀሐይ ። እንዘ ፡ ይረፍቁ ፡ በድራር ፡ ሌሊት" ፡ ዘዐርብ ፣ አመ፤፲ ወ፳ ፡ ለወርኅ ፣ ጊዜ ፡ዘአዘዞሙ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡ከመ ፡ይጥብሑ ፣በግዓ ፡ቦቱ ። ወዓዲ ፡ነሥአ፣ ኅብስተ ፡ ዲበ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ሶበ''፣ ጠብሑ፤! ፡ በግዓ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ዝውእቱ፣ ሥጋየ ፡ ዘያገብእዎ ፡ በእንቲአክሙ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ለስርየተ ፣ ኃጣው ኢሆሙ፤፡ ፡ዘንተ ፣ግበሩ፤ ፡ ለተዝካርየ ። ወሶበ ፣ በልዕዎ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ጽዋዓ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ደምየ" ፡ ዘለኪዳን ፣ ሐዲስ ፡ ዘይትከዓው ፡፣ በእንተ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ለስርየተ ፡ ኃጣውኢሆምሙ ። ወዘንተ ፡ግበርዎ ፡ለተዝካርየ ።
13 He stayed with them from the day of Sunday which is the tenth of the month, until the Thursday which is the fourteenth of them (i.e., of the days of the month). He arrived together with His disciples on the evening of that day, at the moment of the slaughtering of the lamb at the setting of the sun. While they were at supper on the night of Friday, the fifteenth (day) of the month on which God had ordered them to slaughter the lamb, He took bread into His hands when they slaughtered the lamb and said to them: “This is my body which is given for you and for many for the remission of their sins: do this in my memory” (Lk 22:15). And when they had eaten it, He took the chalice and said: “This is my blood for the new covenant which is poured out for many for the remission of their sins: and do this in my memory” (1 Cor 11:25). The “old” lamb type of Jesus Christ. Meaning of the blood
አጠየቆሙ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ በግዑ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘኮነ ፡አመረ ፡ኀቤሆሙ! ፡ከመ ፣ ይጥብሕዎ ፡በይእቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ከመ ፡ይድኃኑ ፡ ቦቱ። እስመ ፡ውእቱ ፡በግዕ'' ፡ዘቀዲሙ ፡ኮነ ፡ለኪዳን ፡ቀዲሙ ፡ለአድኅኖተ ፡ ደቂቀ" ፡ እስራኤል ፡እምፈርዖን ፡ወሠራዊቱ ። ወዝንቱ” ፡በግዕ ፡ ኪዳን ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ለአድኅኖተ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ደቂቀ ፣ አዳም ፣ ምውታን” ፡ ወሕያዋን፡፤ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፣፡ እለ ፡ ይትወለዱ” ፣ ውስተ ፣ ዝዓለም ፣ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም” ፡ እምዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ። ነጽር” ፣ ኦፍቁር ፡ምንተ” ፡ ይሜኒ ፡ "ቃሉ ፡ለእግዚአብሔር” ። ሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ቅብዑ ፡ ደሞ ፡ዲበ ፡ መድረካተ” ፡ አናቅጸ ፡ አብያቲክሙ ፣ዘመልዕልት” ፡ ወዘመትሕት” ፣ከመ ፡ትድኃኑ” ፡ቦቱ ፡ እመልአከ ፡ ሙስና” ።
14 With this word He confirmed to them that He was the lamb of God that He had indicated to them, so that they might slaughter it on that day because in Him they were saved. That was in fact the lamb that in ancient times was (sacrificed) for the covenant; then to save the children of Israel from Pharaoh and his army. Instead, this is the lamb of the new covenant for the salvation of all the children of Adam, (those) dead and (those) living, and of all those who are born in this world unto eternity, from the devil and his army. Observe, O beloved, how sweet is the Word of God when He says: “Anoint (with) its blood the upper and lower doorposts of the doors of your houses so that through it you may be saved from the angel of destruction” (Ex 12:7, 13).


[p. 144] {#po.57.p144}
እስመ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ለመሃይምን” ፡፣ ውእቱ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ መንፈስ” ፣ የኀ ድር ፡ ውስቴቱ ፣ በከመ ፣ ቤት ። ወአንቀጸ” ፣ ሥጋ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አፍ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወመድረካት”” ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ከናፍር ፣ ቭዝመልዕልት ፣ ወዘመትሕት ፡ ውእቱ” ፣
15 The house of the faithful is his body because the Spirit dwells in it as in a house. The door of the body is the mouth, and the doorposts are the upper
ስነኒሁ ፣ ለሰብእ ። እስመ ፡ መሃይምን፤' ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይሰቲ ፡ ደሞ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፣ ይት ቀባእ ፡ ከናፍሪሁ ፣ ወስነኒሁ ፡ ወይከውን ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ወሶበ" ፡ ይሬእዮ ፣ መልአከ ፡ ሙስና! ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ሰይጣን ፡ ይጐይይ ፣ እምትእምርት ፡ ወኢይበ ውእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ፡ ወኢያማሰን ፣ ነፍሶ ፡ በከመ ፡ ያማስ ኖሙ ፣ለነፍሳት ፡ እለ ፡ ኢየአምኑ' ፡ ዝኢይተቀብኡ ፡ እምደሙ፡ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ወበአማን ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ እሬእዮ ፡ ለደም ፡ ዲበ፣ "አናቅጸ ፤ አብያቲክሙ ፣ ይከውነኒ ፡ ትእምርተ ። ወእሴውረክሙ ፡ ወኢየኀድጎ ፣ "ለመልአከ ፣ ሙስና"' ፣ ይቅረብ ፡ ኀቤክሙ ፡
and lower lips, the teeth of the person. When the faithful drinks the blood of Christ, his lips are anointed and his teeth will become for him a sign, and when the angel of destruction, namely, Satan, sees it, he flees from the sign and does not enter into the body of the faithful and does not destroy his soul just as he destroys the souls that do not believe, that do not anoint themselves with the blood of Christ. And God says in truth: “For I, if I see the blood on the doors of your houses, it will be for me a sign and I will hide you and will not let the angel of destruction approach you” (Ex 12:13).

### The power of the blood of Christ
እስመ ፡ደሙ ፣፡ለክርስቶስ ፡ዘይሰትዮ'! ፡መሃይምን'፤ ፡ውእቱ" ፡ትእምርተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘበመዐልት ፡ "የዐቅቦ ፡ ወይሴውሮ" ፡ በሌሊት ፡ እምሰይጣናት ፡ መላ እክተ ፡ ሙስና ። ወለእመ ፡ ይበጽሕ! ፡ መሃይምናን" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፣ ወትረ ፡ ወይቀውም ፡ በጊዜ፤' ፡ የቅዳሴ ፡ ወፉርባን” ፣ ይትቀባዕ” ፡ ደሞ ፡ ለክ ርስቶስ ፣ ለነፍሱ ፣ ወይኀትማ” ። ወሶበ ፡ ወፅአት ፡ እምሥጋ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትዕርግ፣ ኀበ ፡ ገነት ፡ ይሬእይዋ"፡! ፣ ሠራዊተ ፡ ሰይጣን= ፣ እለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አየር ፡ እንዘ፣ ኅትምት ፡ በደሙ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ኢይክሉ ፡ ይቅረቡ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ወኢይከልእዋ”፡ ፡ እምፅርገት ፡ በከመ ፣ ይከልኡ ፡ ለባዕዳነ” ፡ አላ” ፡ የኀድግዋ ፣ ትዕርግ ፡ ውስተ ፡
16 The blood of Christ that the faithful drinks is in fact the sign of Christ that by day guards him and by night hides him from the demons, angels of destruction. If the faithful comes to church always and is present at the time of the sanctification and the communion, he is anointed (with) the blood of Christ and seals his soul (Jn 6:27). And when (his soul) has gone out from the body to ascend into Paradise, the army of Satan which is in the air will see it (Eph 6:12), being sealed with the blood of Christ (Rev 7:4, 14). They will not be able to approach it and will not prevent it from ascending as they prevent others, but will let it ascend into Paradise.


[p. 146] {#po.57.p146}
ገነት ። ዝንቱ! ፡ ይከውን ፡ ለዘይቀውም ፡ ወትረ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ሞቱ።
This is what will happen to him who is present always during the sanctification until the day of his death.

### Proleptic meaning of the paschal meal and its rites

17 As God said to the children of Israel: “Do not cook the meat of the lamb with water but eat it roasted with fire” (Ex 12:9), so in truth Christ, the lamb of God, has made us eat His body, roasted with fire. In fact, this word became a type of this. He said: “Eat it with your loins girded” (Ex 12:11), so the faithful eats the body and blood of Christ with the loin of his body fastened with the belt, and with the loin of his heart fastened with faith in Him, that this is His body in which He was incarnated. He said: “Eat it and let your staffs be in your hands” (Ex 12:11), so the staff of the faithful is his cross. Because Moses with his staff sank Pharaoh and his army, and saved the children of Israel, so also Christ with the wood of His cross destroyed the devil and his army and saved the children of Adam who believed in Him. The staff of the faithful is his cross because when he feeds on the body of Christ, he signs his forehead with his finger, he strikes his enemy with (his) staff with which Christ struck him. 19 The devil and his army envy them for this reason and pursue their fall through the lust of the flesh which is a low passion. The foot is in fact under the body and so is the passion under the flesh. God said: “When you eat the body of Christ, let your sandals be on your feet” (Ex 12:11). This means: “Hide the place of the low passion from the carnal works so that the enemy does not strike us.” It is therefore necessary that the faithful abstain from this work on the day in which he eats the body and blood of Christ, from the setting of the sun until the setting of the next sun. All those married with legitimate marriage should fast from this, in the night that precedes the day in which they intend to approach the body of Christ. On the day in which they communicate, let them abstain until the setting of the sun and let them not approach their women from the night until the following evening. they sanctify the body and blood of Christ, a manifest sign of this hidden sign, just as they fasten with the belt their loin as a manifest sign of the hidden girding of the loin. (God) said: “Eat it in haste” (Ex 12:11). This means: “Do not arrive late to the Eucharistic celebration and do not be lazy but hasten (to go) to it always.” “Eat it in haste (Ex 12:11) and be worthy of it before death reaches you because it is the life of your soul from death and your salvation is from it.” In the same way, the Jews hastened to crucify Him and shouted to the governor saying: “Crucify him! Crucify him!” (Mt 27:22-23). “No bone of the lamb shall be broken.”
ይቤ ፡ወኢታትርፉ ፡እምነኔሁ ፡እስከ ፡ሳኒታ!' ፡ወኢምንተኒ፤! ፡ወኢትስብሩ ፡ ዐፅሞ ። ምንተ” ፡ይሜኒ ፡ዝንቱ ፡ቃል! ፡ዘአስተጋብአ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡በዝንቱ”" ፡ መካን ። እስመ ፡ወንጌል ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ መጠወ ፡ነፍሶ ፡ ይቤሉ ፣አይሁድ ፡እምቅድመ ፡ሞቱ፤፤ ፡እስመ ኢያእመሩ ፡ከመ ፡ውእቱ ፡ሞተ፣ ወኢይትከሀል" ፡ከመ ፡ ‹ይትረፍ ፡ስቁለ"' ፡ እስከ ፡ ሳኒታ ፡ከመ” ፡ ኢይሙት፡፡፣ በሳኒታ"፤! ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ቀዳም” ፡ ንሖር” ፣ ንስብር ፡ አቀኑ'ያጺሁ ፡ ወአቀኑ ያጺሆሙ”፡ ፣ ለእለ ፡ ተሰቅሉ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሙቱ ፡ ወናውርዶሙ ፡ እም ላዕለ ፡ዕፀው ፡እምቅድመ ፣ምሴት "። ወመጽኡ” ፡ወሰበሩ ፡ አቀ“ያጺሆሙ ፡ለእለ ፣ ተሰቅሉ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወሶበ ፣ መጽኡ ፣ ኀቤሁ” ፣ ከመ ፡ ይስብሩ ፡ አቀያጺሁ” ፡ ረከብዎ ፣ ጥቀ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ወኢሰበሩ ፡ ዓፅሞ ። ወተፈጸመ ፡ ቃለ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ዝይቤ ፣ ኢታትርፉ ፡ እምኔሁ ፤ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሳኒታ ። ወኢትስብሩ” ፣ ዓፅሞ ።
21 He said: “Do not let any of it remain until the next day (Ex 12:10) and do not break its bone (Ex 12:46)”. How sweet is this word that God has gathered in this place. Indeed, the holy Gospel says that when Christ offered his soul, the Jews before his death said: “Let us go! Let us break his legs and the legs of those who had been crucified with him so that they may die and we may take them down from the wood before evening”. (They said this) not being aware that he was dead, and it was not possible that he remain crucified until the next day, so that he would not die the next day, and that was the Sabbath day. They came and broke the bones of those who had been crucified with him. And when they came to him to break his legs, they found him already dead and did not break his bone (Jn 19:31-36). The word of God was fulfilled which says: “Do not let any of it remain until the next day (Ex 12:10) and do not break its bone”.

### On the methods of consuming the paschal lamb
ይቤ ፡ ወኢታፍልስዎ ፡ እምቤት ፡ እስከ! ፣ ቤት እስመ ፣ እሙንቱ፣ ኢያፍለስዎ፡ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ እምአዕዋድ ፡ ዘሰቀልዎ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ እስከ' ፡ መካን ፡ ካልእ ፡ ይቅብርዎ ፡ አላ ፡ ቀበርዎ ፡ ውስቴቱ፡፤ ። ይቤ ፡ ወብልዑ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወከርሦ ፣ ወእገሪሁ ፡ ወዘኢይትከሃል ፡ ለበሊዕ ፡ አውዕይዎ”' ፡፣ በእሳት ። ዝብሂል ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ ሕቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ምሥጢሩ ፡ እስከ ፡ ትጠይቅዎ' ። ወኢይትረፍ፡ ፣ እምኔሁ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ይትኀባእ ፡ እምኔክሙ ። ሕቱ ፡በእንተ'" ፡ዘይትሌዓል ፣ እምኔሁ፤! ፡በከመ ፣ይትሌዓል ፡ርእስ"''፤ ፡ወለዘይትኀባእ ፡እምኔሁ ፡በከመ ፡ከርሥ ። "ወለዘ ፡ ቀርበ" ፡ በከመ ፣ እግር ። ወኩሉ ፣ ዘይትኀባእ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወኢትክሉ ፣ ተአምርዎ”" ፣ ወኢትናፍቁ" ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ወኢትኅድግዎ ፡ ወኢትግፍፅዎ ፡ ወኢ ታኅሥርዎ ፡ አላ ፡ አውዕይዎ ፡ በእሳተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ታአምርዎ ። እስመ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እሳት ፡ እንተ ፡ ትወርድ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኅብስት ፣ ወይሬሰዮ" ፡ ሥጋሁ ፣ ለክርስቶስ ፣ ወውእቱ ፣ ዘይከሥት፤' ፣ ለክሙ ፡ ዘተኀብአ ፣ እምሥጢሩ ፣ እምኔክሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰአልክምዎ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ይክሥት ፡ ለክሙ፣
22 He said: “Do not transfer it from one house to another” (Ex 12:46) because they did not transfer our Lord from the squares in which they had crucified him to another place to bury him, but they buried him within them. He said: “Eat its head, its entrails, and its feet, and what is not possible to consume, burn it with fire” (Ex 12:9-10). This means: “Examine this word on all its mystery until you understand it”. “And let none of it remain” (means): nothing shall be hidden from you. Examine that which of it is elevated, just as the head is elevated, and that which of it is hidden, as the belly (is hidden), and that which is near, like the foot. Everything of it that is hidden and you cannot know, do not doubt it, do not abandon it, do not push it away, do not despise it, but burn it with the fire of the Holy Spirit, until you know it. For the Holy Spirit is the fire that descends upon the bread and makes it the body of Christ, and it is He who reveals to you that which of the mystery is hidden from you, if you ask Him to reveal that to you. Meaning of the liberation from Egypt
ዝክተ ። ይቤ ፡ወአንሰ ፡በይእቲ ፡ዕለት ፡እሬስየክሙ ፡አግዓዝያነ ፡እምድረ ፡ግብጽ ፡ እምግብርናተ ፡ ፈርዖን ። ወከማሁ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ዘተሰቅለ ፣ ባቲ" ፣ ክርስ ቶስ ፡ረሰየነ” ፣ አግዓዝያነ” ፣ እምግብርናተ ፣ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ኃያል ። ኩሎ ፡ዕለተ፣ ዘዘብሑ፡! ፡ መሃይምናን” ፣ ዘንተ ፡ በግዐ ፡ ይከውኑ ፡ አግዓዝያነ ፡ እምግብረ” ፡ ዝዓለም ፡ ወእምብዝኃ” ፣ ኃጣውኢሆሙ ፡ ወይድኃኑ” ፡ እምግብርናተ ፡ ዲያብ ሎስ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ተሰምየት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ፍሥሕ" ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሁ” ፡ ፍል ሰት ፡ እስመ ፣ ባቲ ፣ አፍለሶሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡፣ እስራኤል ፡ እምግ ብርናተ ፡ ፈርዖን” ፣ ወኩነኔሁ” ፡ እስከ ፣ ገዳም ፡ ወቦቱ ፡ አፍለሰነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ እምግብርናተ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወኩነኔሁ ፡፣ እስከ ፡ ገነት ። ይቤ ፡ ጠብሑ ፡ ዘንተ ፣ በግዐ ፡ በከመ ፡ በዛቲ ፣ ዕለት ፡ ለለዓመት ፡ ከመ ፡ ትዝክሩ” ፣ እፎ ፡ ረሰይኩ ክሙ ፡አግዓዝያነ ፡እምግብርናተ ፡ፈርያዖን ።
23 He said: “And I on this day will make you free from the land of Egypt, from the slavery of Pharaoh” (Ex 6:6). Likewise, on that day in which Christ was crucified, he made us free from the slavery of the powerful devil. All the days in which the faithful sacrifice this lamb, they will be free from the work of this world and from their numerous sins, and they will be saved from the slavery of the devil. For this reason, such a day was called “Pascha”, whose translation is “exit”, because in it God made the children of Israel exit from the slavery of Pharaoh and from his oppression, to the desert. In it, Christ transferred us from the slavery to the devil and from his oppression to Paradise. He said: “Slaughter this lamb every year, as on that day, so that you may remember how I made you free from the slavery of Pharaoh” (Ex 13:3-10).

### Paschal supper and Eucharist
ይቤ፤ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ኩሎ ፣ ጊዜ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትበልዑ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፣ ኅብስት ፡ ወትሰትዩ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ጽዋዕ ፡ ትዘከሩኒ፤፡ ፡ ወትዜንው ፡ ሞትየ። እስመ?" ፡ በሞቱ ፡ አግዐዘነ ፡ እምግብርናተ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ ለነ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፣ ወደሞ ፡ ባበ፣ ንሬእዮነ ፡ ንሠውያ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኃጣውኢነ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ጥብሉል ፣ በመግነዛት፤' ፣ እንተ ፣ ይእቲ ፡ አጽርቅት ፡ ስኩብ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጻሕል ፡ ሶቤሃ፣ ንዘከር" ፣ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ከመዝ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጥብሉለ፤!! ፡ በመግነዛት" ፡ ስኩበ፣ በውስተ ፡ መቃብር ። ወሶበ ፡ ንሬኢ ፡ ደሞ ፡ ዘተክዕወ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽዋዕ ፡ ንዘከር"' ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከመዝ ፣ ከዐውዎ ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ረገዝዎ ፣ ውስተ" ፣ ገቦሁ ። ወናአኩቶ”" ፡ ወንትአመን ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ምስሌነ ። ወንትጋደል ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ገቢረ ፡ ሥምረቱ ፡ወዐስዮቱ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ክሂሎትነ ፡በእንተ ፡ዘገብረ ፡ምስሌነ ፡ ከመ ፡ንረሳ'' ፣መንግሥቶ ፡ዘለዓለም ። እስመ ፡ውእቱ ፡ገብረ ፡ዘንተ ፡ምግባራተ፣
24 Christ said: “Every time you eat of this bread and drink from this chalice, remember me and announce my death” (1 Cor 11:24-26), because with his death he freed us from the slavery of the devil and instituted for us his body and his blood. When we see him to sacrifice him every day for our sins and he is wrapped with the linens, that is, the cloths laid upon the paten, we remember that he was similarly wrapped with the linens (and) laid in the tomb. When we see his blood poured into the chalice, we remember that it is thus that they poured (his blood) for us, when they pierced him in his side (Jn 19:34). We thank him and profess him for what he did for us. We struggle to fulfill his will and remunerate him with all our capacity for how he acted toward us, so that we may inherit his eternal kingdom: he has indeed performed these works to make us inherit it.
ከመ ፡ያውርሰነ ፡ኪያሃ። ይቤ ፡በሌሊት ፣እንተ"” ፡ትጠብሑ”"' ፡ባቲ ፡ዘንተ ፡በግዐ ፡አወፅአክሙ” ፡ እምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወአግፅዘክሙ፡፤ ፡ እምግብርናተ ፡ ፈርዖን ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ወኢ ትትመየጤ” ፣ትብልዑ ፡ብሑአ ፡ወኢይትረከብ ፡በኅቤክሙ ፡ብሑአ” ፡ሰብዐተ ፡ መዓልተ ። ወኩሉ ፡ ነፍስ ፣ ዘይትረከብ ፡ በኀቤሃ ፡ ብሑአ” ፡ ትትፈለጥ ፡ እምሕ ዝባ ። ፈቀደ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ ንጹሐነ ፡ እምብሑአ ፣ ግብጻዊያን ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ፈርዖን ። ወይግበሩ ፡ ብሑአ ፡ ሐዲሰ” ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተጠመቅነ ፡ ወበ ላዕነ ፣ ሥጋ ፣ በግዑ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሰተይነ ፡ ደሞ ፡ ወፃእነ ፡ እምግብረ” ፡ ዝዓለም ፡ ዘይጠፍእ ፣ እምፍትወተች ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ዘየኃልፍ ፡ ወግዕዝነ ፡ እምግብርናተ ፣ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፣ ወይደልወነ” ፣ ከመ ፡ ኢንተመየጥ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ በኩሉ ፣ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወትነ ።
25 He said: “In the night in which you slaughter this lamb, I will make you exit from the land of Egypt and I will free you from the slavery of Pharaoh and his army, and do not return to eat leaven, and let no leaven be found with you for seven days. Every person with whom leaven is found shall be separated from his people” (Ex 12:15). In this way, he wanted them to become free from the leaven of the Egyptians, the men of Pharaoh, and that they should make a new leaven. Likewise, we, if we are baptized and eat the flesh of the lamb of God and drink his blood, we will have exited from the behavior of this perishing world, from the transitory lust of the flesh, and we will be free from the slavery to the devil and his army, and we are bound never to return (to it) in all the days of our life.


[p. 156] {#po.57.p156}

### Admonition not to yield to the leaven of the demons
ወኢንትገበር ፡በብሑአ፤ ፡ሰይጣናት ፡እንተ ፡ይእቲ ፡ሀኬት ፡እምስብሐቲሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወቅዳሴሁ ። እስመ፡ ፣ ሰይጣናት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፣ተዐበዩ ፣ ወተሀከዩ ፡ እምስብሐታት ፡ወቅዳሴ ፡ወድቁ ፡እምሰማይ ።ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ምግባር ፡ ብሑእ ፣ በኀቤሆሙ ፡ ያብሕኡ ፡ ቦቱ ፣ ደቂቀ ፡ አዳም ፡ እስከ ፣ ይወድቁ ፡ ከማሆሙ ። መሀረነ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፣ከመ ፡ኢንትመየጥ ፡ወኢንግበር ፡ሀኬተ ፣እምስብሐቲሁ ፣ ወቅዳሴሁ ፣ ወኢንትዐበይ ። እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ክልኤቱ ፡ ምግባራት ፡ እሙንቱ፣ ብሑኦሙ ፣ ወዘገብረ ፣ ጥሙ ፣ ምዕረ" ፡ ካልአ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ሰብዐተ ፣ መዓልት ፣ ወሞተ" ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ውስትተ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ዘለ ዓለም ። በከመ ፣ ይቤ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ዘተረክበ ፡ ውስተ ፣፡ ቤቱ ፡ ብሑእ፤፤፣ ይትፈለጥ ፡ እምሕዝቡ ። ይደሉ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይትሃከዩ ፡ በፅ ለተ" ፤ እምነ ፡ መዓልታት ፡ እምስብሐቲሁ ፡ ወቅዳሴሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘ” ርዐ ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፣ ዝውእቱ ፣ ብሑኦሙ ፡ ሐዲስ ። ዘንተ ፡ ለእመ ፣ ገብርዎ"፣ ወትረ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ትዕቢት ፡ ወኢአድልዎ ፡ ይትባሕኡ" ፡ በመንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ ወይነግሥ!" ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ።
26 Let us not enslave ourselves to the leaven of the demons, which is sloth regarding glorifying God and his sanctification, because when the demons grew proud and failed in the glorifications and the sanctification, they fell from heaven. Such work became leaven among them with which they made the children of Adam ferment until they fell like them. God taught us not to regress, not to practice laziness regarding his glorification and his sanctification, and not to grow proud. Indeed, these two behaviors are their leaven, and whoever performs them for one or two days among the seven days, and if he dies on that day, he will fall into eternal condemnation together with them. As God said: “He in whose house leaven is found shall be separated from his people” (Ex 12:15). It is necessary that the faithful do not become lazy in any of the days that he has established regarding the glorification and the sanctification of God, that is, their new leaven. If they do this always, without pride or hypocrisy, they will be fermented with the Holy Spirit and will reign together with Him in eternity. Allegory of the parable of the leaven (Mt 13:33)
ወውእቱ ፡ ብሑእ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ፡፣ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በእንቲ አሁ ፡ በምሳሌ ። እስመ ፡ ብእሲት ፣ አብአቶ" ፡ ፣ውስተ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ መሣልስ ፡ ሐሪጽ ፣ ወኮነ ፣ ኩሎ ፡' ብሑአ” ። ወብእሲትሰ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ቤተክርስቲ ያን” ፣፡ ለእመ፣ ሰብእ፣ ወትረ ይሴብሕ” ፣፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቄድስ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ትትኅባእ ፡ ጸጋ።= ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ መሣልስ” ፣ ነፍሱ ፣ ወመንፈሱ ፡ ወሥጋሁ ፡ ወይከውን” ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መንፈሳዌ” ፡ ወኢይከውን ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ ወኢለሠራዊቱ” ፣ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሥልጣነ፡' ፡ ወዉኢይትከሀሎሙ” ፣ ያሩውድ ቅዎ ፡ውስተ ፡ኃጢአት” ፡በፈቃዱ ፡እስመ ፡መንፈስ ፡ቅዱስ ፡የዐቅቦ ፡እምኔሆሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ከመ ፡ውእቱ ፡ወትረ ፡ይሴብሖ ፡ወይቄድሶ ፡ወይሰድዶሙ ፣እምኔሁ ። ወለእመ ፡ ፣ውእቱ ፣ ስሕተ" ፡ ወአበሰ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፣ ሶቤሃ ፡ ያነስሖ"'፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ” ። ወይመይጦ ፡ ኅበ ፣ ንስሐ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይነሥእ ፡ ስርየተ ፡ ኃጢ አቱ” ፣ ወኢይወድቅ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ” ፡ ወትረ ፡ የይሴብሖ ፡ ወይቄድሶ ፡
27 This is the leaven of which our Lord Jesus Christ speaks in a parable. A woman introduced it into three measures of flour and all became leaven (Mt 13:33). That which he calls “a woman”, she is the church. If a man praises God always and sanctifies, in it (in the praise) the grace of the Holy Spirit is hidden in the three measures of his soul, and of his spirit, and of his body. Everything will become spiritual and there will be no predominance over him by Satan, nor by his army: they will not be able to make him fall into sin by his will because the Holy Spirit will protect him from them by the fact that he praises and sanctifies him always, and will drive them away from him. And if he errs and sins involuntarily, then the Holy Spirit will lead him to repent. He will make him return to repentance so that he may obtain the forgiveness of his sin and never have to fall, if he will always praise and sanctify God in the time of prayers

ለእግዚአብሔር! ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ጸሎታት ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ሱባዔ ፡ ወኢይትሀከይ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፣ በዕለት ፡ እምዕለታት፤፣ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤ፣ ዘይትረከብ ፣በኀቤሁ ፣፡ ብሑአ! ፡በሰብዐቱ ፡ መዓልት ፡ ይትፈለጥ ፡ እምሕዝቡ ።
and of the sanctification every week, and does not become lazy from this on any day, as God commanded and said: “He in whose house leaven is found in the seven days shall be separated from his people” (Ex 12:15).

### Parenesis on commitment to prayer
ወኢይደሉ ፡ ለመኑሂ ፡ እምነ ፡ መሃይምናን ፣ በዕለት ፣ እመዋዕለ ፡ ሕይ ወቱ ፡ ይትሀከይ ፣ ወይጐነዲ ፡ እምስብሐቲሁ ፡ ፣ወቅዳሴሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር" ፡ ወይጠፍእ ፡ ወይትፈለጥ ፡ እምሕዝቡ ። እስመ ፡ ፣የይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ ኢይ ትረከብ ፡ በኀቤክሙ ፡ ብሑአ" ፣ በሰብዐቱ ፡ መዓልታት ፣ ዝብሂል ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፣ ሕይወቱ ። እስመ ፡ ዐውደ ፣ ኩሉ ፡ መዓልታት ፡ ሰብዐቱ! ፡ ወይደሉ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይሴብሖ፤! ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቄድሶ" ፣ በኩሉ ፡ መዓ ልታት" ። ወበዝንቱ! ፡ ግብር ፡ ይድኅን ፣እምጠፊእ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ወይነሥእ ።፣ የሕይወተ ፡ ወመንግሥተ ፡ ዘለዓለም” ። እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ውእቱ እስመ ፣ሰብዐቱ ፡ መዓልት ፡ዘአመረ ፣እግዚአብሔር ፡በእንቲአሃ ፡ከመ ፡ይኩን፣ ውስቴታ ፡ ናእት ፡ ከመ ፣ ንትዐቀብ ፡ እምብሑኣእ ፡ ዘቀዲሙ ፡ ዘያጠፍእ ፡ ኩሎ ፣ መዋዕለ ፡ሕይወተነ ። ወናሁ ፡ ረሰዮ ፡በዝንቱ'' ፡ካዕበ ፡ትእምርተ ፣ገሃደ ፡በከመ ፣ ረሰዮ" ፣፡ ለውእቱ” ፣፡ ጣዕበ ፣ ትእምርተ ፡ ገሃደ ፡ በከመ ፡ ረሰየ ” ኩሎ"፣ ምግባራት” ፡ውስጠ ፡ወገሃደ ። ወከመ” ፣ አሣእን” ፡ ወፊቃር ፡ወፍሥሕ ፡ወበግዕ ፡
28 No faithful must on any day of his life become lazy nor delay for the glorification and for the sanctification of God to (then) perish and be separated from his people. God has indeed said: “Let no leaven be found with you for seven days” (Ex 12:15; Dt 16:3-4), that is, for all the days of his life, because the cycle of all days is seven. It is therefore necessary that (the faithful) always glorify God and sanctify him every day. Through this work, he will be saved from eternal perdition and will obtain life and the eternal kingdom because God is just. The seven days, God indicated them so that in them there may be unleavened bread and we abstain from the old leaven (1 Cor 5:6-7) which destroys all the days of our life. Behold, he has made even in this a manifest sign, just as he made all works hidden and manifest. Just as the sandals, the belt, the Pascha, and the lamb have the inner and the manifest, in the same way, the seven days also have the inner and the manifest. Old leaven and new leaven
ቦሙ ፣ "ውስጠ ፡ ወገሃደፆ ። ወከማሁ ፣ ካዕበ ፣ ሰብዐቱ"”፣ መዓልት ፣ ቦሙ፣ ውስጠ” ፡ ወገሃደ” ። ወውእቱኬ ፣ እስመ ፣ ንሕነ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ጥብሐቱ” ፡ ለበግዕ ፣ ወትንሣ ኤሁ ፣ እሙታን ፡ ንንበር ፣ ህየንተ ፣ ሰብዐቱ ፡ መዓልት ፡ ዘናእት"፤ ፡ ንግበር ፡ ሰብዐተ ፡ ሱባዔ ፡ ኢንጹም ፡ ወኢንስግድ ፣ ወኢይሰየም ፡ ካህን ፡ ወኢይቄድሱ”" ፣ ቤተክርስቲያን ፡፣ ሐዲሰ ፡ ወኢምሥዋዐ ፡ ወኢሰብሳበ ፡ ርዓዊ ፣ ወኢያጥምቁ ፣

of whom death is feared. And we do not perform anything of the leaven of the Christian faith except the glorification and the sanctification and the slaughter of the lamb. When the seven weeks end, that is, the forty-nine days, on the fiftieth day we begin all these works which are the new leaven. We will begin a new work, every year, on the day of the feast of the fiftieth day, that is, the time of the harvest of which God spoke to Moses as it was necessary that they sacrifice to him the firstfruits of their fruits (Dt 16:9-10). It is a sign for us so that we might perform this, because we (also) every year begin in it the firstfruit of the fruits of spiritual works, that is, the new leaven, which are fasting and prostration, priestly ordination, the consecration of churches. On the daily Eucharistic celebration and the merits that derive from it

every day he obtains the remission of sins and becomes free from the power of Satan. None of the Christians must move away during the time of sanctification. If it is possible for him, let him communicate, otherwise let him not communicate, because Christ wants from him that he be present and stand before him during the sanctification and glorify him and sanctify him, whether he is fasting or whether he has eaten. I manifest to you how the leavened bread becomes the body of Christ and how the wine and water mixed become his blood so that you may know his power, his majesty, and his glory.

### Bread, wine, and water, flesh and blood
ጤይቅ”"፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘህልው"' ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ኮነ ፣፡ ህልወ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ወውእቱኬ፤' ፣ ከመ” ፡ ሐዋርያት ፣ ኢኮኑ ፡ ዘይሬ እይዎ” ፣ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ። እስመ ፡፣ ውእቱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዘእምብርሃን ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ እምአብ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይት ፈጠር ፡ ዓለም ፡ አልቦቱ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወኢደመ” ፣ ዝኢያስተርኢ ፡ ወዘኢይትዐወቅ ፡ ዘኢይገሠሥ ፣ወኢይትአኀዝ! ። ወሶበ ፡ነሥአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ሥጋ ፡ወደመ ፣እማርያም ፡ ድንግል ፡ ኮነ” ፣ አሐደ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወነጸርዎ፤ ፡ በውእቱ” ፡ ሥጋ ። ወሐቀፍዎ ቦቱ ፡ወገሰስዎ ፡ ቦቱ ፣ ወለከፍዎ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በውእቱ” ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ዘነሥአ ፡ እማርያም ፣ ድንግል ፡ እምነ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ወወይን ፡ ወማይ ፣ ነሥኦ ። እስመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ በኩሉ ፡
31 Know for certain that Christ is God present with us, as he was present with his disciples, that is, that the apostles did not see that he was united to his body. He is God, Son of God, light from light, born of the Father before the creation of the world (Jn 1:1-3; Nicene Creed) without flesh or blood, invisible, unknowable, impalpable, ungraspable. But when he took for himself the flesh and the blood from the Virgin Mary, he became one with Him and they saw him in that flesh, they embraced him, they felt him, they touched him through that flesh which he took from the Virgin Mary (1 Jn 1:1-3). He took it from the bread, from the wine, and from the water.
አሚር፤ ፡ይመውእ፦ ፡ላህበ ፡ነፆራር ፡እምሥጋሁ ፡ለሰብእ ፡ ፣ወይበልዕ ፡እምኔሁ! ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ ይርኅብ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ይበልዕ ፡ኅብስተ ፡ይከውኖ ፣ሥጋ ፣ህየንተ፣ ዘሐጸጸ ፣ እምኔሁ ። ወሥጋሁ ፣ ለሰብእ ፡ እምነ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ወኩሎ፤ ፡ አሚረ ፣ ይመውኦ ፡ ላህበ ፡ ዖራር ፣ ለደሙ ፣ ለሰብእ ፡ ወየሐጽጽ ፡ ወይጸምእ ፣ ሰብእ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይሰቲ ፡ ማየ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ደመ ፣ ህየንተ ፡ ዘሐጸጸ'! ፣ እምኔሁ ። ወደሙሰ ፡ እምነ ፥ ማይ ፡ እስመ ፡ እምነ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ወእምነ ፡ ማይ ፡ ይልህቅ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ወደሙ ፣፡ለሰብእ ፡እምጊዜ ፡ ተፈጥረ ።
Indeed, man defeats daily the flame of dizziness in his human body and eats from it. Man therefore feels hunger immediately, and when he eats the bread, it will become for him flesh, in substitution of that which had decreased in him. The body of the person depends on the bread, and the flame of dizziness defeats daily the blood of man, for which man fails and becomes thirsty. When he drinks the water, (this) becomes for him the blood that had decreased in him. His blood is therefore from the water, because from the time in which man was created, it is through the bread and the water that the flesh and the blood of man grow

### Formation of the human embryo
ወውእቱኬ ፣ ከመ ፣ ብእሲት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተወክፈት ፡ ዘርአ፤! ፡ ወትበልዕ''፣ ኅብስተ ፡ ያውሕዝ" ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ እምኔሁ ፡ ክፍለ ፡ በጠባይዓት ፡ እስከ ፡ ዘርእ ፡ዘውስተ ፡ከርሥ ፡ወይከውን” ፡ ሥጋ ። ወሶበ ፡ሰትየት ፡ማየ ፡ውሕዝ ፡እም ኔሁ ፡ ክፍለ ፡ በጠባይዓት"' ፡ እስከ ፡ ዘርእ! ፣ ወይከውን ፣ ደመ ፡ ወትገብር ፡ ጠባይ ዓት፤' ፣ ከመዝ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ በኃይለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መጠነ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ፅን ሰት” ። ወሶበ ፡ወለደት ፣ ብእሲት ፡ያውሕዝ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡ለውእቱ ፣ኅብስት ፡ ወማይ ፡ዘይፈቅድ ፡ይከውና" ፡ለብእሲት ፡እምኔሁ ፡ሥጋ ፣ወደመ” ፡ያውሕዝ፡! ፡ አጥባቲሃ”። ፡ ወታጠብዎ ፡ ለሕፃን ፣ እንዘ ፡ውእቱ ፣ሐሊብ ፡እምቅድመ ፡ይኩን ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወደም” ። ወታበስሎ ፣ ጠባይዓት ፡ በውስተ ፣ ሕፃን ፡ በኀይለ ፡ ላህብ ፡ ወይ ከውኖ” ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ሥጋ ፣ወደመ ። ወይልህቅ ፡ከመዝ ፡እስከ ፡ይጸንዕ ፡ለበሊዐ” ፣፡ ኅብስት ፡ወሰትየ ፡ማይ ፡በከመ ፡ወሳዲቱ ፡ 'እስከ ፡ዕለተ ፡ሞቱ” ።
32 This is like the woman who, when she receives the sperm and eats the bread, from it God makes a part flow into the natures up to the seed that is in the womb, and it will become flesh. And when she drinks the water, (God) makes a part of it flow into the natures up to the seed (that is in the womb) and it will become blood. The natures act in this way every day by the power of God during the days of pregnancy. After the woman has given birth, God makes that bread and water flow which the woman needs (to provide) flesh and blood (to the newborn). Her breasts make (milk) flow and she nurses the child while that is still milk, before it becomes flesh and blood. Nature cooks it inside the child with the force of heat and from it will draw flesh and blood. It grows in this way until it has the vigor to eat the bread and to drink the water in the same manner as its mother, until the day of its death.

### The supernatural pregnancy and motherhood of Mary. The childhood of Jesus

33 But when the Virgin Mary conceived our Lord Jesus, she did not possess seed from which to conceive, because she was a virgin, but our Lord himself dwelt inside her by the Holy Spirit. He took for himself a part of the flesh from which it grew from the bread and took a part from the blood from which it grew from the water, and he takes (sic) a part from the wine; he made only for himself from that flesh. Because the Virgin Mary did not drink only water, nor any other among all the peoples, except the Arabs, the Westerners, and the blacks, because there is no wine in their country, but all people on any day set the table without there being on it wine mixed with water. From the bread and from the water and from the wine, our Lord, to Him be glory, took the flesh in the womb of his mother. When he gave birth to him and nursed him with milk, it was also from this. And when his body grew, just as our bodies grow, he ate bread and drank water mixed with wine. He became similar to us in everything except sin (Heb 4:15). Institution of the Eucharist
ኃጢአት። ወሶበ ፡ፈቀደ ፣ይቤዝወነ ፡በነፍሱ ፡ወይዕርግ' ፡ውስተ ፡ሰማያት ፡በሥጋሁ ፡ ሠርዐ ፡ ለነ ፡ ሥርዐተ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ወትረ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ በከመ ፣ ኮነ ፡ህልወ'' ፡ምስለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ወአዘዘነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንንሣእ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ዘእምኔሁ ፣
34 When he wanted to redeem us with his soul and ascend into the heavens with his body, he arranged for us an ordinance to remain with us always, until eternity, just as he was present with his disciples, and he ordered us that we take the bread from which (subsists) our flesh and his flesh, and the water and
ሥጋነ ፡ ወሥጋሁ ፡፣ ወማየ' ፡ ወወይነ ፣ ዘእምኔሁ ፡ ደምነ ፡ ወደሙ ፣ወናዕርጎሙ ፣ መልዕልተ ፡ ምሥዋዒሁ ፡ ወንስአሎ ፡ በስሙ፡ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘመሀረነ ፡ ኪያሁ ። ወይ ወርድ ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ በመንፈስ' ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ኀደረ ፣ ላዕለ ፡ ሥጋሃ ፡ ወደማ ፣ ለማርያም ፣ ድንግል ፡ ወይከውን ፣፡ አሐደ ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ። በከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አሐደ፣ የምስለ ፣ ሥጋ ፡ ወደም ፡ ዘነሥአ! ፣ እማርያም ። ወይሄሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ዘበአማን ፣ ንነጽሮ ፡ ወንጠይቆ ፣፡ወንገስሶ"' ፡ ወንለክፎ ፡ በከመ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፡
the wine from which (subsists) our blood and his blood, and we elevate them above his altar and implore with his holy name that he taught us so that he may descend upon them with the Holy Spirit, through which he dwelt in the flesh and in the blood of the Virgin Mary and becomes one with them, just as he became one with the flesh and the blood that he took from Mary. And he will be with us for real, we will see him, we will have certainty of it, we will feel him, we will touch him, just as he was with them.

### The Eucharistic sacrifice is worth as much as that on Golgotha
ሞተ ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ በከመ ፣ ሞተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ውእተ ፣ የመዋዕለ ፡ ጥብሉል!' ፡ በአጽርቅት ፡ስኩብ!! ፡ውስተ ፡ ጻሕል ፡በከመ ፡ጠብለልዎ ፡በመግነዝ ፡ ወወደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብር ፡ "ከዐወ ፡ ደሞ" ፣ በእንቲአነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽዋዕ ፡ በከመ ፡ተክዕወ ፡ደሙ ፡ዲበ" ፡ጎልጎታ ። ወለእመ ፡፣ርኢናሁ ፣በዝንቱ ፡አርአያ ፣ ትሑት" ፣ ወኅሱር ፡ ወነአምን" ፣ ቦቱ ፡ ወንትአመን ፡ በዕበዩ ፡ በከመ ፡ አምነ፣7 ቦቱ' ፡ፈያታይ፤' ፡ዘየማን ፡በድካሙ ፡ወተአምነ”" ፣በዕበዩ ፡ወንስአል' ፡እምኔሁ ፡ ስርየተ ፣ ኃጣውኢነ ፣ ብዙኃ” ። ወበእንተ ፣ አሚኖትነ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በድካሙ ፡ በከመ፣ ነሥአ ፣ እምኔሁ ፣ ፈያታዊ"! ፣ ዘየማን” ። ወይደልወነ” ፣ እምኔሁ ፡ ቃል" ፡ ዘደለዎ፤፤ ፡ ለፈያታዊ” ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ወንትአመን”' ፡ቦቱ ፡እንዘ ፡ንብል ፡ተዘከረነ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡አመ ፡ትመጽእ ፡በመንግሥትከ ፡ወይቤለነ ፡በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለፈያታዊ ፣ አሜን ፣ እብለክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ዮም ፡ ትሂሄልው” ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ ውስተ ፣
35 He died for us, just as he died for the people of those days, wrapped in the cloths, laid on the paten, just as they wrapped him with the funeral cloths and placed him in the tomb. He poured out his blood for us in the chalice just as his blood was poured out on Golgotha. And even if we see him in this humble and despised appearance, we believe in him and profess his greatness, just as the thief on the right believed in his weakness and professed his greatness (Lk 23:33, 40-43) and we implore from him the remission of our many sins. By reason of our faith in his weakness, just as the thief on the right obtained it from Him, we will deserve from Him the word that the thief deserved from Him, and we profess him saying: “Remember us, O Lord, when you come into your kingdom” (Lk 23:42) and he will say to us as he said to the thief: “Amen, I say to you that you today will be with us in my paradise” (Lk 23:43).

ገነትየ ። ዘንተ”፣ በአማን ፣ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፣ እለ” የአምኑ፣ ቦቱ፣ ሶበ ፡ ይኔጽርዎ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ግብር ፡ ድኩም ፣ ወንትአመን” በፅበዩ ፡ ወበክሂሎቱ ፣ በኩሉ ፣ ጊዜ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ እመሂ ፡ ቀረብነ” ፣ ወእመሂ ፣ ኢቀረብነ” ። እስመ ፡ ፈያ ታዊ” ፣ ሶበ ፣ አምነ ፤ ቦቱ ፡ ወተአመነ ፡ በመለኮቱ ፡ ወበመንግሥቱ ፡ በጊዜ ፣

Indeed, when the thief believed in him and professed his divinity and his kingdom, in the moment of his weakness, it was not

ድካሙ ፣ ኢያጽህቆ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይብላዕ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወይስተይ ፡ ደሞ ፡ አላ ፡ ሶቤሃ፣ ይቤሎ ፡ አንተ ፡ ትሂሉ ፡ ዮም ፡ ምስሌየ' ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነትየ፡ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ይደ ልዎሙ ፡ለኩሎሙ ፥እለ ፡፣ይትጋደሉ ፡ወበጽሑ" ፡ወይቁሙ ፡በጊዜ ፣ቅዳሴ ። ዝሎ ፡ አሚረ ዖ ወዘይገብር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፣ ለእመ ፡ በጽሖ"'፣ ሞት ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነቱ" ፡ እስመ ”ሥ እግዚእነ'! ፣ በኪዳኑ ፡ ጽድቅ ፣ ውእቱ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ፤!፣ መሃይምናን" ፣ ዘይቀውም ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ይቁም ፡ በፍርሃት ፡ ወበረዓድ ። ወይ ስአል፤፤ ፣ ወያስተበቀኑፅ ፣ ኀበ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ በቅድሜሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይረስዮ ፡ ድልወ ፡ ለገነቱ ፡ ወይስረይ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሰረየ" ፡ ለፈያታዊ ፡ ወያብዝኅ" ፡ ኃ ሠ ፡ በአሚኖቱ" ፡ ወተጋድሎቱ"' ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ተዘከረኒ" ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ አመ ፡ትመጽእ ፡በመንግሥትከ ።
necessary for him to eat his flesh, nor to drink his blood, but (Jesus) said to him immediately: “You today will be with me in my paradise!” (Lk 23:43). The same will be deserved by all those who strive and arrive and are present at the time of the sanctification every day. Whoever will do this with this faith, if death reaches him, on that day he will be together with Christ in his Paradise, because our Lord is justice in his covenant. Therefore, it is necessary that all the faithful who are present at the time of the sanctification stand with fear and trembling. Let him implore and entreat from him before whom he stands so that he may make him worthy for his Paradise and forgive him as he forgave the thief; let him increase the pursuing of his faith and his (spiritual) exercise, saying: “Remember me, Lord, when you come into your kingdom” (Lk 23:42). Disposition of the faithful after the elevation during the Mass

37 He shall not mock and shall not think of things of this transitory world and shall not dare to sit, nor pronounce any word except the response to the celebrating priest or to the deacon who serves together with him. He shall not exit from the church until all his body is elevated above the altar. After his elevation, let all raise their eyes to heaven to implore him with effort so that he may renew inside them the grace of the Holy Spirit, just as he renewed (it) to the apostles after his ascension (Acts 2:1-4). They are bound to read two Psalms, the 46th and the 50th, because through them he announces his ascension. Let them beseech him so that he may not remove from them his Holy Spirit (Ps 50:13). All the faithful

must fulfill this after the elevation of the Body from the altar so that the grace of the Holy Spirit may be renewed inside them always. If they have not memorized these two Psalms, let them say this: “O my Lord Jesus Christ who ascended to the heavens, give me the grace of the Holy Spirit that you gave to your apostles after your ascension (Acts 2:1-3), do not remove (it) from me (Ps 50:13), neither in this world, nor in the one to come. For to you (is) the glory, the honor, the thanksgiving together with your merciful Father and with your Holy Spirit who is coequal with you in divinity, now and always and unto the ages of ages, amen”.

1 Fifth homily: declaration on the combat with which the demons fight against the faithful and how they defeat them. O beloved brother! May God illuminate the eyes of your mind with the light of the Holy Spirit the consoler so that you may understand the mysteries of his divinity. In the book on the incarnation of the Son of God and on his crucifixion, I have already explained to you that God created Adam and his progeny to make them ascend to the exalted order from which the devil and his army fell. When they sinned and the devil reigned over them and killed them and made them descend to Sheol because they had obeyed him, the Son of God redeemed them with his soul, died for them, and made their soul ascend from Sheol, up to the Paradise inside which they lived at the beginning (Gn 1-3). It was necessary to reach, on the part of the living children of Adam, the number of the army that fell together with the devil.

2 He had indeed found them lacking in their number and made it so that baptism would free the children of Adam from the slavery of the devil so that they would die pure and ascend to Paradise. And they will bring them together with those who ascended from Sheol, until they are according to the number of the army of the devil; they will ascend and inherit the exalted heavenly order from which the devil fell. The army of the devil nourishes envy toward the children of baptism by reason of their order and fights them with sinful actions so much that they die sinners, so as to inherit together with them eternal damnation and they move away from the eternal kingdom. All

this I reminded you in this book but I did not explain to you what the faithful perform to defeat the demons with it. Pay attention to what I mention to you in this book so that you may ascertain this. May God grant you the understanding of everything! Condition of humans at the moment of their creation

3 You, O beloved, know that the angels are pure spirits, without bodies, while the beasts are animal bodies, without a rational soul, and their life is their blood because they do not have a rational soul. When God created Adam to give him the investiture over the order from which the archangel devil fell, God created Adam, a pure, rational spirit like the angels and made him dwell in a living flesh. As for the cadavers of the animals, his life is his blood. When (God) knew, to Him the glory, that the rational soul was chained in the animal flesh, and was one thing together with it, and that it did not have the power to perform its will like the angels, on the instant he breathed upon the face of Adam his holy spirit (Gn 2:7), and the grace of the Holy Spirit dwelt inside the rational soul of Adam, and it became one thing (with the Holy Spirit). His soul prevailed over his animal body with the force of his Holy Spirit, thus his body obeyed his soul and they agreed to perform the will of their Creator.

Obedience of Adam to the devil and its consequences. Jesus Christ redeems man from the slavery of the demon 4 But when Adam obeyed the devil and believed in that which he brought to him as a pretext, making his Creator a liar, the grace of the Holy Spirit separated from him and the devil reigned over him. An impure spirit (emitted) from the demons of the devil settled in his soul instead of the Holy Spirit, and forced him to commit sin without his will (Rom 7:21-23). Thus he did toward all those who were born from his seed, generation after generation. But when our Lord Jesus Christ came and offered himself for Adam and for his descendants who died with the oppression in sin, our Lord descended to Sheol and made the souls of all ascend from it and introduced them into Paradise (1 Pt 3:19, 22; Eph 4:8-9) because they sinned involuntarily, by constraint. As for Christian baptism, it frees the living among the children of Adam from the impure spirit that the devil places upon them to induce them to sin. But with baptism, the Holy Spirit returns to them that God breathed upon the face of Adam (Gn 2:7) and defeats the army of the devil and gives to them the strength to perform the will of God when he dwells inside them, if they glorify him and sanctify him in the times that he has established for them. Conditions for remaining in the state of grace

ቅዱስ ። እስመ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ይቤ ፡ በወንጌል፤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ እስመ ፣ ሰብእ ፡በከመ ፡ንጉሥ ፡ዘምስሌሁ ፡፲፻፻ ፡ወሰይጣንሰ ፡ጸላኢሁ ፡ይመስል ፡ንጉሠ፣ ዘምስሌሁ፥ ፡ ፳፻፻ ፡ ወኢይክል ፡ዘምስሌሁ ፡፲፻፻ ፡ ይመውኦ" ፡ለዘምስሌሁ ፡በ፳፻፻ ። ኢይመውኦ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በኀይለ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ናዛዚ ፡ ኅያል ፣ ረዓይት ። ወጳውሎስ ፣ ሐዋርያ ፡ ይቤ ፣ በመልእክቱ ፡ ለብሔረ ፣ ሮሜ ፣ እስመ ፣ ብእሲ ፣ ዘአልቦቱ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ይትገበር ፡ ለገቢረ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፣ ወኢይክል ፡ይግበር ፡ሕገ ፡እግ ዚአብሔር ።
as they were before Christian baptism. No one can defeat them without the power of the Holy Spirit because our Lord Jesus Christ in the holy Gospel said: “Man is indeed like a king who has with him ten thousand (people), while his enemy Satan resembles the king who has with him twenty thousand, and that, he who has with him ten thousand cannot defeat him who has with him twenty thousand” (Lk 14:31). He does not defeat him without the power of the Holy Spirit, the consoler (Jn 14:26), the powerful, the giant. The apostle Paul in his letter to the Romans said: “The man who does not have the Holy Spirit submits himself to commit sin without his will and cannot execute the law of God” (Rom 7:14-23; 8:9).

### The Holy Spirit within the believer
ወዘይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታእምር" ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እፎ ፡ ትሄሉ ፡ ውስቴት"' ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወንትጋደል ፡ በዝንቱ ፣ ሶበ ፡ ይሄሉ ፡ ውስቴትነ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ንገብር፣0፣ ሥምረቶ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ፈቃድነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ተፈ ልጠ ፡ እምኔነ ፡ ንገብር! ፣ ኃጢአተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃድነ ። ወአንሰ ፡ እከሥት ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦፍቁር ፡ እፎ ፡ ትሄሉ ፡ ውስቴትነ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወእስሰእለከ ፡ በእግዚ እነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትዕቀቦ ፡ ወትግበሮ ፡ ወትምህሮሙ ፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፣ ይክሉ፤፤ ፣ እምደቂቀ ፣ ጥምቀት ፣ ወአንተ ፡ ትነሥኦ!፤ ፡ ዕሜተ ፡ ዘአልቦቱ" ፣ ምሳሌ ። ወትትጐለቀ፦ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ወጳውሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያቲሁ" ፣ ቅዱሳን ። ወለእመ ፣ አንተ ፣ ጠየቅከ" ፣ ወዐቀብከ ፡ ወገበርከ'' ፣ ቦቱ ፡ ወለእመ ፡፣ ኢትሜ ህሮ' ፡ ለዘይክል ፡ እምደቂቀ ፡ ጥምቀት ፡ መንፈስ” ፡ ቅዱስኒ ፣ ይትፈለጥ” ፣
6 But what you must now know is in what way the Holy Spirit is present within us. If He is within us always, we strive to fulfill the will of God and not our own will, just as we commit sin without our will if (the Holy Spirit) separates from us. But I reveal to you, O beloved, in what way the Holy Spirit is present within us, and I ask you for the sake of our Lord Jesus Christ that you preserve it, practice it, and teach it to all the children of baptism who are capable of it, and you will have a reward that has no comparison. You will be numbered with Peter and Paul, His holy apostles, if you are convinced of it, (if) you preserve it and put it into practice; but if you do not teach it to those among the children of baptism who are capable, the Holy Spirit will separate

እምኔከ ። ወይሜጥወከ ፡ ለሠራዊተ ፣ ዲያብሎስ ፣ ያጠፍኡከ ። እስመ ፡ አንተ፣ አእመርከ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወኢመሀርከ ፣ ለአኃዊከ ፣ መሃይምናን ፡ ወለቡ ፡ ለዘአጤይ ቀከ' ፡እምዝንቱ ።
from you. He will deliver you to the army of the devil so that it may destroy you, because you have come to know the good and have not instructed the faithful, your brothers. Pay attention to what I explain to you about this!

### Characteristics of the human spirit and its body
አንተ ፡ ኦፍቁርየ ፡ ተአምር ፣ ከመ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ኢይበልዕ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ወኢይ ሰቲ ፡ ማየ ፡ ወኢይጥዕም ፡ ጥዕመተ ፡ ዝዓለም ፡ ወኢይትፌሣሕ ፡ በፍሥሐ ። አላ፣ መብልዐ ፡ ወመስቴሃ ፡ ወጥዕመታ ፡ ወፍሥሐሃ ፡፣ ስብሐቲሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፣ ወቅዳሴሁ ፡ ወሰሚዐ ፡ ቃሉ ፡፣ መለኮታዊ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር፡ ፡ በኦ ሪት ፡ ወበወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አኮ ፣ በኅብስት ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ዘየ ሐዩ ፡ አላ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዘይወፅእ ፡ እምአፉሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወሥጋሰ ፡ በኅብስት ፡ "የሐዩ፡ በከመ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ በቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ የሐዩ ፣ በከመ ፡ መላእክት ። ወለእመ ፣፡ "ቦበ ፡ ኃጥአ ፣ ብእሲ ፡ ዘይበልዕ ፡ ወዘይሰቲ"፣ ይመውት ፡ ሥጋሁ ፣ እስመ ፣ ላህብ ፣ ዘተፈጥረ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ይበልዕ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፣ ወ'ደሞ ፡ ወይመውት ፣ ሥጋሁ። ወከማሁ ፣ ሶበ ፡ ኃጥአ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ስብሐ ቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወቅዳሴሁ ፡ ወሰሚዐ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ትመውት ፡ መንፈሱ፣ እምኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። እስመ ፡ ሰይጣናት ፡ ይመውእዋ ፡ ወተአብስ ፡ በግ ብር ፡ወትትፈለጥ" ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ።
7 You know, O my beloved, that the spirit does not eat bread, nor drink water, and does not taste anything of this world, and does not enjoy pleasure. Its food, its drink, and its joy are the glorification of God and His sanctification, and the listening to His divine word. As God said in the Torah and in the holy Gospel: “Man does not live by bread alone, but by every word that comes from the mouth of God” (Dt 8:3; Mt 4:4). The flesh, however, lives on bread, in the same way as animals, while the spirit lives on the Word of God, like the angels. If a man lacks food and drink, his body dies because the heat that is created in it devours his flesh and his blood, and his body dies. In the same way, if a man fails in the glorification of God, and in His sanctification, and in the listening to His word, his spirit will die before God. Indeed, the demons will defeat him and he will necessarily sin, and the Holy Spirit will separate from him. | Reasons for the fall of the devil, a deterrent lesson for humans. The effect of grace

ወለእመ ፡ ፈቀድከ ፡ ታእምር'፤ ፡ ጥያቄ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ተዘከር ፣ ግብረ ፡ ዲያብ ሎስ ፣ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ እለ ፣ ኮኑ ፡ መኖፍስተ ፡ ንጹሐን ፡ እምሊቃነ ፡ መላእክት ። ወሶበ ፡ ተከልኡ ፣ እምስብሐቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወቅዳሴሁ ፡ ተኀየለት ፡፣ ላዕ ሌሆሙ ፡ኃጢአት ፡ወተፈልጠ ፡እምኔሆሙ ፡ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወወድቁ፣
8 If you want to know the certainty of this, remember the actions of the devil and his army who were pure spirits among the archangels. When they refused to glorify God and to sanctify Him, sin prevailed over them and the grace of the Holy Spirit separated from them, and they fell into eternal darkness.
እስከ ፡ ጽልመት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ከማሁ ፣ ፍቁርየ'"' ፣ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይትከላእ ፡ ወየኀድግ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ወቅዳሴሁ ፡ወሰሚዐ ፡ ቃሉ ፡በበጊዜሁ ፡ትትፈለጥ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተፈልጠት ፡ እምነ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ወተኃድጎ"” ፣ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፣ ያገብርዎ ፣ በዘያሠምሮሙ ። ወ”ዘሰ ፡ ወትረ ፣ ይሴብሖ ፡ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ወይቄድሶ ፡ወይሰምዖ"” ፡ቃሎ ፡በበጊዜሁ ፡ትሄሉ፤' ፣ ወትረ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወትሰድድ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ መናፍስተ ፣ የርኩሳን ፡ ሠራዊተ'!! ፣ ዲያብሎስ ፣ ወኢይትከሀሎሙ''፡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ ወኢይከውን ፣ሎሙ ፡ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሥልጣን ፡ ወታጌብሮሙ ፡ጸጋ ፡መንፈስ ፡ቅዱስ፣ ለገቢረ ፡ሥምረተ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡ዘእንበለ ፡ፈቃዱ ። ኦፍቁር ፡ አልቦ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ይግበር ፡ ሥምረቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በሀልዎተ!" ፣ ጸጋ፤፤፤ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ ትሄሉ፣0፣ ጸጋ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ውስቴቱ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በስባሔ፤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣፡ ወቅዳ ሴሁ ፣ ወትረ ፡ ወሰሚዐ ፡ ቃሉ ፡፣ በበጊዜሁ ። እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ መስተሣ ሀል ፡ አእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ያጽህቆ ፡ ለሕይወተ''' ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ" ፡ ወለ ሕይወተ ፡ ሥጋ። ወኢይትከሃሎ!፤” ፡ ዘእንበሌሆሙ!” ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ያጽ ህቆ ፣ ፣ለስብሐታት ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፡ ወሰሚዐ ፡ ቃለ ፡እግዚአብሔር"" ፡ እስመ ፡ በው እቱ ፡ ተሐዩ ፣፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ በከመ!” ፡ አጠ የቁከ፤” ወውእቱ ፡ ያጽህቆ ፡ ለዘይበልዕ ፡ ወለዘይሰቲ"”"፡ እስመ ፡ በውእቱ ፣ የሐዩ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ውስተ ፡ ዝዓለም ። ወሶበ ፣ አእመረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰብእ ፣ ያጽህቆ ፡ ለ፪ ሕይወት ፡ ወያፈቅር ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምሕይወተ ፣ መንፈሱ"” ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ሠርዐ ፡ ለሕይወተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ጊዜያተ ፡ ፣ወለሕይወተ ፣
Similarly, my beloved, all those who refuse and neglect the glorification of God and His sanctification, and the listening to His word regularly, the grace of the Spirit separates from them, just as it separated from the devil and his army, and abandons them to them so that they may subjugate them as they please. As for him who always glorifies God and sanctifies Him and listens to His Word regularly, the grace of the Holy Spirit will always be within him and will drive away from him the unclean spirits, the army of the devil; they will never prevail over him and will have no power over him. The grace of the Holy Spirit will compel him to fulfill the desire of God, without his own will. Structure of humans willed by God: spirit and body 9 O beloved, there is no one who can fulfill the desire of God without the grace of the Holy Spirit being within him, and there cannot be within him the grace of the Holy Spirit without the glorification of God and His sanctification always, together with the regular listening to His Word. God, the merciful, knew that man needs the life of the Holy Spirit and the bodily life and that he cannot do without them. He indeed needs the glorifications and the sanctification and the listening to the word of God, because, as I have already assured you, through this his soul lives with God until eternity. And this is necessary for him who eats and for him who drinks, because it is through it that his body lives in this world. God knew that man needs two lives and that (man) loves the life of his body more than the life of his spirit. For this reason, He established times for the life of the spirit, but


[p. 186] {#po.57.p186}
ሥጋ ፡ ጊዜያተ፤ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምጊዜያተ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅልል ፣ ዝንቱ፣ ላዕለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ወይግርር፡ ፡ወትረ ።
for the body He established times that are broader than the times of the spirit, so that this may be (more) light for man and he may always comply.

### Parenesis on the value and rhythms of prayer times
ለቡ ፡ ኦፍቁርየ ፡ ጊዜያተ ፡-እለ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሥርወ ፡ መድኃኒት ፡ ወቦሙ ፣ ትሄሉ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣፡ ውስተ ፣፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ከመ ፣ አጠየቁከ ። ሠርዐ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወበኩሉ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር" ፣ ኀበ ፣ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን" ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሀኬት ፡ ወይሰብሖ ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ በጽባሕ ፣ መዓልት ፡ ሎ ፡ አሚረ ። ይጴጹሊ ፣ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወይሰብሖ ፡ ወይስአል ፡ ስሬተ'፣ ኃጣውኢሁ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ እንተ ፡ ኀለፈት ። ወይስአል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይረሲ ፣ ኀቤሁ ፣ መልአኮ ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕቀቦ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እምሠራዊተ ፡ ሰይጣን" ፡ ወምክንያቶሙ ። ወበይእቲ'' ፡ ጸሎት" ፡ የዐቅቦ፤፤ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ በይእቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ኩለንታሃ ። ወከማሁ" ፡ ይሖር"' ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቤተክርስቲያን ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ፅርበተ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ርእሰ፣ ጥንተ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ይሰብሖ ፡ ወይስአል!፣ ስሬተ፡ ኃጣውኢሁ'' ፡በመዓልት ፡ዘኀለፈ ። ወይኅሥሥ ፡ከመ ፡ይፈኑ ፣ሎቱ ፡መልአኮ" ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ ዘየዐቅቦ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ እምክንያተ ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ ሰይጣን ። ወበይ እቲ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ የዐቅቦ ፣ እምኔሆሙ” ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት 7፣
10 Pay attention, O my beloved, to the times that are the root of salvation, and through them the Holy Spirit will be within man always, as I (already) assured you. God has ordained that (every) person go to church without laziness, every day and every night, and that he praise Him inside it at the dawn of every day, always. He should pray to Him and praise Him, and ask for the forgiveness of his sins of the night that has passed. And he should implore that He place His pure angel beside him so that he may protect him (Ex 14:19; Lk 22:43) on that day from the army of Satan and from their pretext. With this prayer, the Holy Spirit will protect him from them throughout that entire day. Similarly, he should go to church at the sunset of the sun, the beginning of the night, glorify Him and implore the forgiveness of his sins of the past day, and seek that He send him His pure angel who protects him (Ps 91:11) in that night from the pretext of the army of Satan. With this prayer, the grace of the Holy Spirit protects him from them, throughout that entire night.
ኩለንታሃ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ክልኤቱ ፡ ጸሎት ፣ ይደልወሙ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ። በጽባሕ ፡ ወበምሴት ፡ ይጸልይዎሙ” ፣ ውስተ ፣ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን፤! ፡ በሰጊድ ፡ ወገዓር” ። ወለእመ ፡ኢሀሎ ፡ ‹ቤተክርስቲያን ፡ውስተ ፡ሀገር” ፡ ይደልዎ” ፡ከመ፣ ይጹሊ” ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቴክዝ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወየሐዝን ፡ እስመ” ፡ ኢረከበ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያነ” ። እስመ ፡ ቤተክርስቲያን ፡ ይእቲ ፡ገነቱ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር” ፡
11 These two prayers are mandatory for the faithful every day, morning and evening, with prostration and with groaning inside the church. If there were no church in the town, he must pray in his house with the sadness of his heart, and grieving because he did not find a church. The church is indeed the Paradise of God as I (already) revealed to you in the book of the hermeneutics of the


[p. 188] {#po.57.p188}
በከመ ፡ከሠትኩ ፡ለከ' ፡ በመጽሐፈ ፡ትርጓሜ ፡ ቃላተ ፡ኦሪት ፡ወኢያሱ ፡ወልደ፣ ነዌ ። ወዘተሀከየ ፡ ወኅኀደገ ፡ አሐደ ፡ እምእሉ ፡ ክልኤቱ ፡ ጸሎት ፣ ወኢሰብሖ፡፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ይነስሕ ፡ ወይትነሳሕ፣ እምዝንቱ ፡ ወኢይትመየጥ"' ፡፣ ይግ በሮ ፣ ከመ ፡ ኢይትሀከይ ፡ ወኢየኅድግ፡ ፣ የአሐደ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ እምእሎንቱ ፡ ክል ኤቱ' ፡ጸሎታት” ፡ አው ፡ይገብር ፡ግብረ ፡ሥጋ ፡ ኀላፊ ፡በጊዜ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ጸሎት ወበጽሖ ፣ ሞት ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትነሳሕ ፡ ወይወድቅ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ጽልመት ፡ ዘለዓለም ፣ ዘወድቀ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ እስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ተከልአ ፡ እምስብሐቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፣ ከማሆሙ ። ዘንተ ፡ ለእመ ፣ ኮነ ፡ተከልአ"› ፣እምስብሐቲሁ ፣ለእግዚአብሔር ፡በሀኬት ፡ ውእቱ ፣ይከውን፤'!፣ በአምሳለ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ"፡ ።
words of the Torah and of Joshua, son of Nun. He who becomes lazy and neglects one of these two prayers and does not glorify God, let him make contrition and repentance for this and not return to doing it so that he does not become lazy and does not neglect any time of these two prayers. If (someone) performs transient carnal actions during that prayer and death overtakes him on that day, before he has done penance, he will fall into the eternal darkness into which the devil and his army fell because he refused the glorification of God like them. This (happens) if he has refused the glorification of God out of laziness: he will be according to the likeness of the devil and his army. Observing the times of prayer
ወለእመ"' ፣ ኮነ” ፣ ተከልአ ፣ እምዝንቱ ፡ በምግባረ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ኀላፊት""፣ አው፤' ፣ የኃሥሥ ፣ ግብረ ፡ ኃላፌ፤' ፡ እምንዋየ ፡ ዝ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወረሰዮ ፡ ይቴይስ የእምስብሐተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ እስመ”! ፡ ይከውን ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠ ራዊቱ ። ወለእመ ፡ ተከልአ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ እንዘ ፡ የኃሥሥ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ውእቱ፣ ይከውን ፡በአምሳለ” ፡ይሁዳ ፣አስቆሮታዊ ፡ዘረሰየ”! ፡ለብሩር ፡ኃላፊ ፡ ይቴይስ። ፡ እምክርስቶስ ፡ ዘህልው” ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወይከውን ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ እለ ፡ ያመልኩ፣ ጣዖታተ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኀደገ” ፡ አምልኮ” ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወስብሐቲሁ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ይኅሥሥ”። ፣ ወርቀ ፡ አው ፡ ብሩረ ። ወወርቅሰ ፡ ወብሩር ፡ ጣዖታት ። ወበእንተዝ ፣ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፣ አንትሙ ፡ ኢትክሉ ፡ ፤ለክ ልኤ ፡አጋእዝት”' ፡ የተቀንዮ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር” ፡ወለንዋይ ። ወኢይደሉ ፡ለመኑሂ ፡ እምነ ፡ መሃይምናን” ፣ ይትሀከይ ፣ እምሐዊረ ፡ ቤተክርስቲያን ፡ በጊዜ ፣ እሉ ንቱ ፡ ክልኤ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ ወኢያመክኒ ፡ በግብር! ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ምግባራት ፡ ወይከ ውን” ፣ዝንቱ ፡ምክንያተ ፡ለጠፊኦቱ።
12 If he refuses this for a work of transient life, or to pursue a transient work among the wealth of this world and makes it more important than the glorification of God, he will be according to the likeness of the devil and his army. If, while pursuing wealth, he refuses the time of prayer, he will be according to the likeness of Judas Iscariot who made passing silver more important than Christ (Mt 26:14-16) who exists in eternity. He will be according to the likeness of those who worship idols because he has abandoned the worship of God and His glorification and has set out to pursue gold or silver: but gold and silver are idols (Ex 32:1-6; Ps 115:4; 135:15). Therefore God, to Him be glory, said: “You cannot serve two masters: serve God and wealth” (Mt 6:24ff). No faithful should become lazy about going to church at the time of those two prayers and should not make any kind of pretext because this pretext will be for his perdition.


[p. 190] {#po.57.p190}

13 And so he must reach (the church) and be present during the entire sanctification and never move away during the entire sanctification, whether he can communicate or not communicate. He must indeed glorify God and sanctify Him, and His glorification is the prayers that we have remembered, and His sanctification is reaching (the church) and being present at the sanctification and (receiving) communion. Because if he is present during the sanctification, he will listen to the reading of the books which is the Word of God that comes out of His mouth, of which our Lord Jesus Christ said that the spirit lives by it and not by bread (Mt 4:4). Bread is indeed the life of the body while the Word of God is the life of the soul, and his soul lives by the listening to the holy books and by the listening to the Gospel. And he will sanctify God together with the angels, because the priest says: “You are God before whom the angels and archangels stand. The many-eyed cherubim and the six-winged seraphim glorify always, without silence”. The sanctification together with the angels
በለ ፡አርምሞ ።። ወሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ካህን ፡ ይሰጠውዎ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በአሐዱ ፡ቃል ፡ቅዱስ ፡ቅዱስ ፡ቅዱስ ፡-እግዚአ ፡ኃያላን ፡ሰማያት፤'! ፡ ወምድር" ፡ ምሉእ ፡ እምስብሐቲከ ፡ ቅዱሳት ። ዘንተ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ 'ይቤልዎ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ፣ ይቀውሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ይቄድስዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ሥሉስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ሠለስቱ ፡ ቅዳሴያት ። ወውእቱ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ይቄድሶሙ ፡ እምኃ ጣውኢሆሙ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዘይሴብሐኒ ፡ አነ ፡ እሴ ብሖ ፡ወዘይቄቂድሰኒ ፡አነ ፡እቄድሶ ። ወበዝንቱ ፡ የቅዳሴ ፡ ይትቄደስ ፡ መሃይምን ፣ እምኃጣውኢሁ ፣ እስመ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ በልሳን" ፡ ሶርያዊ ፡ ወበዕብራዊ ፣ ትርጓሜሁ ፣
14 When the priest declaims, all those who are in church answer him with one voice: “Holy! Holy! Holy! Lord of hosts, the heavens and the earth are full of your holy glory” (Is 6:3). All those who are present in church pronounce this sanctification: they sanctify the triune holy God with these three sanctifications and He then sanctifies them from their sins. As the prophet Isaiah said, “for him who glorifies me, I will glorify, and him who sanctifies me I will sanctify” (1 Sam 2:30; Jn 17:19). With this sanctification, the faithful is sanctified from his sins because the meaning of “sanctification” in the Syriac and Hebrew language is “purification” and the sanctification


[p. 192] {#po.57.p192}
ንጽሕ ፡ወቅዳሴ፤ ፡ ንጽሐ" ፡ውእቱ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ዝብሂል ፡ንጹሕ ፡ውእቱ ። ለቡ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ትርጓሜ ፡ካዕፅበ ፡ከመ ፡ትጠይቅ ፡እስመ ፡በቅዳሴ ፡ይትቄደስ ፡መሃይምን ፣ እምኃጣውኢሁ ።
is purification. “Holy” indeed means “pure”. Pay attention, therefore, to this meaning so that you may be certain that the faithful is sanctified from his sins by means of the Eucharistic celebration.

### Epiclesis: first part
ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፣ ያድንን፥! ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወይበል፣ ከመዝ ። ንስእለከ ፡ ኦእግዚኦ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ አግብርቲከ ፡ኃጥአን ፡እለ ፡እንበለ ፡ድልወት ፡ከመ ፣ ትፈኑ ፡ላዕሌነ ፡መንፈስከ ፡ቅዱስ ፡ወላዕለ ፡ዝንቱ ፡ቀሩርባን ፡ከመ ፡ ትቀድሶሙ ፣ ወትረስዮሙ፤ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፣ ወደሞ ፣ ለኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ወልድከ ። ኦፍቁር ፡ለቡ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ክብረ ፣ ዘይከውኖሙ፥ ፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ለእለ! ፡ ይቀውሙ ፡ ውስተ፣ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፣ እስመ ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ትወርድ ፡ ላዕ ሌሆሙ ፡እምቅድመ ፡ትረድ'! ፡ላዕለ ፡ ርባን ፡ እስመ ፡ካህን ፡ይብል ፡ፈንዎ፣ ላዕሌነ ፡ ወላዕለ ፣ ዝንቱ ፣ ቀሩርባን ። ውእቱሰ! ፡ ይወርድ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እም ቅድመ ፡ሞኑርባን ፡ወበከመ ፣ይትቄደስ" ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ምድራዊ ፡ ኀላፊ ፡ወይሬሰዮ ሰማያዊ” ፣ ዘኢየኃልፍ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ይቄድሶሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፡ ይቀ ውሙ ፣፡ ቅድማሜሁ ፡ በርደቱ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፣ ወያነጽሖሙ" ፡ እምኃጣውኢሆሙ ፣ እንተ ፡ ታጠፍእ ። ወይሬስዮሙ- ፡ ድልዋነ ፡ ለሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ እስመ ፡ ፍካ ሬሁ!" ፣ ለመንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ መንፈሰ ፡ ንጽሕ ፡ ውእቱ ፣በከመ ፡ ተርጐምኩ ፡ለከ ። የወውእቱ ፡ ያነጽሕ'”፤ ፡ ለዘይወርድ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እምኃጣውኢሁ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወለእመ ፣
15 After this sanctification, let him bow his head and say this: “We implore you, O Lord, we your sinful servants, without worthiness, that you send your Holy Spirit upon us and upon this offering so that you may sanctify them and make them the body and blood of Jesus Christ, your Son”. Pay attention, O beloved, to this great honor that all those present in church will obtain, because the Holy Spirit of God descends upon them before He descends upon the offering. The priest says indeed: “Send Him upon us and upon this offering”, and He descends upon the people before the offering. And as He sanctifies the transient earthly bread and makes it heavenly and intransient, in the same way, He sanctifies all the people, present before Him, with His descent upon them and purifies them from their sins that destroy. And He makes them worthy of eternal life (Jn 6:53-58) because, as I have already explained to you, by Holy Spirit is meant Spirit of purity. He purifies from his many sins, him upon whom He descends; even if they were like the number (of the grains) of the sand of the sea, it is not

ኮነት፤ ፡ ከመ፡ ፡ ጭልቁ?! ፡ ጥጻ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ኢይሰአኖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያነጽሖ ፡ እምኔሁ ፣ እመሂ ፡ ቁርበ ፡ ወእመሂ ፡ ኢቄርበ" ፡ በከመ ፡ ያነጽሕ ፡ ለኩሉ፡ ፡ ዘይወርድ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ በጥምቀት ፡ ቅድስት ፡እምኩሉ ፡ኃጢአት ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ዝንቱ ፡ሶበ ፡ ይወርድ ፡መንፈስ ፡ቅዱስ ፡ላዕለ ፡ ቀሩርባናት"' ፣ ቅድሳት! ፡ ይብል ፡ ካህን ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ አንተ ፡ ጸድቅ ፣ በኪዳንከ ፡ በከመ" ፡ ቀደ ስኮ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቱርባን ፡ በርደተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ቅድሰነ' ፡ ለነኒ" ፣ ካዕበ ፡ እምአበሳነ ፡ ዘኅቡእ ፣ ወዘገሃድ ። ወአርሕቅ ፡ እምኔነ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ኅሊና ፡ዘኢይሠምር ፡ቲጊሩትከ'' ፡ወአንጽሐነ ፡ፍጡነ' ፣ ፍጹመ ፡ለነፍሳቲነ ፡ወሥ ጋነ ፡ ወመንፈስነ ፡ ወኅሊናነ ፡ ወአልባቢነ ፡ ከመ ፡ በልብ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ወበብርህት ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ወከናፍር ፡ ንጹሐት ፡ንትኀበል!'' ፡ በሞገስ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፍርሀት ፡ወንሰምየከ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በከመ ፡ መሐረነ ፡ ወልድከ ፡ ዋሕድከ”" ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣፡ ወይቤለነ ፣ ሶበ ፡ ትጹልዩ ፡ በሉ ፣ ከመዝ ። ይብሉ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡፣ ሕዝብ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዘበሰማያት ፣ ይትቀደስ ፡ ስምከ"! ፣ ትምጻእ ፡ መንግሥትከ ፡ ወይኩን ፡ ፈቃድከ ፡ በከመ ፡ በሰ ማይ ፡ከማሁ ፡ በምድርሣጓ ፡ሲሳየነ ፡ዘለለ ፡ ዕለትነ ፡ ሀበነ ፡ ዮም ፡ ኅድግ ፡ለነ ፡አበ ሳነ ፡ወጌጋየነ ፡ከመ ፡ንሕነኒ ፡ንኅድግ ፡ለዘአበሰ ፡ለነ ፡ወኢታብአነ ፡ውስተ ፡መን ሱት ፡ አላ ፡ አድኅነነ ፡ ወባልሐነ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ እኩይ ፡ በኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እግ ዚእነ፡ ፡አሜን ።
impossible for Him to purify him from them, whether he communicates or does not communicate, just as He purifies from all sins all those upon whom He descends in holy baptism. Epiclesis: second part and the “Our Father” 16 After that, when the Holy Spirit descends upon the holy offerings, the priest says: “Lord, you are faithful to your covenant! As you have sanctified this offering by the descent of the Holy Spirit upon it, so sanctify also us from our sin, the hidden one and the manifest one. Remove from us every thought that does not please your goodness, purify us quickly, completely; our souls, our body, our spirit, our thought, our hearts, so that with a pure heart and a luminous soul, with pure lips we may dare with grace, without fear to call you ‘our Father’ as your only-begotten Son Jesus Christ taught us and said to us: ‘When you pray say this’”. All the people say: “Our Father who art in heaven, hallowed be thy name, thy kingdom come, thy will be done on earth as it is in heaven, give us this day our daily bread, forgive us our sin and our error as we also forgive those who have sinned against us and lead us not into temptation but save us and deliver us from all evil through Jesus Christ our Lord. Amen” (Mt 6:9-13). Catechesis on the “Our Father”

17 Observe, O beloved, how great such a prayer is at this time because Christ, the only-begotten Son of God, is present together with them and they stand in His presence. By saying to His Father: “Our Father who art in heaven” they manifest

ሥቱ ፣ ከመ ፣ እሙንቱ ፣ ሱቱፋን ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ በልደት ፡ መለኮታዊት ፡ ወይስእ ልዎ፤ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይቀድስ ፡ ስመ ፡ ልደት ፡፣ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይስምዩ፡ ፡ ነፍ ሶሙ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ጸላእቱ ፡ በምግባራቲሆሙ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይስአልዎ፤ ፣ ከመ ፡ያምጽእ ፡ሎሙ ፡መንግሥቶ ፡ዘውእቱ ፡መንፈሱ ፡ቅዱስ ፡ከመ ፡ይማእ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ጸላእቶሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ያጌብርዎሙ ፡ ለኃጢአት ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይስአልዎ፣ ከመ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ፈቃዶ፤ ፡ዲበ ፡ምድር ፡በከመ ፡በሰማይ ፡ውስተ፡፣ መላእክት ፡ ወሥምረቱሰ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፡ ወቅዳሴሁ ፡ ወትረ ። እስመ ፣መላ እክት ፡ ይሴብሕዎ ፡ ወይቄድስዎ ፡፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፅርዐት ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ እመሰ፣ ተሀከዩ ፡እምወድቁ ፡በከመ ፣ወድቀ ፡ዲያብሎስ ፡ወሠራዊቱ ። ወከማሁ ፡ይደሉ ፣ ላዕለ ፡ እለ ፡ የአምኑ" ፡ ቦቱ ፡ እምደቂቀ ፡ እጓለ ፡፣ እመሕያው ፡ ከመ ፣ ይሰብሕዎ፣ ወይቀድስዎ ፡ወትረ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሀኬት ፡ ወይስአልዎ'" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ ሎሙ፣7፣
that together with Him they participate in the divine birth and ask Him to sanctify the name of (their) birth within them so as not to call themselves “sons”, while they are His enemies in their actions. Furthermore, they ask Him to bring them His kingdom, that is, His Holy Spirit to defeat from them their enemies who subject them to sin. They also supplicate Him that His will be done within them on earth, as in heaven among the angels. His will is His glorification and His sanctification always. Indeed, the angels glorify Him and sanctify Him without interruption because if they were to become lazy, they would fall as the devil and his army fell. It is similarly incumbent upon those who believe in Him among the children of man that they glorify Him and sanctify Him always, without laziness, and ask Him to fulfill this for them with His good pleasure, always.
ዘንተ ፡በሥምረቱ ፡ወትረ ። ወበእንተ ፣ዘይብሉ''! ፡ ሲሳየነ ፡ዘለሳኒታ ፡ሀበነ ፡ዮም ፡ ዝብሂል ፡ በዓለም ፡ ዘይመጽእ ፡በሰማይ ፡እስመ ፡ኅብስቶሙ ፣ዘይቤ፤"' ፡ውእቱ"'፤ ፡በዓለም ፡ዘይመጽእ ፣ ኅብስተ" ፣ ዘይቤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኅብስተ!፤ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፣ ወቅዳሴሁ ። ወሥጋሁ ፡ ወደሙ ፣ ዝውእቱ ፣ ኅብስተ ፣ መላእክት ፡ ስብሐታት ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፡ ወሥጋ ፡ ወደም ፡ እስመ ፣ መላእክት ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ኅብስተ"' ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ግብር ፡ቦቱ ፡ የሐይው ፡ ወይትፌሥሑ ። ወከማሁ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ለእመ፣ ኮኑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ዘለሳኒታ ፡ የሐይው ፡ ባቲ ፡ ከማሆሙ'' ። ወይእቲ" ፡ ኅብ ስቶሙ ፡ በውስተ ፣ ሰማይ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይስእልዎ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በገዓር ፣ እንዘ ፣ ይብሉ ፡ ስብሐቲከ ፡ ወቅዳሴከ ፡ ወዮርባንከ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ሲሳየነ ፡ ዘሳኒታ ፣ በሰማይ ፣ ሀበነ ፡ ዮም ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ። እስመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢተሠነቅነ፣ ዮም ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ኢንነሥእ ፡ ሳኒታ ፡ በሰማይ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይሰአልዎ” ፡
18 Regarding what they said: “Give us this day our daily bread”, this means, for the world that will come in heaven, because the expression “their bread” means “in the world that will come”. What He called bread, the bread of the angels, is the glorification of God and His sanctification. His body and His blood is the bread of the angels (Ps 78:25), (that is), the glorifications and the sanctification, because the angels do not possess bread, if not this action in which they live and rejoice. Similarly, if the faithful will be in the heaven of tomorrow, they will live in it like them. And this bread of theirs is in heaven, therefore they implore God with groaning saying: “Your glorification, your sanctification and your offering, which is our bread of tomorrow in heaven, give it to us today on earth, because if we do not provide for ourselves today on earth we will not obtain (it) tomorrow in heaven”. Therefore they ask Him that He not


[p. 198] {#po.57.p198}

lead them into temptation that prevents them from this, but that He save them from evil in Jesus Christ, without whom there is no salvation (Acts 2:12). Prayers after the “Our Father” 19 Then the priest will answer them saying: “Yes, we pray to you, O holy Father, good who loves goodness, and lead us not into temptation; do not let all sins prevail over us but save us from actions that are not useful, from their thought, from their agitation, from their look, from their touching, destroy him who puts to the test, drive him away from us and rebuke all his agitations that are planted within us. Cut away from us his stratagems that lead us to sin and deliver us with your holy power through Jesus Christ our Lord, amen”. Then the people bow their head and the priest implores: “God who is faithful to his covenant, by the authority that He gave to his disciples so that they might bind and loose all the bonds of sin (Jn 20:23), that He may absolve them from their sins and forgive all their faults, save them from evil and make them reach his heavenly kingdom”. The Eucharistic celebration source of the forgiveness of sins

20 After this long prayer, let him elevate the Body on his hands above all the people, just as He was elevated by them on the wood of the cross (Jn 3:14). All the people should invoke Him as the thief invoked Him: “Remember me, O Lord, when you come into your kingdom” (Lk 23:42). They will be worthy of the forgiveness of all their sins and of the joy in Paradise, just as the thief was worthy. He who is present at the time of the sanctification, with faith and with fear, whether fasting, or having eaten, whether he communicates, or does not communicate, will receive all this grace. If it were not possible for him to communicate, let him recognize the grace he has received. If instead it is possible for him to communicate, he will become like the centurion who said to the Lord: “I am not worthy that you should enter under the roof of my house, but command with your word and my servant will live” (Mt 8:8). He obtained immediately what he desired

with his faith and our Lord admired the beauty of his faith. So it will happen to him who strives and is present during the time of the sanctification, and if he cannot communicate, let him forgo the Eucharist with humility and poverty and with faith he will receive what the centurion received. The duty of the faithful to remain until the conclusion of the Eucharistic celebration. The example of Judas

21 After that, it is incumbent upon the faithful, both he who communicates and he who does not communicate, that he be present in fear and trembling until Christ is elevated from the altar. His elevation is when all His Body is finished and it is finished and His blood is elevated from the chalice. After that, when he has seen that it has been elevated, he will ask Him that the grace of the Holy Spirit which He sent to His disciples after His Ascension (Lk 24:50-51; Acts 2:1-4) may dwell in him. And if he has been present for the entire time of the sanctification and leaves before the elevation of Christ and the elevation of His blood from the chalice, his portion will be together with Judas Iscariot, of whom the holy Gospel says that he, on the night of the day of the Friday of the crucifixion, when Christ communicated to his disciples from his body and from his blood, abandoned the apostles and went out alone, before Christ had gone out.

22 Satan took possession of him immediately when he went out before the holy oblation was finished, because the holy Gospel says that he communicated and went out immediately (Jn 13:26-30) while the disciples remained with Christ until He went out, and with Him they went out. Similarly, all those who go out of the church before the elevation of Christ have become according to the likeness of Judas. Even if they had a great commitment, they must not go out.
