ወኢያገብሮሙ ፡ ለግብረዝ ፣ ዓለም ፡ ወያዕርፍ፤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በሰሚርዐ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ወይትኅሠሥ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ደሞ፡ ፣ እምኔሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ። አላ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይት ጋደል ፡በእንተ ፡በቀዔቶሙ ፣፡በከመ ፡ነፍሱ ፡ወያሜኒ' ፡ሎሙ ፡በተጸምዶቶሙ፡፣፡ ለስብሐቲሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወቅዳሴሁ ፡ ወሰሚዐ ፣ ቃሉ ። ወኢይትገበሩ፤፡፣ በግብር ፣ እምኩሉ ፣ ግብረ ፣ ሥጋ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘይበቀኑዕ ፣ ለሲሳይ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፣ ወዝውእቱ" ፡ መብልዕ ፡ ወመስቴ ፡ዘኢይክል ፡ሥጋ ፡ዘእንበሌሁ ።
stranger who lodges in his house. Let him not subject them to the work of this world and let him rest only by listening to the Word of God: God will require their blood from him, because he sought the good only for himself. It is instead his duty to strive for their profit as for his own and to do them good in the observance of the glorification of God, in his sanctification and in the listening to his Word. And let them not be subjected to any bodily work except what is useful for nourishment, that is, the food and drink without which the body cannot do.

### The consumption of food to support spiritual exercise
ወይግበሩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወቅዳሴ ። ወለእመ ፡ ተክህሎሙ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ቀዳም ። እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ፈትሐ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ምሴት ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይቤ ፣ አዕርፍ ፡ ወአፅ ርፋ ፡ ለብእሲትከ ፡ ወወልደከ ፣ ‹ወገብረከ ፡ ወአመተከ ፡ ወፈላሲ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ቤትከ ፡ ወአዕርፍ ፣ አድገከ ። ወዕረፍተ ፡ አድጉሰ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ዕረፍተ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ዘውእቱ"' ፡ አድገ ፡ነፍሱ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ያዕርፍ'' ፡ በመብልዕ ፡ ወመ ስቴ ፡ በዕለተ ፣ እሑድ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ዕለት" ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሀል፤!፣፡ ለተጸምዶተ ፡አንብቦ ፡መዓልተ ፡ኩለንታሃ ፡ወይኩን ፡ጽኑዓ ፡ወፍሙሐ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ለእመ ፣ ርኅበ ፣ አመክነየ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ በረኀብ ፡ ወይትሀከይ ፣ እምሕ ይወታ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ አዘዘነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ናብልያዖ ፡ ወናስትዮ ፡ ወኢኖ ጹሞ" ፡ በፅለተ ፡ እሑድ ፡ ግሙራ" ፡ ወኢኖድክሞ"፤ ፡ በሰጊድ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ እንስሳዊ ፡ ድኩም ፡ ወያመከኒ ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ደክመ ፡ ወይትሀከይ ፡ ዘንተ'' ፣ ቃለ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈትሐ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ለሥጋ ፡ መብልዕ" ፡ ወመስቴ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጽናዕ ፡ ለግብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ በዕለተ ፡ እሑድ ፡ ወኢያመክኒ"” ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ በምክንያት” ።
23 And let them do this the night before the glorification and sanctification, or, if they can, let them do this on the day of the Sabbath because God has granted this to them, until the evening. He has in fact said: “Rest and make your wife, your son, your slave and your slave-girl, and the stranger who is at your house rest, and make your donkey rest” (Ex 20:10//). By the rest of his donkey he means the rest of his body which is the donkey of his soul. And he ordered that it should rest with food and drink on the day of Sunday, which is the day of his rest, so that he may dedicate himself to reading all day and be steadfast and happy. If in fact he is hungry, he will make excuses to his soul because of the hunger and will become lazy in his life. For this reason God has commanded us that we make him eat and drink, and that we never make him fast on the day of Sunday, that we do not weary him with prostration. It is in fact a weak animal body and when it is tired it makes excuses against us and becomes lazy. This the Word of God has granted to the body: food and drink so that on the day of Sunday it may be steadfast for the work of the spirit and not make excuses against it (i.e., the day of Sunday) with pretexts.

### Invitation to the recipient to imitate the example of Solomon
ነጽር ፡ ኦፍቁር ፡ ዘከሠትኩ ፣ ለከ ፡ ወጠይቆ ፡ ወለብዎ፤! ፡ ወመሀሮሙ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ፡ ወለባዕዳን ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መሠረታ ፡ ለሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡
24 Observe, O beloved, what I have set before you, examine it, understand it and teach it to the faithful and to others because this is the foundation of eternal life, for


[p. 266] {#po.57.p266}
ወለሕይወተ ፣ ዝዓለም ፡፣ ወሥርው ፣ ለክኤልቱ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ በምድር ፡ ወበሰማይ ፡ ወአድኅኖት፤ ፡ እምክኤልቱ፡ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወዕረፍት ፡ እም ፡ ክኤልቱ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ድካም ፡ ወብዕል ፡ እምክኤልቱ ፡ ንዴት ፡ በምድር ፡ ወበሰማይ ። እስመ ፣ አንተ ፡ ተአ ምር ፡ ዘጽሑፍ ፡ በነገሥት! ፣ ከመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አስተርአዮ ፣ ለሰሎሞን ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ተመነይ ፡ እምነየ ፡ ዘፈቀድከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ተመነይኩ፡ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተሀበኒ ፡ ልበ ፡ ጠቢበ ፡ ከመ ፡ አሜኒ' ፡ እፍታሕ ፣፡ ማእከለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ሕዝብ ፡ ፀቢይ ። ይቤ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ወሠምረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በ”ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ እስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ተመነየ ፣ እምኔሁ ፣ ግብረ ፡ ባሰማያዌ ፡ ዘይሂሉ ፡ ኢተመ ነየ ፣ እም''ኔሁ ፣ ግብረ! ፣ ምድራዌ ፡ ኀላፌ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በከመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ኢተመነይከ ፡ ነዊኅ ፡ መዋዕለ ፣ ወኢብዙኃ ፡ ብዕለ'፤ ፡ ወኢነፍሰ ፡ ጸላእ ተከ'፤ ፡ አላ ፡ ተመነይከ ፡ እምኔየ" ፡ ጥበበ ፣ ወውእቱ" ፡ ግብር ፡ ዘያሠምረኒ ። አንሰ" ፣ እሁበከ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ዘተመነይከ ፣ እምኔየ ፡ ዘኢወሀብኩ ፡ ለመኑሂ ፡ እምቅድ ሜከ ፣ ወኢእም ፡ ድኅሬከ ። ወእዌስከከ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ዘኢተመነይከ'' ፡ እምኔየ ፣ ነዊኀ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ወብዙኃ ፡ ብዕለ ፡ ወነፍሰ” ፡ ጸላእተከ ። ነጽር ፡ ኦፍቁር” ፡ ከመ ፣፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ሶበ ፡ፈቀደ ፡ግብረ ፡ዘይሄሉ ፡ባሕቲቱ”'፡ወኢኀሠሠ ፡ግብረ ፡ ኀላፌ ፡ ወወሀቦ! ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘይሂሉ ፡ ዘተመነየ ፡ ወወሰኮ ፣፡ ላዕለ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ግብር ፡
the life of this world; the root of the twofold grace on earth and in heaven; and salvation from the twofold condemnation; the rest from the twofold work of toil, and the wealth from the twofold poverty, on earth and in heaven. You know what is written in the (books of) Kings that God appeared to Solomon and said to him: “Ask of me what you desire!” (1 Kgs 3:5). Solomon said to him: “I have wanted from you that you give me a wise heart so that I may exercise justice well in the midst of this great people” (1 Kgs 3:9). The book says: “This word pleased God because he desired from him a heavenly thing that remains” (1 Kgs 3:10). He did not desire from Him a transitory earthly thing. And God said to him: “Since you have not aspired to long days, nor much wealth, nor the life of your enemies, but from me you have desired wisdom and it is this fact that pleases me, I will give you the wisdom that you wanted from me and that I did not give to anyone before you, nor after you. To this I will add to you what you did not aspire to from me, long days, much wealth and the soul of your enemies” (1 Kgs 3:11-13). Observe, O beloved, that when Solomon desired only a thing that remains and did not seek a transitory thing, God gave him the lasting thing he had desired and added to this transitory thing, what he had not desired because He knows that he needs it.

### The reward for those who dedicate themselves to Sunday prayer
ኀላፌ= ፣ ዘኢተመነየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡የአምር ፡ከመ ፡ውእቱ ፡ይፈቅዶ” ። ወከማሁ ፡ ይቤ ፡በወንጌል”፥+ ኅሙ ፡ መንግሥትየ ፡ ወጽድቅየ ፡ ወአነ ፡ እዌ ስከክሙ ፡ዘትፈቅዱ ፡ እምግብረዝ” ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ መብልዕ ፡ ወመስቴ ፡ ወአ ልባስ ። ከማሁ ፡ ኩሉ ፣ ዘይጸመድ ፣ ለስብሐተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወቅዳሴሁ ፡ ወሰሚዐ ፣ ቃሉ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ እሑድ ፡ ኩለንታነሃ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ መንግሥተ ፣ ሰማያት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ይሁቦ ፡ዝክተ ፡ወይዌስኮ” ፡ ሠናየ ፡ወፅረፍተ ፡በኩሉ ፣ ዘያጽሕቆ ፡ እምግብረዝ ፣ ዓለም ፡ መብልዐ?' ፡ ወመስቴ ፣ወአልባሰ ፡በከመ ፡ገብረ ፣ ለሰሎሞን” ።
25 Thus he says in the Gospel: “Seek my kingdom and my justice and I will add to you what you need of the things of this world, that is, food, drink and clothing” (Mt 6:33). Likewise, all those who dedicate themselves assiduously to the glorification of God, to his sanctification, to the listening to his Word throughout the whole day of Sunday, with this they desire the Kingdom of Heaven. God will give them that and will add to them good and rest in everything for which they strive in the affairs of this world, food, drink, clothing, just as he did with Solomon.
ወዘኢይጸመድ ፣ በዝንቱ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ አፍለጠ” ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ እመንግሥቱ”፣ ዘለዓለም ፡ ወወረሰ ፣ ኩነኔ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ያመነድቦ ፡ በዝዓለም ፣ የወያደክሞ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፣ ወይትኅጐል ፣ እምክልኤ ፣ ፅረፍት ፣ ኅቡረ ፡ በሰማይ ፡ ወበምድር ። እስመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጻድቅ” ፣ ወከ ሀሊ ። ወዘሰ ፡ ይጸመድ ፣ ለስብሐተ” ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወያብዕል ፡ ምስለ ፡ እግዚ
26 Whoever instead does not dedicate himself to this, has separated his soul from his eternal kingdom and has inherited eternal condemnation, (and) God will afflict him in this world, will subject him to toil throughout all the days of his life; he will lose both rests together, in heaven and on earth. God is in fact just (2 Thess 1:6) and powerful. As for him who dedicates himself to the glorification of God and celebrates

together with God throughout the whole day of his resurrection, he will be together with God also on the day of the resurrection of the dead to celebrate in glory and honor just as he did on earth on the day of Sunday. Whoever instead does not commit himself to the glorification of God and to his sanctification and to the listening to his Word on the day of Sunday, will fall from the glory of God and from his honor, just as the devil and his army fell when they denied themselves the glorification and sanctification on the day of Sunday. They in fact fell in it, and in it will fall all those who do not dedicate themselves assiduously to the glorification of God and to his sanctification; as (those) fell, (these too) will fall. 27 I have already set before you in the book of the illustration of the spiritual struggle that those burdened by work are bound to the midnight prayer of the day of Sunday and that on the day of Sunday they should dedicate themselves to the glorification and sanctification, in the five times: matins, the third, the sixth, the ninth, vespers, in addition to reading throughout the day. Since they are exempt from transitory work, they must dedicate themselves to the prayer of the hours in addition to reading. Instead, on the other six days they are not obligated except as I have declared to you in the book of the struggle, that they pray where they are, according to the measure of their strength and their knowledge, and God will hear them while they carry out their work. Epilogue and announcement of the next homily
ኦፍቁር ፡ናሁ ፡ከሠትኩ ፣ለከ ፡ክብሮ ፣ለዕለተ ፡ሰንበት ፡ወቦቱ ፡፡ሕይወተ ፡ መሃይምኖናን፡፡ ፡ ወትፍሥሕቶሙ ፡ በምድር” ፡ ወበሰማያት፤ ። ለብዎ ፡ ወግበር” ፡ ቦቱ” ፤ ወመሀሮሙ ፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ዘትረክብ” ፡ እምኩሎሙ።፤። ፣ መሃይምናን ። ወናሁ ፣ሀሎኩ ፣እከሥት” ፣ለከ ፡ምክንያተ ፡ጾመ ፡ክልኤቱ” ፡መዓልት ፡ረቡዕ፣ ወዓርብ ፡ በሰላመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር”፤ ፡ አሜን” ።
28 O beloved, behold I have manifested to you the honor of the day of Sunday: in it is the life of the faithful and their joy on earth and in the heavens. Understand it and act in it and teach it to all those you meet among all the faithful. Now I am preparing to explain to you the reason for the fasting of the two days, of Wednesday and Friday, in the peace of God, amen.

### HOMILY VII

Proem. Apostolic origin of the fast of Wednesday and Friday
ድርሳን ፡ሳብዕ ፡በእንተ ፡ከሚት'፤ ፡ምክንያተ፤ ፡ጾም ፡ክልኤ ፡ዕለተ ፡ረቡዕ ፡ ወዐርብ ። ኦእኀ' ፡ ፍቁር ፡፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያብርህ ፡ አዕይንተ ፡ ልብኩ' ፡ በብር ሃነ ፡ መንፈስ" ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ናዛዚ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትለቡ ፡ ምስጢራተ! ፡ ፣መለኮቶ ተካየድ ኩከ ፡ከመ ፡እክሥት?' ፡ለከ ፡ምክንያተ ፡ጾም ፡ጾመ ፡ክልኤ ፡መዓልት ፡ረቡዕ፣ ወዐርብ ። ለቡ ፡ዘእቤለከ ፡ ወአእምር ፡ ጥያቄሁ ፡ ወኢትግድፎ" ፡ እስመ ፣ አበው ፣ ሐዋርያት ፡ቅዱሳን ፡ይቤሉ ፡በቀኖናሆሙ፤፤ ፡ ቭ ቿ ወ ፪ ፡ካህንሰ'፡ ፡ዘኢይጸውም ፣ ፪፤› ፣ መዓልተ ፡ረቡዓ'" ፡ ወዐርብ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይሰዐር ፡ እምክህነቱ ። ወለእመ ፡፣ኮነ፣ ሕዝባዊ" ፡ ይሰደድ እም ፡ ቤተ ፣ክርስቲያን ። መተሩ ፡ ወአውገዙ ፡ከመ ፡ካህን ፣ ወሕዝባዊ ፡ ውጉዛን ፡ እምእግዚአብሔር ፡ ለእመ" ፡፣ ኢጾሙ ፡ እሉ ፡ ክልኤቱ''፣ መዓልተ" ፡ወትረ ። ለቡ ፡ዘእቤሌከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትጠይቅ ፡ምክንያተ ፡ ዝንቱ ።
1 Seventh homily. Exposition on the reason for the fasting of the two days: Wednesday and Friday. O beloved brother, may the Lord illuminate the eyes of your heart with the light of the Holy Spirit the Comforter so that you may understand the mysteries of his divinity. I promised you that I would explain to you the reason for the fasting, for the fasting of the two days: Wednesday and Friday. Understand what I tell you, recognize its certainty and do not neglect it because the fathers, the holy apostles in their canon 64 say: “The priest who does not always fast on the two days, Wednesday and Friday, let him be deposed from his priesthood, if he is a layman let him be expelled from the church”. They decided, under penalty of anathema, that the priest and the layman, if they do not always fast on these two days, are excommunicated from God. Pay attention to what I tell you so that you may be certain of the reason for this.
ሶበ ፣ አውጽኦሙ ፡እግዚአብሔር ። ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡እምድረ ፡ግብጽ ፡ ሴሰዮሙ ፡ በገዳም ፡ ጓ ፣ ዓመተ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አብኦሙ! ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ፣ ከናአን ፣ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ምድረ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወአንገሦሙ ፣ ውስቴታ ፡ ወወሀ ቦሙ ፣ መዊአ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰብዐቱ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ እለ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ባቲ ፣ ቀተልዎሙ ፣ ወነግሥ ፣ ባቲ ። ወሀለዉ ፡ በአግዋሪሆሙ፥ ፣ አሕዛብ ፡ ከሐድያን ፡ እለ ፡ የኀድሩ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አጽኖፈ፣ ፡ ባሕር ፡ እምጋዛን ፡ ወአስቀሉኒ' ፡ እስከ ፡ መርሶ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ፍልስጥኤማዊያን ፡ ወከናአናዊያን ። ወኮኑ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል!" ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ዘይኤ ብሱ'! ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ወያነሥእ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ አሕዛበ" ፣ እለ ፡ የኀድሩ ፡ በአግ ዋሪሆሙ ፡ወያዐግትዎሙ" ፡፣ ወያመነድብዎሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይትመየጡ ፣ ኅበ ፡ እግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ወይኔስሑ” ፣ ወይሰድዶሙ! ፡ እምኔሆሙ ። ወቀዳሚ"' ፡ አዘዞሙ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ከመ ፡ይሕንጹ ፡ሎቱ፤' ፡አሐደ ፡ቤተ ፡መቅደስ ፡በአሐቲ ፡ሀገር፣፣ ወይሠዑ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ ወቀዮሩርባናተ ፡ ወኢይከውኖሙ ፡ ምሥ ዋዓ" ፡ በካልእ ፡ መካን ፣ ዘእንበሌሁ ። ወሐነጸ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፣ መቅደሰ ፡ በሀ ገረ ፣፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወውእቱ ፣ ቀዳሚ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ዘተሐንጸ ፡ በኢየሩሳ ሌም" ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወተሐንጸ” ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ምሥ ዋዐ ፡ ለጐርባን ፡ እስመ ፡ ቤተ ፣ መቅደስ ፣ ውእቱ"! ፣ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወም ሥዋፅ፡፡ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ይሠውዑ” ፡ላፅሌሁ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ከኖአን ፡ እስከ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅ ደስ ፡፻ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ በበዓመት” ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በኦሪት ፡ በበዓለ ፡ ናእት ፡ወበዓለ ፡2” ፡ወበዓለ” ፤ ጥምቀት ፡ዘውእቱ ፡በዓለ ፡ መጸለት” ፡ ይበጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ቤተ ፡መቅደስ” ። ወበእሉ ፡፻ ፡ ጊዜያት ፡ ይሠውዑ” ፡ መሥዋዕቶሙ ፡ወቀ፦ ርባኖቲሆሙ ። ወረገመ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፣ለኩሉ ፡ዘይሠውዕ ፡ሎቱ” ፡እምኔሆሙ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ አው ፣ ባዘዐጥን ፡ ፅጣነ ፡ ሎቱ" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ምድር፣7፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ በቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ዘበሀገረ”ፆ፡ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ።
Trials of Israel in the desert and in the land of Canaan. God requires a single place of worship: Jerusalem 2 When God brought the children of Israel out of the land of Egypt, he nourished them in the desert for forty years; after which, he introduced them into the land of Canaan which is the land of Jerusalem and made them reign in it. He gave them victory over seven peoples (Ex 3:8, 17) who lived in it, they killed them and reigned over it. In their vicinity there were traitorous peoples who lived along all the ends of the sea, from Gaza and Ashkelon up to the port of the Philistines and the Canaanites. When the children of Israel sinned against God, he raised against them peoples who lived in their vicinity; they surrounded them and afflicted them until they returned to God and were converted and (God) drove them away from them. And at the beginning God commanded them to build him a temple in a city and that they should sacrifice to him in it a sacrifice and offerings and that they should not have another altar outside of it. And Solomon built for Him a temple in the city of Jerusalem and this is the first temple in all the earth that was built for God in Jerusalem. In it an altar was built for the offering because the temple is the church and on the altar inside it they immolate the sacrifice. All the children of Israel were accustomed to come from all the land of Canaan to that temple three times every year as God had ordered them in the Torah (Dt 16). They arrived at the temple, at the feast of unleavened bread, at the feast of Pentecost and at the feast of the baptism, that is, at the feast of tabernacles. In these three times they immolate their sacrifice and their offerings and God cursed all those among them who would have sacrificed to him or who would have incensed for him the incense in all the ends of the earth, outside the temple of the city of Jerusalem (Zech 14:16-19).

### Prophecies on the coming of Christ the liberator

3 Among them there were those through whom God spoke and they prophesied that God would send them Christ, and that he would destroy their enemies and would save them from them and would make them inherit their land and would make their dead live, and that the living among them would not return to die for eternity. The prophets were accustomed to pronounce this word to the children of Israel but these did not understand its meaning because the word of the prophets that Christ would liberate them from their enemies, this means, from the devil and his army. They said to them that He would make them inherit the land of their enemies; this means that He would make them inherit the heavenly rank of the devil from which he and his army had fallen. And what he said to them that he would make their dead live means that He would make them rise from Sheol to the life of the Paradise of delight: this is the eternal life for which they live forever and that they will not return to die, as I have set before you in the book of the explanation of the humanation of the Son of God and of his crucifixion in the book of the hermeneutics of the Torah. History of Israel in summary: from the exodus to the Roman occupation

the kingdom of Babylon. They built the temple and returned to their worship, to their customs and to their feasts. They were under the regime of the kings of Persia until Alexander, king of the Greeks, defeated them, until Rome defeated the Greeks and they became under the regime of Rome. The king of Rome resided in Rome and from there appointed the officials of the city of Jerusalem and its surroundings. When Rome defeated the children of Israel they thought that when Christ came to liberate them from them, as God had promised, their enemies were them. Many of them became secretaries of the governors of Rome and served them and informed them about the nobility of their law and said to them: “God has promised us that he will send us Christ our king and will liberate us from you and will make us reign over you”. And the Romans mocked them listening to this discourse, because Rome and all the peoples worshipped idols. The Magi kings in Bethlehem

Arrived at the city of Jerusalem, they entered it preaching and saying: “Where is the king of the Jews who is born? We have in fact seen a star in the east and have come to worship him” (Mt 2:2). elected by Rome over the city of Rome was called Caesar. Herod was set in an eminent manner over Jerusalem, vicar of Caesar.

### Herod's reaction to the visit of the Magi

6 Herod was very frightened to hear that the king of the Jews was born. He remembered what the Jewish secretaries used to discuss with him regarding him (i.e., Christ). Herod had the Jews come and asked them: “Where did your prophets tell you that your king would be born?”. They confirmed to him that the prophet Micah had said that he would be born in Bethlehem (Mi 5:1/ LXX 5:2). He had the Magi come and asked them: “How many years is it since you saw the star?”. They said to him: “Two years”. He said to them: “Go to Bethlehem and search for the child and if you find him, inform me also so that I may come and prostrate myself to him” (Mt 2:7-8). He said this to them with perfidy, so that if by chance he had found him, he would have killed him, and a king who would have destroyed the Romans would not have survived for the Jews. When they went out of Jerusalem, the star appeared to them and went before them until it made them arrive at the place inside which Christ was, and they prostrated themselves to him. He ordered them in a dream that they should not return to Herod and that they should go by another way to their country. Flight into Egypt and return to Galilee after the death of Herod. Growth and public life of Christ

killed thousands of children in Bethlehem and all its surroundings (Mt 2:16). After Herod died, Joseph returned by order of the angel of God to the land of Jerusalem and dwelt in a city among the cities of the land of Canaan named Nazareth and his work is from Galilee (Mt 2:22-23). Christ was raised in it and grew like us in everything except sin (Heb 4:15) because he came to break sin from us (for our benefit) and to liberate us from it (Mt 1:21). He dwelt in Nazareth until his years were thirty, like Adam at the moment of his creation and manifested his power to the children of Israel his people with signs and wonders. He took for himself from them eighty-four disciples; twelve he called apostles (Lk 6:13) and the seventy-two (Lk 10:1) who followed them he called disciples. People thought that He was a prophet and others said that perhaps he was Christ but they did not dare to express this for fear of Rome. The twelve apostles had clarified that he was Christ and thought in the same way as the Jews that he would destroy Rome and would make them reign over their land.

### Reactions to the person of Christ
በምድሮሙ"” ። ወኢኮነ ፡ በኀቤሆሙ ፡ አእምሮ"፣ በእንተ ፣ ዲያብሎስ፡፣ ወሠራዊቱ ። ወኮነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ያንሶሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ሐዋር ያቲሁ ፡ ወአርድእት'' ፡ ወኢይትኀበል ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ይክሥት" ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ክር ስቶስ ፡ እምፍርሃተ ፡ ሮም ። ወኮነ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወአርዳኢሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ለለ ፡ዓመት ፡በእሉ ፡፻ ፡በዓላት ፡ዘዘከርናሆሙ ፡ቀዲሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይበውእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምኩራብ ፡ ይጌስሥ ጾሙ” ፣፡ ለካህናተ ፡፣ አይሁድ ፣ ወለጸሐፍ ቶሙ ፣ ለፈሪሳውያን” ፣ እለ ፣ እሙንቱ"! ፡ ይሰምይዎሙ ፡ ዮም”= ፣ መገብተ ። ይጌሥጾሙ” ፣ በእንተ ፡ እከየ ፡ ምግባሮሙ” ፣ ወበእንተ ፡ ፍቅሮሙ ፡ ለብሩር”"፣
8 There was not in them the knowledge of the devil and his army. Christ traveled throughout the land of Judea and with him his apostles and the disciples but no one dared to express that he was Christ, for fear of Rome. He and his disciples went to the city of Jerusalem every year in those three festivities that we remembered at the beginning. When he entered the temple, he admonished the priests of the Jews, their scribes, the Pharisees, those who today call administrators. He admonished them for their bad work and for their love for silver and for their enslavement to it rather than to the law of God that hung over them. They hated him and envied him and acted so that


[p. 282] {#po.57.p282}
ወተገብሮቶሙ፤፣ ባቲ፣ እምትምህርተ፡፣ ሕገ፡፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘይደሉ፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ። ወኮኑ ፣ ይጸልእዎ ፡፣ ፣ወይቀንኡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ! ፣ ‹ወረሰይዎሙ ፡ ለሕ ዝብ ፡ይጸልእዎ ፡ወይጸርፉ፥ ፡ላዕለ ፡ መንክራቲሁ ። ወይቤሉሎ' ፡ ‹እስመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ አልቦ ፣ እምእግዚአብሔር ፡ አላ ፣ እም ፡ ሰይጣን ፣ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይስዕሮ ፣ ለሰንበት ፣ ወያነሥኦሙ ፡ ለሙታን ፡ ወቦቲ፣ ይፌውስ"'፣ ድዉያነ'!፡ በቃሉ፣ ወያአዝዞሙ ፡ ኢይፍትሑ ፡ አንስቲያሆሙ፤፡ ፡ ወኢይንሥኡ ፣ ብዙኃን"! ፡ አንስተ ፡ ወኦሪትሰ” ፡ አዘዘት ፡ በዝንቱ ።
the people hated him and insulted his wonders and said that it was not from God but from Satan (Mt 9:32-34; 12:21-25) because he transgresses the Sabbath (Mt 12:1-8) and raises the dead and in it heals the sick with his word (Mt 12:9-13). He orders that they do not divorce their wives and that they do not take many women while the Torah has commanded this (Mt 5:32; 19:3-9). With this discourse they corrupted the heart of the people. However, the people, for the great wonders they saw, did not obey them and did not listen to them. He lived in this way for three years and no one dared to believe that he was the Christ for fear of Rome.

### The glorious entry of Jesus into Jerusalem

9 At the age of thirty-three years from his birth he went together with his disciples to Jerusalem before the feast of unleavened bread, according to his habit. When he arrived at a village near Jerusalem named Bethany, that which today is called ‘Azrawit, he spent the night of Sunday there, that is, the tenth of the month of Nesan, five days before the feast of the Passover of the Jews (Jn 12:1-8). The morning of the day of Sunday he went out of the village and ordered his disciples to go to the village named Bethphage and bring him a donkey and a colt (Mt 21:1-2). They spread their clothes over them and in one go our Lord Jesus Christ climbed onto it. When his disciples saw this, it seemed to them that He had begun to reign and to destroy the Romans, enemies of the Jews.

Judah and God promised him that Christ our Lord would be born from his lineage. They shouted to him (saying): “Save us from the Romans our enemies!” and David remembered this prophecy in Psalm 116. He said: “Our Lord deliver us, Lord smooth our path, blessed is he who comes in the name of God” (Ps 117:25-26 LXX). Thus they said: “Save us O son of David! Blessed is he who comes in the name of God”. David did not prophesy about their salvation from Rome, but rather from the devil and his army, but the disciples and the Jews thought that he would liberate them from Rome. When they shouted this word from the top of the Mount of Olives, a high mountain that rises from the city of Jerusalem, their voice reached all the Jews who dwell in the city and the strangers who came for the feast of the Passover from all the cities. The crowds acclaim Jesus. Purification of the temple (Jn 2:13-17)

11 Everyone went out to welcome him and in their hands there were palm branches and olive branches and they shouted before him: “Hosanna son of David” (Mt 21:8-9). Everyone believed that he was the Christ their king for the wonders they had seen previously and they were convinced that he would destroy the Romans, their enemies, with the voice of his mouth (Is 11:4) and they did not fear them when they declared his work. They traveled in his name for the great love towards him and out of respect for him they did not let the donkey and the colt walk on the earth but spread their clothes under their feet. When

he entered the city of Jerusalem, and entered their temple and everyone was before him, he made the command just as kings do. He found in fact in the temple those who sold and bought cows and sheep because that day was the tenth of the month. That was the day in which God had ordered them in the Torah to purchase the sacrifice of the Passover to be slaughtered on the night of unleavened bread as I set before you in the book of the explanation of the lamb. He gave order to his disciples that they should make them go out of the temple with lashes: this is the work (= prerogative) of kings. He healed the blind and the lame (Mt 14:13-21; 15:29-37): this is the work (= prerogative) of God.

### The activity of Jesus before his arrest

12 He taught the people the word of God in conformity with the ministry of the priests; however, envy and fury increased in the priests. Their hope was uprooted and when they saw him already reigning they were convinced that they were destroyed. All the people obeyed him and Pilate, the Roman governor in the city, was silent and did not speak and did not know what had happened. The evening of the day of Sunday he returned to Bethany, spent the night there and went to the city on the day of Monday and found that all the people had preceded him in the temple to rejoice with him and to receive what they had hoped from him: the destruction of their enemy, the resurrection of their dead and their eternal life. He spent all that day in the temple teaching them and rebuking their priests. The evening he returned to Bethany and spent the night there and went to the city, entered the temple and taught the people just as he had done on the day of Sunday and Monday. There were among them those who said to one another: “When will he destroy our enemies?”. Their teachers said to them: “On the day of Friday he will destroy them and will liberate us from them because that is the day of Passover and in it he liberated our fathers from Pharaoh and his army with the slaughtering of the lamb and in it he will liberate us also from our Roman enemies”.

13 The evening of the third day he returned to Bethany, he and his twelve disciples and the morning of the day of Wednesday the apostles wanted him to enter the city. He said to them: “Know that the day of Friday will be the Passover, the slaughtering of the lamb and I will be crucified” (Mt 26:1). Upon hearing this word they did not understand the force of his word, and their joy turned into mourning, their life into death, their kingdom into destruction. This was the greatest mourning that entered their hearts. But Judas doubted instantly and entered the city alone and met all the people waiting for Christ in the temple, as (they did) every day. When they saw Judas, they stood up and welcomed him because he was his disciple. They said to him: “Where is the master?”. He smiled mocking them and said to them: “He has fled”. This discourse was for them harder than a two-edged sword because what they had hoped for was annihilated in them; that he would save them from their enemies and would make them reign over their land, the resurrection of their dead and their eternal life. Fear of the people. The priests plot against Jesus and make a deal with Judas on Wednesday

For this reason they were saddened for themselves and for their goods much more than the apostles. The priests found a pretext to divide them, remembering the admonitions against them. They agreed unanimously in their conclave

that they would search for him wherever he was (Jn 11:57), to win the sympathy of Rome on his account, before (Rome) would require him from them, and that perhaps it would forgive them their misdeeds. They said to one another: “Who will guide us (to go) to his place?”. They took counsel and came to Judas, his disciple, one of the twelve and inquired about his place. They promised him thirty pieces of silver and agreed that he would deliver him to them. All this event happened on the day of Wednesday and in it the disciples of our Lord and those among the people who had believed in him, mourned, while instead in it there was joy for the priests, for the scribes, for the Pharisees his enemies, for Judas, for the devil and his army. The reason for the fasting of Wednesday

15 They took counsel to kill our Lord, and for this reason the apostles ordered us to fast always on the day of Wednesday, every week, in order to be participants together with them in their mourning on earth, and to be participants together with them in their joy in the heavens. They excommunicated every priest and layman who would not fast it, because on that day they participate with Judas, with the devil and his army, and with the priests of the Jews in their joy, because fasting is mourning while food is joy. As for Judas, he went out to Bethany and spent the night of Thursday there with our Lord and his disciples and stayed with them.

### The Last Supper. The arrest of Jesus

devil and from his army, just as a lamb was sacrificed, a salvation for the children of Israel from Pharaoh and his army. He said to Judas: “O Judas! What you must do, do it quickly” (Jn 13:27). He began to pray a long prayer with prostration, sweat, sadness, and with the vigil (Lk 22:39-44) in order to hide his divinity from the devil as I illustrated to you in the book of the incarnation of the Son of God and of his crucifixion. It was so until Judas arrived to him and together with him many of the servants of the high priests and Jews. At midnight on Friday (Mt 26:47-48), they arrested him and bound him, as they bind the wicked and brigands. They led him to the courtyard of the high priest and no one was present with him except Peter, because everyone had abandoned him and fled (Mt 26:56). Peter followed him from afar (Mt 26:58) and did not have the courage to declare himself, to say that he was his disciple because of the great fear that reached him on the night of Friday. The violence against Jesus Christ. The passion of Christ as ransom for Adam and Eve 17 | The high priests, the teachers, and the scribes mocked him and spat on his face, they slapped him from the rest of the night until the morning. On the morning of the day of Friday they gave him to Pilate, the Roman governor, and reported about him that he had elected himself king. Pilate refuted and opposed them but the priests said to the people: “What Pilate is saying to you is to test you, to see your hypocrisy in what you do by handing him over.” All the people shouted saying: “Crucify him! Crucify him” (Mt 27:22). They shouted saying this until the third hour of the day of Friday (Mt 27:22). “This man said that he is the king of the Jews and you, O Pilate, if you release him will have become an enemy of Caesar your lord” (Jn 19:12). Pilate feared and gave him to the servants of his Roman lord who were standing by him. They scourged him for our sins at the third hour of the day and that is the hour in which Adam was created, on the day of Friday; furthermore, they mocked him as they do against those who rebel against kings. They clothed him in a scarlet cloak (Mt 27:28)

in the likeness of the robes of kings, (they put) a reed in his hand (Mt 27:29) in the likeness of a staff, a crown of thorns on his head (Mt 27:29). They did this against him at the third hour in which Adam was created and he was promised the eternal kingdom. 18 They clothed him in luminous robes and girded him with the crown of the angels so that he could be in the exalted order from which the devil and his army fell. They clothed our Lord in royal robes, they mocked him because of the robes of Adam from which he was stripped for his transgression, (they put on him) a crown of thorns in place of the royal crown that was taken from him for his transgression in the fourth hour in which he named all the beasts and the birds. In it (the fourth hour) they issued the death sentence against our Lord and made him go out to crucify him. In the fifth hour, in which Eve was created from the rib of Adam (Gn 2:21-22) on the day of Friday, in it our Lord carried the wood and they made him go out to hang him upon it. In the sixth hour in which Adam and Eve moved with their legs toward the tree and stretched their hands toward it and ate from its fruit, in it (i.e., the sixth hour) they crucified his hands and his feet on the wood of the cross, with his hands and his feet stretched out in place of the tree from which Adam ate. Jesus accepts the bitterness of the death of Friday 19 As Adam ate from the tree at the sixth hour on the day of Friday and became naked of the robes of the angels, so our Lord was hung on the wood, naked, from the sixth hour until the ninth hour. As he expelled Adam from Paradise at the ninth hour of the day of Friday, so in it our Lord tasted vinegar

ዘድሙር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሐሞት ፡ህየንተ፤ ፡ መሪር ፡ ሞት ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኢሞተ 7፣ በግፍዕ ፡ በይብስተ ፡ ደም ፣ እምፍርነት" ፡ አላ ፡ ሞተ ፡ በፈቃዱ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይይብስ፡ ፣ደሙ ። ጥዕመ ፡መሪረ ፡ብሒአ ፡በ8 ፡ሰዓት ፡ህየንተ ፡ምረረ ፡ሞት ዘጥዕመ ፡ አዳም ፣ ወዘርኡ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ሞኦ ፡ ለዲያብሎስ ፡ መልአከ፣ ሞት፣ ወሠራዊቱ ፣ ወቀተሎ ፣ህየንተ ፡ ቤዛሁ ፡ ቀተሎ ፡በህ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ በዕለተ ፡ዐርብ፣ ወአውረዶ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ታሕቲት ፡ ወሞቅሖ ፡ በህየ ፡ ወአድኅኖ ፣ ለአዳም ፣ ወለኩሉ ፡ዘርኡ ፡እመዋቅሒሁ፤ ፡ህየንተ ፡ ቤዛ ፡ሞት ፡ዘእንበለ ፡ግፍዕ ፡በከመ ፡ ከሠትኩ ፡ለከ ፡በመጽሐፈ ፡ትርጓሜ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ወስቅለቱ ። ተፈጸመ" ፡ ቃለ ፣ ነቢያት ፣ ዘይቤሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘያጠፍእ፤! ፡ ጸላእቶሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፣ አዳም ፡ ወያድኅኖሙ ፣እምኔሆሙ" ፡ ‹ወየሓይዉ ፡ ሙታኒሆሙ" ።
mixed with gall (Mt 27:34), in place of the bitter death because he did not die by compulsion with the drying up of the blood (caused) by fear, but he died by his own will (Jn 10:17-18) without his blood drying up. He tasted the bitter vinegar at the ninth hour in place of the bitterness of the death that Adam and his descendants tasted, and immediately he defeated the devil, the angel of death, and his army and killed him. He killed him in ransom for him (i.e., for Adam), at the ninth hour on the day of Friday and made him descend into the abyss of the underworld. There he chained him, and saved Adam and all his descendants from his chains, in ransom for death, without injustice, as I declared to you in the book of the explanation of his incarnation and of his crucifixion. The word of the prophets has been fulfilled who said that he would destroy the enemies of the children of Adam and would save them from them and would give life to their dead (Is 59:20; Jer 23:6).

### Motivation for the Friday fast
አጥፍኦ ፡ ለዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠሥራዊቱ ፡ በቃለ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ዘይቤ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ሞቱ፣ ከመ ፡ ከሠትኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ወአድኅኖ ፡ ለአዳም ፣ ወለዘርኡ" ፡ እምኔሆሙ ። ወአሕየዎሙ ፡ እሞተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ወአዕረጎሙ ፣ ኀበ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘለ ዓለም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነተ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ እስከ' ፡ ያወር"'ሶሙ ፣ ሥርዓተ ፡ ዲያብ ሎስ ፡ ጸላኢሆሙ ፡ ዘወድቀ ፣ እምኔሃ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፣ እለ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ በዘተሰቅለ ፡ ፅድ'' ፡ ወአንስት ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወሕዝባዊያን ፡ ከመ ፣ ይጹሙ ፣ በዕለተ ፡ ዐርብ ፡ ወትረ ። ወይሕዝኑ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፣፡ ለእግዚ እነ ፡ወያሕምሙ” ፣ ሥጋሆሙ ፣ በጾም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሳተፍዎ" ፡ ኅዳጠ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡
20 He destroyed the devil and his army with the word of his mouth (Is 11:4) which he pronounced at the moment of his death, as I declared to you in this book and saved Adam and his descendants from them. He brought them to life from the death of Sheol and made them ascend to eternal life in the Paradise of delight until he would make them inherit the order of the devil their enemy from which he had fallen. For this reason, it is incumbent upon all those who believe in Him who was crucified, men and women, priests and laymen, that they fast on the day of Friday always, and that they mourn together with the disciples of our Lord. Let them make their body suffer with fasting to participate a little in our Lord in the condemnation


[p. 298] {#po.57.p298}
እምኩነኔ ፡ ሕማም ፡ ዘተወክፈ ፣ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ በሥጋሁ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ዐርብ ፡ ወው እቱ ፣ ይኖልቀ-”! ፤፣ ሎሙ ፡ዘንተ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሕማሙ ። እስመ"ጎ= ፣ ይእቲ ፡ ክሂሎ ቶሙ ፡እስመ ፣ሥጋ ፡የሐምም ፣እምረኀብ ፡ወጽምእ ።
to the suffering that he received for them in his body on the day of Friday. This will be computed for them as his passion, this is in fact what they can do, because the body suffers from hunger and thirst.

### Apostolic excommunication for those who do not fast on Wednesday and Friday
ወበእንተዝ ፡ አውገዙ ፡ ሐዋርያት?” ፡ ለዘኢይጸውም ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ረቡዕ ፡ ወዕለተ ፡ ዐርብ ፣ እስከ ፡ ፣ ሰዓት ፡ እስመ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፣ ዕድ” ፣ ወአንስት” ፣ ይጹሙ ፡ቦሙ ፣እስከ ፡8 ፡ሰዓት ፡ወይትመሰሉ ፡ከመ ፡አርድእት ፡ በሐዘኖሙ ፡ ወይሳተፉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በሕማማተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ ሱቱፋነ ፣ ምስሌሁ ፣ በፍሥሐሁ ፡ ወበጸጋሁ ፣ ወሱቲቴፋነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ በፍሥሐሆሙ ፣፡ወክብሮሙ ። ወዘበልዐ ፡ውስቴቶሙ”" ፡ እምቅድመ ፡8 ፡ሰዓት፣፣ ጥቀ ፡ አኅሰሮ ፡ ለዘተሰቅለ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ወአኅሰረ ፡ ሕማማቲሁ ፡ ዐበይተ ፡ ዘተወክፈ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ። ወይሳተፍ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጸላኢሁ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወዲያብሎስ ፡ ወሠራዊቱ ፡ በፍሥሐሆሙ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ክፍሉ ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ። ወይቤሉ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ለእመ ፣ኮነ ፡ ካህነ?” ፡ ይሰዐር ፡ እምክህነቱ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሕዝባዊ ፡ ይሰደድ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ክርስቲያን ።
21 For this reason the apostles excommunicated those who do not fast on the day of Wednesday and the day of Friday until the ninth hour. It is in fact incumbent that all men and women fast on them until the ninth hour and resemble the disciples in their mourning and participate with our Lord in the sufferings of his body so that they may be participants with him in his joy and in his grace, and participate together with his disciples in their joy and in their honor. Whoever eats on them before the ninth hour, will have greatly despised Him who was crucified for him and will have despised his great sufferings that he received for him and will be an accomplice of his enemies, the Jews, the devil and his army, and his lot will be with them. The apostles have established that if he is a priest he must be removed from his priesthood and if he is a layman he must be expelled from the church.
ወእም ፡፪ ፣ሰዓት ፡ይብላዕ ፡እስመ ፣ባቲ ፡ጠፍአ ፣ዲያብሎስ ፡ወሠራዊቱ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወዘርኡ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ምክንያተ ፡ ጾም ፡ በረቡዕ ፡ ወዐርብ ። ወእምድኅረዝ!” ፡ ከሠተ! ፣ ለነ፡ ከመ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ኢሞተ?" ፡ በግፍዕ ፣ አላ፣ በፈቃዱ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ረገዝዎ ፡ወውኅዘ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወደም ፡ እምገቦሁ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ሞቱ፣ ወዓዲ ፡ አውረድዎ ፡ እምዕፅ ፡ ወቀበርዎ ፡ በምሴት ፡ በዕለተ ፡ዐርብ ። ወበዕለተ ፡ እሑድጎ። ፣ ተንሥአ ፡ እምውታን ፡ ወወሀበነ ፣ ፍሥሐ ፡ ትንሣኤሁ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ፣"ይደሉ ፡ላዕለ ፡ኩልነ”?›፤ ፣ ከመ ፡ንጹም ፡ወንሕዝን ፡ምስሌሆሙ ፡በ፪ ፡መዓልት ፣ ወንትፈሣሕ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወናብዕል ፡ በዕለተ ፡ እሑድ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ወኩሉ””፣ ሱባዔ ፣ ከመ ፣ ንኩን ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፣ በስብሐቲሆሙ ፡ ወክብሮሙ ፡ ዘለዓለም ።
22 Let (the one who fasts) eat at the ninth hour, since in it the devil perished with his army while Adam and his lineage rejoiced. This is the reason for the fast of Wednesday and Friday. After that he manifested to us that he did not die by compulsion but by his own will (Jn 10:17-18), when they pierced him and water and blood flowed from his side (Jn 19:34), after his death, then they made him descend from the wood and buried him on the evening of the day of Friday. On the day of Sunday he rose from the dead and gave us the joy of his resurrection. For this reason it is incumbent upon all of us that we fast and mourn together with them on the two days; and let us rejoice together with them and celebrate the day of Sunday, always and every week, so that we may be together with them in their glory and in their eternal honor. Let this be as much as


[p. 300] {#po.57.p300}
ዝንቱ ፣፡ ይኩን ፡ ለኩልነ ፡ ንንሣእ ፡ በጸጋሁ ፣ ወበምሕረቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ለዓለመ ፡ዓለም ፡አሜን ።
all of us will receive by the grace and by the mercy of our Lord Jesus Christ, for ages of ages amen.

### HOMILY VIII
ድርሳን ፡ሳምን ።
Proem


[p. 302] {#po.57.p302}
ድርሳን ፣ሳምን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ከሚተ ፡ጾም ፡-ምንት ፡ውእቱ ፡ ወእፎ ፡ ይደሉ፣ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ። ኦእጉጥ ፡ ፍቁር ፣ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያብርሀ! ፡ አፅይንተ ፡ ልበከ፡፣ በብርሃነ ፡ መንፈስነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ናዛዚ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትለቡ ፣ ምሥጢራተ ፡ መለኮቱ ። ቀዳሚ ፣ ጸሐፍኩ ፡ ለከ ፣ ክብረ ፡ ስብሐት ፣ ወቅዳሴ ፡ ወጊዜያተ ፡ ሥሩዓት ፣ ወከማሁ ፡ጸሐፍኩ ፡፣ለከ ፡ክብረ ፡ጾም ፣ክልኤ" ፡መዓልት ፡ረቡዕ ፡ወዐርብ ፡ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ዕድ ፡ ወአንስት ፡ አግዓዚያን' ፣ ወአግብርት ።
1 Eighth homily, declaration on what fasting is and how it must be exercised. O beloved brother, may God illuminate the eyes of your heart with the light of the Holy Spirit the consoler so that you may understand the mysteries of his divinity. At the beginning I wrote to you about the honor, the glory, the sanctification, the established times and similarly I wrote to you, about the honor of the fasting of the two days, Wednesday and Friday, and that it is mandatory for all the faithful men and women, free and slaves.

### Fasting is abstinence from sexual relations and not only from food
ወእፈቅድ'' ፡ አጠይቀከ'፤ ፡ከመ ፡ ታእምር" ፡እስመ ፡ጾም ፡አልቦ" ፡ውእቱ፣ "ከሊአ ፡ እመብልዕ" ፡ ወመስቴ ፣ ባሕቲቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ መብልዕ ፡ ወመስቴ ። አኮ፣
2 I desire to assure you that you know that fasting is not merely an interdiction from food and drink because food and drink is not an evil

and does not contain sin. In fact everything that our Lord did when he appeared on earth in the flesh did not contain sin. He performed all human work except sin (Heb 4:15): he ate and drank, therefore he indicated to us that food and drink do not contain sin. It is for this reason that in his holy Gospel he says: “Everything that enters the mouth does not defile man” (Mt 15:17-20//). Fasting is not an interdiction from eating and drinking in which there is no sin, instead fasting is an interdiction only from the lust of coitus which for us happened from sin. Adam and Eve created without sexual lust. | Relationships between cold and heat in the human body 3 This is that when God created our father Adam and our mother Eve and left them in Paradise in them there was no lust of coitus, because God created their flesh equally (balanced) in the four natural elements. Human flesh was created from the four natural elements: earth and water, the

wind and fire; the dry and the moist, the cold and the hot. Each of them contrasts the other: the hot contrasts the cold, the dry contrasts the moist. If a natural element adds itself to another it defeats it, devours it, and destroys it. When Adam and Eve were created there was no natural element in them that added itself to another, but God created four natural elements with equal measures in (their) potential. The heat with equal measure in power, with the cold; the dry, equal with the moist in power. None could overcome the other because the potential in them was equal: each of them guarded itself. For this reason in Adam and Eve there was no lust of coitus, because coitus does not occur without the force of heat which is vertigo. If the heat was of equal measure with the cold, which with bile is vertigo, the dry was of equal measure with the blood, for this reason in Adam and Eve there was no lust of coitus: in the same way as children until their


[p. 308] {#po.57.p308}
እስከ ፡ልህቀቶሙ ፡ኢይሄሉ፤ ፡ውስቴቶሙ ፡ፍትወተ ፡አውስቦ ፡በእንተ ፡ድካመ ፡ የ፲ላህብ ፡ ወርጡብ፡ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተአዘዙ ፣ አዳም ፡ ወሔዋ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ ወሰምዑ ፡፣ እምፍትወቱ ፣ ወገደፈቶሙ ፣ ኀይለ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወረ ሰየ ፡ ላህብ ፡ ይማኦ ፣ ለቂሪር ፡ ወይቡስ ፡ ለርጡብ! ፡ ኮነ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፣ ፍት ወተ ፡ አውስቦ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሰዓት ። ወፍትወተ ፡ አውስቦሰ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፣ እምጊዜ ፡ ተዓድዎት ፣ ወበእንተዝ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጹሙ፡፥ ፡ በእንቲአሃ ፡ በጊዜ ፡እምጊዜያት ።
adult age in whom there is no lust of coitus, because of the great weakness of the heat and humidity inside them. When Adam and Eve obeyed Satan and gave heed to his lust, the strength of God abandoned them, it made it so that the heat overcame the cold, the dry the moist: in that hour the lust of coitus occurred in them. The lust of coitus occurred in them from the moment of the transgression (onward) therefore (God) ordered them to fast periodically because of it.

### The rules of sexual impurity in the Torah. Jesus freed us from them
ወበእንተዝ ፣ ይቤ ፡ በመጽሐፈ' ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ዘፀአት ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሶበ ፡ፈቀደ ፡ከመ ፡ይረድ ፣መልዕልተ ፡ደብር" ፡ከመ ፣ይትናገሮሙ ፡ለደቂቀ፣ እስራኤል ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ለሙሴ ።" አዝዞሙ ፡ከመ ፡፣ ይትፈለጡ ፡እምአንስቲሆሙ፤ ፣ ፻ ፡ መዓልተ" ፡ ወይንጽሑ፤' ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፣ እወረድ ፣፡ ወእትናገረከ” ፡ በቅድ ሜሆሙ ። ፣ወይቤ ፡ ካዕበ" ፡ በኦሪት ፡ በዳግም" ፡ ሕግ ፡ እስመ ፣ ብእሲ ፡ ለእመ፣ ሰከበ ፣ ምስለ ፡፣ ብእሲቱ ፡ ይደልዎ! ፡ ይትሐፀብ ፡ በማይ ፡ ወይከውን ፣ ርኩሰ” ፣ እስከ ፡ ምሴት ፡ ይእቲ" ፡ ዕለት ። ወለእመ ፡ ኢተሐፅበ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ርኩሰ ፡ እስከ፣ ፳፪ ፡ፅለት ። ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፣ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ከመ ፡ያጠይቀነ ፡እስመ ፡ዛቲ፣ ፍትወት ፡ኮነት ፡ውስቴትነ ፣እምጊዜ ፡-ተዓድዎት ፡ወይእቲ ፡ ታረኩሰነ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ወፅአት ፡እምኔነ ። ወይደሉ ፡ላዕሌነ ፡ንጹም ፡እምኔሃ ፡በከመ ፡መጠነ ፡ክሂሎትነ፣ እስመ ፣ ይእቲ ፡ ውስቴትነ ፡ ጠባይዓዊት ። ወሶበ ፡ መጽአ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወፈትሐነ ፡ እማእሰረ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወአሰረነ ፡ በአርዑቱ” ፡ ጥዑም ፣ ወቀሊል ፡ኢአዘዘነ ፡ከመ ፡ንትፈለጥ ፡ እምአንስቲያነ ፡፻ ፡ መዓልተ ፡እምቅድመ ፣ ንስማፅ"፤ ፡ ቃሎ ፣፡ በከመ ፣ ገብረ ፡ በደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ።
4 For this reason in the book of the Torah of Exodus it says that when God wanted to descend on the summit of the mountain to speak to the children of Israel, he said to Moses: “Order them to separate themselves from their wives for three days and purify themselves, after which I will descend and I will speak to you before them” (Ex 19:15). It also says in the Torah of the second law: “If a man lies with his wife he must wash with water and will be impure until the evening of that day. If he does not wash he will be impure for seven days” (Lv 15:18, 24). God pronounced this word to confirm to us that this lust existed inside us from the time of the transgression and that it makes us impure if it comes out from our interior. We are obligated to fast from it in the measure of our capacity, because it is inside us; it is natural. When our Lord Jesus Christ came and loosened us from the chain of the law of the Torah and bound us to his sweet and light yoke (Mt 11:30), he did not command us to separate ourselves from our wives for three days, before we listen to his word, as he did with the children of Israel. He did not make us impure because of our lying with our wife with whom he permitted us to sleep.

### The new rules
ወኢረሰየነ ፡ ርኩሳነ' ፡ በእንተ ፣ ምስካቢነ ፣ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲትነ ፣ ዘፈትሐነ ፡ ከመ ፣ ንሰክብ ፡ ምስሌሃ ። ወኢአዘዘነ ፡ ንትሓጸብ ፡ በማይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስክበትነ፡፣ ምስሌሃ ፡ ወኢበእንተ ፡ ፍትወት! ፡ ወኢከልአነ ፡ እምጸሎት ፡ ወኢበዊአ ፡ ‹ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን' ፡ በእንተ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡በከመ ፡ገብረ ፡በደቂቀ ፣እስራኤል ፡ አላ ፡አቅለለ፣ እምላዕሌነ ፡ አርዑቶ ፡ ወጠዐምነ' ፡ ሕጎ ፡ በከመ ፡ ንክል ፣ ንጹሮ ። ወይቤ ፡ ‹(ከመ፣ ይእቲ ፡ አልባቲ ፡ ርኩስና ዖ ፣አላ ፡ መብልዐ ። ወዘሰ ፡ ይበልዕ ፡ ኢይትከላእ ፡ የእምጸሎት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘበልዐ ፣ ኢይትከላእ'' ፡ እምበዊአ፤! ፡ ቤተ" ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ "ወኢእምቀዊም ፡በጊዜ ፡ቅዳሴ" ፡ አላ ፣ ይትከላእ ፡እምነሚአ ፡ቀርባን ፡ባሕቲቱ ። ከምዝ ፡ አዘዘነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ንክል" ፡ ጸዊረ ፣ ሕግ ፡ ወኢናመክኒ"፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ምክንያተ ፡ ወኢንትከላእ ፡ እምጸሎት ፡ ወኢበጺሕ' ፡ ወእምቀዊም'' ፡
5 He did not command us to wash with water because of our sleeping with her, nor because of the lust. He did not interdict us from prayer, nor from entering the church because of this, as he did with the children of Israel, but he has lightened his yoke from upon us and we have tasted his law (Mt 11:30) so that we can bear it. He said that it does not contain impurity, but eating (does). And he who eats is not interdicted from prayer for having eaten, he is not interdicted from entering the church and from being present at the time of sanctification but he is interdicted only from receiving communion. Thus God commanded us so that we can take charge of the law and not make excuses against it (i.e., the law) and not deny ourselves to prayer, nor to going and being present at the time of sanctification with various excuses.


[p. 310] {#po.57.p310}
በጊዜ ፡ቅዳሴ ፡በአመክንዮ ፡ ምክንያት ። ነጽር ፡ኦፍቁር ፡ምንተ" ፡የዐቢ ፡ጽያሔሁ” ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ እምዕጽበቱ” ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እልክቱ"! ፡ወበከመ ፡ይቤ ፡፣ሐዋርያ ፡ጳውሎስ” ፡እስመ ፡ውእቱ ፡አዕፀበ ፡ሳዕለ ፡ እሙንቴ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ አሚኖቶሙ ፡ ወጹሐ” ፡ ላዕሌነ” ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሣህሉ ፡ ለነ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ አዘዘነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንጹም ፡ እምፍትወት” ፣ ጊዜያተ ፡ ሥሩዓተ” ፣ ከመ ፡ ኢንኩን ፡ ንብላዕ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጾም ፡ ወንትመሰል ፡ ከመ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ እለ ፡አልቦሙ ፣ልበ ። ወሶበ ፡ሠርዐ ፣ለነ ፡ጾመ” ፡እምዛቲ ፡ፍትወት ፡አእመረ፣ ከመ ፡ ጻላኢ ፡ ይፀብአነ” ፡ ባቲ ፡ ወያጽህቀነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ናማስን ፡ ጾመነ” ፡ ሶበ ፡ ንገ ብራ ። ሠርዐ ፡ ለነ ፡ በምሕረቱ” ፣ ሥርዓተ” ፣ በክሂሎቱ ፣ ለእመ ፣፡ ጾምነ ፡ ንመ ውኦ ፡ ለጸላኢ ።
6 Observe, O beloved, how greatly he has smoothed the hardness toward us, compared to those (of the Jews). As the apostle Paul says: “Since He made it hard toward them because of their little faith, while for us he smoothed (the path) for his mercy toward us.” Therefore he ordered us to fast from lust at established times so that we do not eat always, without fasting and resemble animals that do not possess reason. When he ordered us to fast from this lust, he knew that the enemy fights us with it and puts pressure on us so that we destroy our fasting if we commit it (i.e., the lust). In his mercy he arranged for us an ordinance in his power that if we fast, we defeat the enemy.
ወበእንተዝ ፡ አዘዘነ ፣ በመዓልታት ፣ ዘንጸውም ፡ በውስቴታ፤ ፣ እምዛቲ ፣ ፍትወት ፡ ከመ ፣ ንጹም ፣ እምበሊርዐ ፣ ሥጋ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ፍትወተ"። ወዘ ይወፅእ ፡እምኩሉ ፣እንስሳ ፡ይከውን ፡ውስቴቱ ፡ፍትወት ፡ዘትትሀወክ ። እስመ ፡ ዘይበልዕ ፡ ሥጋ ፣ እንስሳ ፡ ቦቱ" ፡ ፍትወተ ፡ ዘትትሀወክ' ። ወፍትወቱ ፡ ውስተ ፣፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ እም ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መብልዕ ፡ ዘበልዐ ። ወለእመ ፣ ተሀውከት ፡ ፍትወት ፣፡ ውስተ ፡ሥጋ ፡ምስለ ፡ተቃርኖተ ፡ሰይጣን ፡ተሞአ ፡ሰብእ ፡እምጾም ፡ወኢይክል ፡ ይትቃረኖ ፡ የለሰይጣን ፣ ወኢይትቃረኖ ፡ ለመብልዕ ፡ ወኢይትቃረኖ ዖ ለጠባይዕ ፡ ''ዘውስቴቱ፤!! ። ወለእመ ፣ ተከልአ ፡ እመብልዕ ፡ ዘውስቴታ ፡ ፍትወት ፣ ወተከ ልአ ፡ እመብልዕ ፡ ወመስቴ ፡ በመዓልት ፡ ደክመ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፣ ወኢይክል ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ይትቃረኖ ፡በእንተ ፡ድካመ" ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ወአልቦቱ ፡ሁከተ!' ፡ዘእንበለ ፡ ሁከተ" ፡ ጠባይዓት" ። ወሁከተ ፡ ጠባይዓትሰ ፡ ኢይትቃረኖ' ፡ ለፍርሃተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወኢይተሰኖአው ፡ ምስለ ፡ ስይጣን ፡ እስመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ የንጽሕት ፡ በርእሳ"'' ፡ አላ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ትመውእ ፣ ወትትቃረን" ፡ ለፍርሀ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ለእመ ፡ ተደመረ ፣ ምስሌሃ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ሁከት ፡ እመብልዕ" ፡ እንስሳዊት ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ አዘዙነ ፡ በመ ዋዕለ ፡አጽዋማት ፡ከመ ፡-ኢንብላዕ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ ዘውስቴቱ”' ፡ ፍትወተ ፣ አው ስቦ ። ወኩሎ” ፡ ዘመደ ፡ እንስሳ ፣ ውስቴቶሙ ፣ ፍትወት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ንህብ ፣ ባሕቲቱ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ አዘዙነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢንብላዕ ፡ ዘይወፅእ ፡ እምእንሰሳ ፣ ወአዘ ዙነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንብላዕ ፡ መዓረ ፡ዘንህብ” ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይወፅእ”' ፡እምእንሰሳ ፡ አላ፣
Abstention from the consumption of meat and its derivatives. All animals, except the bee, are carriers of sexual lust 7 Therefore he commanded us the days in which we fast from this lust, so that we abstain from eating animal meat in which there is lust and that which comes out from every animal in which there is lust that moves. In fact he who eats animal meat that contains the lust that moves, and the lust is in his body by way of that food that he has eaten. If with the opposition of Satan, in the body the lust is activated, man is defeated from (practicing) the fasting and it will not be possible for him to contrast Satan. He will not contrast the food and he will not contrast the nature that is inside him. But if he abstains from the food inside which there is lust; if he abstains from eating and drinking by day, his body will tire and Satan will not be able to contrast him because of the fatigue of his body and there will be no movement for him, except the movement of the natural elements. And the movement of the natural elements does not oppose the fear of God and does not ally with Satan, since it is in itself pure but it defeats and opposes the fear of God if to it is added another motion (coming) from animal food. For this reason they ordered us that in the days of the fasts we should not eat animal meat that contains the lust of coitus. All animal kinds contain lust, except the bee alone therefore they ordered us that we should not eat what comes out from animals and they ordered us that we should eat the honey of the bee: it comes out from animals but it is an animal that does not contain lust


[p. 312] {#po.57.p312}
ውእቱ ፡እንስሳ ። ዘአልቦቱ ፡ የፍትወተ ። ወተጠየቅነ”፡ ከመ ፣ ኢንትከላእ ፡ እመብልዕ ፣ ዘይወዕዕ ፡ እምእንስሳ ፡ ዘአልቦቱ ፡ ፍትወት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍትወት ፡ ባሕቲታ ። እስመ ፡ ጾምነ፣ በእንቲአሃ ፡ወአኮ ፡በእንተ ፣ካልእት ፡እስመ ፡ካልእት ፡ኮነት ፡ለነ ፣እምቅድመ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ኮነት ፣ ለነ -፡ እምኃጢአት ፡ ወንሕነሰ ፡ በእንቲአሃ ፣ ንጸውም ፣
8 We are therefore informed that we are not interdicted from food that derives from the animal that does not contain lust, if not exclusively because of the lust. In fact our fasting is because of it (i.e., the lust) and not for anything else, because the rest existed for us before the sin, while it

(i.e., the lust) happened for us by way of sin, and we fast because of it and not for anything else. God knew that if a man stayed with his wife in a house, he could not deny himself to his wife, when the motion of lust defeated him because of animal foods. In fact the heat defeats him because of animal food. For this reason they commanded us that we should abstain from animal food so that we can defeat the natural lust with the fear of God because it submits itself to the fear of God if the animal motion is not added to it. The danger of meat consumption even in the absence of the wife. Sexual intercourse with one's wife is not impure 9 If in the days of the fast, the wife is not with the man and he eats an animal food, the enemy will be capable of devouring him with lust in sleep. If his flesh is not strengthened with animal food, the enemy will not have the weapon with which to fight him and cannot contrast him against lust in sleep if not from time to time: (thus) he will be one who fasts a pure fast. After baptism there is no impure lust, there is no sexual intercourse with one's legitimate wife that is impure, but it is (impure) only the eating. He who eats does not become impure and sin will not be imputed to him if he eats, except on the day of the fast. He sins as Adam sinned when he ate while it had been categorically ordered to him that he should not eat (Gn 2:17). For this reason the apostle Paul ordered the man and the woman that they should not deny themselves to each other if not by their mutual consent (1Cor 7:5) on the nights of the Eucharistic celebration. (Paul) says that they come for prayer (1Cor 7:5), that is, to receive communion. He does not prevent them from praying and from being present at the Eucharistic celebration but only from receiving communion.

### One single night of abstinence from matrimonial duty

10 He does not force them to deny themselves mutually for three days before receiving communion; not even as God commanded the children of Israel (Ex 19:15), but they will abstain from each other that night after which the Eucharist will be celebrated. They will abstain from each other that night and receive communion the following day. At the end of that day in which they received the Eucharist and the night has arrived which is the beginning of the other day, let them join each other, and they will not have committed sin. In fact, the beginning of every day is the evening of the other day; the beginning of the day of Saturday is the evening of the day of Friday, as God commanded in the Torah. And the beginning of the day of Sunday is from the evening of Saturday and so it is of every day. If on a day, a man desires to receive the Eucharist, he must abstain from his wife from the evening of the day before, and let him abstain until the night of the day in which he has communicated. When the night begins, let him do what he desires because that day is another compared to the day in which he received communion. This is the interdiction that the apostle Paul commanded in his letter to the Corinthians. He commanded that husband and wife agree on this and obey each other and warns them so that they guard each other in the fear of God (1Cor 7:5). Conjugal coitus does not invalidate the possibility of praying. On participation in the Mass

11 If he lies with his wife in one of the nights, he is not interdicted from prayer because of this, but he must rise and pray over his bed, the prayer of midnight, because he is pure and his bed is pure, as the apostle Paul says in his letter to the Hebrews (Heb 13:4). When it is dawn he is not interdicted from going to church, just as it is incumbent every day on all the faithful and he does not need to wash just as the Torah says,

but let him wash only the place that has become dirty. Not however as the impure who washes from impurity, but let him wash it from the dirt and enter the church pure, without impurity. He will not have guilt, he will pray with a pure heart and be present at all the time of sanctification. Let him not delay his arrival at the sanctification because the faithful must be present daily at the sanctification, so that from being present at the sanctification they receive the great grace that I exposed to you in the book of the explanation of the lamb but he will not receive communion because he has eaten. If that day has passed in which he lay with his wife and another day has begun in which he has not lain with her, let him receive communion without washing with water because his bed with his wife is legitimate and does not contain impurity.

### Characteristics of impurity
ወለእመ ፡ ኀለፈት”"፤ ፣ ይእቲ ፡ ዕለት" ፡ ዘሰከበ ፡ ምስለ ፣ ብእሲቱ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ በካልእ! ፡ ዕለት ፡ ዘኢሰከበ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ይንሣእ" ፣ ተሩርባነ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይትሐፀብ ፡ በማይ ፡ እስመ ፣ ምስካቢሁ" ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፣ በፍትሕ ፡ አልቦቱ ፡ ርኩሰ” ። እመሰ ፡ ረኩሰ ፡ አኮ ፡ በማይ ፣ ዘያነጽሕ፤ ፡ ርኩስና ፡ አላ ፡ ያነጽሕ ፡ ርስሐተ ፡ ሥጋ፡፡ ፤ ባሕቲቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ምስካቢሁ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፣ በፍትሕ” ፡ ኢይረ ኩስ”። አላ፣ ዝሙት ፡ ውእቱ” ፡ ዘያረኩስ፡ ፤ ወኢይነጽሕ” ፡ እምዝሙት ፣ ወለእመ ፡ተሐፅበ ፡በኩሉ ፡ማያተ” ፣አብሕርት ፡ወአፍላጋት ፡ አላ ፡ ይነጽሕ” ፣ ለእመ ፡ ዓስሐ ፡ ወኢተመይጠ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዝሙት" ። ወዘሰ ፡ ይወፅእ ፣ እምኔሁ ፣ ፍትወት ፡ በንዋም” ፣ በአምሳለ ፣ ብእሲ ፣ ዘሰከበ” ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ በከመ ፣ ይቤ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በሕጉ ፡ መንፈሳዊ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ሠርዐ ፣ ኩነኔ ፡ ላዕለ” ፡ ዘይፈቱ ፡ በንዋም” ፣ በከመ ፡ ሠርዐ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዘሰከበ ፣ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡በፍትሕ ፥ ከማሁ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ላዕለ ፡ ቭይፈቱ ፡ በንዋም ፡ በከመ ፣ ይደሉ ፡ላዕለ ። ዘሰከበ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ በፍትሕ ።
12 Even if he becomes impure it is not with water that he purifies the impurity but he cleans only the dirt of the flesh because his legitimate bed with his wife does not become impure. It is instead adultery that makes one impure and he will not be purified from adultery even if he washed with all the waters of the seas and rivers but he is purified if he converts and does not return to adultery. As for him from whom pleasure flows in sleep in the likeness of the man who lies with his wife, it is as the book of God says in his holy spiritual law (Lv 15). He has established a judgment on him who enjoys in sleep, just as he legislated on him who has sexual relations with his legitimate wife, so it is in force for him who enjoys in sleep in the same way as him who has sexual relations with his legitimate wife.

### Complaint about the ignorance of authentic fasting
ወዘንተ ፡ኩሎ ፡ ጸሐፍኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦፍቁር ፡ ከመ ፡ ታእምር! ፡ እስመ ፡ ጾም ፡ አኮ ፣ ጾም፡ ፣ እመብልዕ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ አላ ፣ ንሕነሰ ፡፣ ንጹም ፡ እመብልዕ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይድከም፤ ፡ ሥኃነ ፡ እምፍትወተ ፡ አውሰቦ ፡ ዘሠርዑ ፣ ጾመ ፡ በእንቲአሃ ፡ ወአኮ በእንተ ፡ካልእት ። ወብዙኀን ፡ሰብእ ፡በእንተ ፣ኀዳጥ ፡አእምሮቶሙ ፣ይጸውሙ ፡ እመብልዕ ፡ ወኢይጸውሙ ፡ እምፍትወት ፣ ዘበእንቲአሃ ፡ ጾሙ ፡ እመብልዕ ፡ ወአኮ ፣ ይገብሩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በፈቃድ ፡ ወኢተዓድወት" ፣ አላ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ኅዳጥ፣፡ አእምሮቶሙ ። እስመ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ይፈቅድ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወኢ ይፈቅድ ፡ይብላዕ ፡ በጾሙ፤ ፣ በመባልዕት ፡ አው ፡ይብላዕ ፆ ሥጋ ፡አው ፡ካልአ ፡ በእንተ ፡ዘአውገዙ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍትወት ፣ እንስሳዊት ። ፣ወይፈቅድ ፣ ከመ ፣ ይምትሩ" ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወኢይፈቅድ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ ፉጐርባነ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ በልዐ፣ ወውእቱሰ" ፡ ይበልዕ ፡በፍትወት ፡ዘበእንቲአሃ' ፡ አውገዙ ፡ እመብልዕ ፡ ወመስቴ ፣ በጾም ። ወአኮ ፡ ይግበሩ" ፣ ዘንተ ፡ በአእምሮ ፡ አላ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አእምሮ ። እስመ ፡ ለእመ ፡ አእመሩ ፡ከመ ፣እሙንቴ" ፡ አውገዙ ፡ ለመብልፅዕ' ፣ ወመስቴ ፡በእንተ፣ ፍትወት ፡ ‹እምፈቀዱ ፡ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶሙ፤' ፡፣ ወኢይግበርዋ" ፣ በከመ ፡ ገብሩ ፣ ዘንተ ፡በመብልፅ”።
13 I have written all this to you, O beloved, so that you know that fasting is not a fast only from food, rather, we fast from food so that in our body the pleasure of coitus weakens; it is for this that they have arranged fasting and not for anything else. Many people because of their scarce knowledge, fast from food but do not fast from the lust because of which they fasted from food. They do not behave in this way voluntarily, nor to transgress, but because of their scarce knowledge. Someone among them is willing to have his head cut off rather than break his fast with foods, or that he eats meat or something else, because of the anathema that they have issued against them because of animal lust. He is willing to have his head cut off and does not want to receive communion after having eaten, and yet he feeds on the lust toward which they issued the anathema from food and drink in the fast. They do not behave in this way with knowledge but without knowledge, because if they had known that they separated (themselves) from food and drink for the lust, they would have preferred to cut off their head rather than do this, as they did this in food. Threats against unconscious transgressors. Against those who confuse fasting with sleep
እሎንቱ” ፣፣ ከመዝ ፣ ነዳያን ፣ ይጠፍኡ ፡ በእበዶሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ፣ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡እስመ ፡እለ፡' ፡ዘእንበለ ፡ሕግ ፡አበሱ ፡ዘእንበለ ፡ሕግ፣ ይጠፍኡ”= ። ወአኮ ፡ዘንተ ፡ዘእብለከ፡ ፡በእንተ ፣፡ሕዝባዊያን ፡ባሕቲቶሙ ፡፣አላ”፣ ብዙኀን ፡ ካህናት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ አእምሮቶሙ ፡ በአንብቦ”፤ ፡ መጻሕፍተ” ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኮኑ” ፡ ዘኢያእምሩጆ ፣ ቃለ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኢያእመሩ 7” ሕገ ፡ወኢቀኖና”› ። ወብዙኀን ፡እምኔሆሙ ፡ይቴልዩ ፡ከመ ፣ጾም ፡አኮ ፡ውእቱ፣ ክልአት"! ፡ እመብልዕ ፣፡ ወመስቴ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ወይትኀበሉ” ፣ ይንሥኡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፣
14 Those such miserable people will perish in their folly as the apostle says about them: “For those who sinned without the law, without the law will perish” (Rm 2:12). What I am telling you does not concern only the laymen but (also) many priests, who because of their scarce knowledge in the reading of the books of God, became ignorant of the word of God and ignorant of the law and the canon. Many of them think that fasting is not only abstinence from food and drink and they dare to receive the body of our


[p. 322] {#po.57.p322}
ለእግዚእነ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ በልዑ፤ ፣ መብልዐ ፡ ፍትወት ፡ ዘዘከርኩ ፡ ለከ ። ወካዕበ ፣ ይትኀበሉ ፡ ይቀድሱ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ከመዝ ። ወቦ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ካዕበ ፡ይቴልዩ ፡ ከመ ፡ጾም ፡ውእቱ ፡ ንዋም ። ይነብሩ! ፡ወይበልዑ ፡ ወይሰትዩ ፡ እምሴት ፡እስከ ፡ የኀልፍ ፣ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ። ወይቤሉ ፣ ለእመ ፡ ኖምነ" ፣ አሐተ ፡ ሰዓተ ፡ ይበ ቀዮዓነ ። ይቴልዩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጾም ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ንዋም ። ወኢያእመሩ፤' ፡ ነዳያን ፡ ከመ፣ ጾም ፡ አኮ ፡ውእቱ ፡ንዋም ፡አላ ፡፻ ፡ሰዓት ፡ዘሠርዐ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡በሌሊት ፣ ወበመዓልት" ።
Lord after having consumed the food of the lust that I reminded you of. They also permit themselves to sanctify while they are like this. There are among them also those who think that fasting consists of sleep: they sit, eat and drink from the evening until midnight passes and say: “For us it is sufficient if we sleep for an hour.” They think that fasting consists of sleep and the miserable ones have not understood that fasting does not consist of sleep, but three hours that God has established in the night and in the day.

### Times and methods of Eucharistic fasting
ወአንሰ ፡ እዜክረክሙ፤! ፣ ዝየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትከሠት ፡ ለዘኢያአምርዋ"! ፡ ወይ ትዐቀብ ፣ቦሙ ። ወለእመ ፡ፈቀደ ፡ይንሣእ ፡ቀኑርባነ ፡በጽባሕ ፣ ይደልዎ! ፡ከመ፣ ይጹም ፡እምሴት ፡ኢይብሳዕ ፡ ወኢይስተይ ። ወለእመ ፡ፈቀደ ፡ይንሣእ ፡ቱርባነ፣ በጊዜ ፡8 ፡ ሰዓት ፡ አው ፡በሰርክ'' ፣ ይደልዎ ፡ ከመ" ፡ ይትከላእ ፡ እመብልዕፅ ፡ ወመስቴ ፡እምቅድመ ፥፡፻ ፣ሰዓት ፡ዘሌሊት” ። ወፈትሑ ፡ወሠርዑ ፣ዘንተ ፣በጊዜ ፡ ፻ ፡ እምሌሊት"' ፡ በእንተ ፣ ጾም" ፡ ከመ ፣ ይስተዩ” ፡ ማየ ፡ ባሕቲቱ” ። ጣንተ፣ ዘዘከርናሁ”! ፡በእንተ ፡ መብልዕ ፡ ወመስቴ ። ወበእንተ ፡ብእሲትሰ ፣ናሁ ፡ከሠትኩ ፥ ለከ ፣፡ከመ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡አዘዘ ፡ከመ ፡ኢይቅረብ ፡ ኀበ” ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡እምጥንተ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ዘበሳኒታሁ ፡ይፈቅድ ፣ቀ'ርባነ ። በከመ” ፡ ከሠትኩ ፡ለከ ፡ዘንተ ፡ገብረ” ፡ ለነ" ፡ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሣህሉ ፣ ላዕሌነ ። ወለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤልሰ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይትፈለጡ ፡ እምአንስቲያሆሙ ፡፤፲” ፣ መዓልተ” ፡ እምቅድመ፣
15 Here, I remind you (of the methods of fasting) so that it may be manifest to those who are not aware of it and they adhere to it (fasting). If he wants to receive communion in the morning, he must fast from the evening; he must not eat nor drink. If he wants to receive communion at the moment of the ninth hour, or at vespers, he must abstain from food and drink (starting) from three hours before the night. They have granted and legislated this on fasting, that from three in the night they drink only water. This is as much as we have mentioned on food and drink. About the wife instead, I declared to you that God commanded that (the man) does not approach his wife from the beginning of the night after which he desires to communicate. As I explained to you, God has accomplished this for us because of his benignity toward us. As (already) I wrote to you, to the children of Israel he commanded to separate themselves from their wives for three days before presenting themselves before him (Ex 19:15).
ይብጽሑ ፡ቅድሜሁ ፡በከመ ፡ጽሐፍኩ ፡ለከ ። ወከማሁ ፡ የብዙኀን ፡ ሰብእ ።” ኅዳጣነ" ፡ ዘበ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ወምስኪናን ፡ ይትጐጸመጽጹ ፡ አፉሆሙ ፡ በማይ ፣ ወይትኀበሉ ፡ ይንሥኡ ፡ ፉርርባነ ። እሎንቱ ፤ ከመዝ ፡ነዳያን ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ እምኩሉ” ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወረስዑ” ፣ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘይቤሎ ፡ለሙሴ ፡ገብሩ ፡በእንተ ፡ባበግዕ ፡ፍሥሐ” ፡ዘኮነ ፡ምሳሌ” ፡ ብእንተ ፣
16 Similarly, many people of scarce knowledge and miserable, rinse their mouth with water and dare to receive communion. Those like that are the most miserable of all people and have forgotten the word that God said to Moses his servant about the paschal lamb that became a type of the body


[p. 324] {#po.57.p324}
ሥጋሁ፤ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ይቤ ፡ ብልፅዎ ፡በሐሞት ፡ዝብሂል ፡ከመ ፡ ልሳን ፡ለእመ፡፣ የብሰ፡ ፡ እምጽምአ ፡ ማይ ፣ ይከውን ፣ መሪረ ፣ በከመ ፡ ሐሞት ። ከሠትኩ! ፡ ለከ፡፣ ዘንተ ፡ከመ ፡ውእቱ ፡ ኢይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፣ መኑሂ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ በመሪር ፡አፍ ፡እምጾም ፣ወክልአት ፡ እምኅብስት ፡ ወማይ ፡ ይከውን ፣ ልሳን ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ወአፍ ፡ዘኢይበውእ'' ፡ ውስቴቱ ፣ ወኢምንትኒ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ እምቅድመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ኢይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባእ ፡ ውስተ ፣ አፉሁ ፣ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምቅድሜሁ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ሕዝቅኤል ፡ነቢይ ፡በእንተ፣ ኖኅት ፡ዘምሥራቀ፤! ፡ ኢቦአ'፡ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ዘእንበለ ፡እግዚአ ፡ኃያላን ፡ዘውእቱ ፡ ከርሠ ፡ ድንግል! ፡ ማርያም ፡ ዘኮነት ፡ ኅትምተ ፡ ወቦአ ፣ ‹እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውስ
of Christ. He said: “Eat it with bitter herbs” (Ex 12:8): this means that if the tongue dries up due to thirst for water, it becomes as bitter as gall. I have declared this to you, that no one should receive the body of Christ except with a bitter mouth. Through fasting and abstinence from bread and water, it becomes a holy tongue. The mouth into which nothing enters on that day, before receiving the body of Christ, it is not necessary that anything enter into his mouth before it. As the prophet Ezekiel said concerning the eastern gate: “No one shall enter by it, except the Lord of the mighty” (Ez 44:1-2), that is, the womb of the virgin Mary sealed, but only God entered it, (while) her virginity remained sealed.

17 Similarly, before the body of Christ, absolutely nothing must enter the mouth. The tongue shall be bitter from fasting, until it has received the body of Christ, as God commanded that they should eat the body of Christ in bitterness. The tongue must not taste anything of the world before having received communion. In all this, I address those who have little knowledge of the norms of the Christian faith, and who behave according to the dictates of their own minds, outside the book of God. They believe that what they have done is right, while the rest is vain. Many of them have believed that fasting is not abstinence from food and drink, but abstinence from one's wife.

ወስሕቱ ፡ ፕልቄ ፡ ግ ፡ ፅለት፤ ፡ ጾም ፡ ዘጾመ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ በእንቲአነ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ኢይትከልኡ ፡ እምአንስቲያሆሙ ፡ በጾም ፡ እምሰኑይ ፡ እስከ" ፡ ዐርብ ፣ የወይሰክቡ ፡ምስለ ፡ አንስቲያሆሙ፤ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ዘቀዳም ። የኀልዩ ፡ከመ ፡ቀዳም ፣ ወሰንበት ፡ኢኮኑ ፡እምጥልቁ፤ ፡ጓ መዓልት ፡ዘጾም'' ። ወእሙንቱ ፡በዝንቱ፤'! ፣ ግብር ፡ ይኤብሱ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ እምእለ ፣ ዘተከልኡ፤፤ ፡ እምብእሲት ፣ ግሙራ ። እስመ ፡ ባዕለተ ፣ ቀዳም ፡ ትትመሰል!! ፣ በዕለተ ፣ እሑድ ፡ በቡራኬ ፣ ወቅዳሴ ። ወይደሉ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፣ይትጋደል ፡ ወይንሣእ" ፡ ጐርባነ ፡ ቦቱ፣ ኩሎ ፡መዋዕለ ፡በከመ ፡ዕለተ ፡እሑድ ። ወፈድፋደሰ'" ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ጾም ፡ዘጾመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡በእንቲአነ ፡እስመ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጣረካ ፡ወቀደሳ"' ፡ ለፅለተ ፡ ቀዳም ፡ "እምቅድመ ፡ዕለተ'' ፡እሑድ ። ወንሕነሰ” ፡ ንብላዕ ፡ ወንስተይ ፡ቦቱ ፡በዐቢይ” ፡ ጾም ፡ወንትፈሣሕ ፡ቦቱ ፡ወናብዕል ፡ከመ ፡ዕለተ ፡እሑድ ። እስመ ፡ጾም ፡ሐዘን ፣ ወመብልፅ ፡፣ ፍሥሐ ። ወዕለተ ፡ ቀዳም ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ቡራኬ ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፡ ወትረ ። ይደሉ ፡ለኩሎሙ ፡መሃይምናን ፡ዕድ ፡ወአንስት ፡ከመ ፣ይትጋደሉ ፡ወይንሥኡ ፣ ዮርባነ ፡ ወይዕቀቡ ፣ነፍሶሙ ፡ ቦቱ" ፡ በከመ ፡ ዕለተ ፡-እሑድ ። ወዘሰ ፡ ይቴልዩ ፣ ከመ ፣ ዕለተ ፡ ቀዳም ፡ ፣ኢትትጥለቀኑ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዐቢይ?! ፡ ጾም ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙ ንቱ ፡ስሕቱ ፡ ሐሳበ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሱባዔ” ፣፡ የቀዳሚ ፡እምሱባዔ ፡”'ጾም ፡ወበእንተ ፡ ሱባዔ ፡፦ ሕማማት ፡ዘውእቱ ፡ሱባዔ ፡ፍሥሕ ።
Problems with the calculation of the forty days of fasting. Holiness of the Sabbath. 18 They erred in the calculation of the forty days of fasting that our Lord fasted for us, and they abstained from their wives during the fast, from Monday until Friday. They slept with their wives on the night of the Sabbath, thinking that the Sabbath and Sunday do not fall within the number of the forty days of fasting. By this action, they sin more than those who abstain totally from their wives, because the day of the Sabbath is comparable to the day of Sunday in blessing and sanctification (Gn 2:3). It is the duty of man to strive to receive communion every day, in the same manner as on the day of Sunday. And even more so in the great fast (i.e., Lent) that our Lord fasted for us (Mt 4:2), because God blessed and sanctified the day of the Sabbath before the day of Sunday (Gn 2:3). We, however, eat and drink during the great fast; we rejoice in it and celebrate in the same manner as on the day of Sunday, because fasting is mourning, while food is joy. The day of the Sabbath is always a day of blessing and sanctification. It is the duty of all the faithful, men and women, to strive and receive communion and guard their souls on it, in the same manner as on the day of Sunday. Those who think that the day of the Sabbath is not calculated within Lent do so because they have erred in the calculation between the first week of the fast and the week of the passion, which is the week of Easter.


[p. 328] {#po.57.p328}

### The fast of Heraclius and Holy Week
እስመ ፣ ሱባዔ ፡ ጥንተ ፡ ጾም ፡ አኮ ፡ እምዐቢይ ፡ ጾም ፡ አላ ፡ የንሕነሰ፣ ንጸውማ፤ ፡ ንስሐ" ፣ በእንተ ፣ ሕርቃል ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ቀተሎሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡ ወበልዐ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ዘተካየዶሙ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ፅውቅ ፡ ብዜናሁ ፡ ለሕርቃል ፣ንጉሥ፣ ወሱባዔ ፡ ፍሥሕ ፣ ኢይትጐለቀ- ፡ ምስለ ፡ ህቢይ ፡ ጾም" ። አላ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ንጸ ውማ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ሕማማተ ፣ መድኃኒነ ። ንወጥን' ፡ ባቲ ፡ እምዕለተ ፡ ቀዳም ፡ ዘሆሳዕና ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ቀዳም ፡ ዘብርሃን ። ይከውን' ፣ ርእሰ ፡ ጓ ፡ መዓልት፣ ዘጾመ ፣እግዚእነ ፡በእንቲአነ ፡እምዕለተ ፡ሰኑይ ፣ዘዳግም ፡ጾም ፣ወተፍጻሜታ"፣ ጽባሕ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ቀዳም ፡ ዘሆሳዕና ፡ ትከውን ፡ ጓ ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወ ግ ፡ ሌሊተ ፣ ንጸ ውማጻ ፣ ሌሊተ ፡ ወመዓልተ ፡ ኢንጹም ፡ እመብልዕ ፡ አላ ፡ ‹እምፍትወት ፡ እንስሳ ዊት" ። እስመ ፣መብልዐ" ፡ኢንክል ፡ንጹም ፡እምኔሁ ፡ዘእንበለ ፡እስከ ፣ሰርክ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ድካመ" ፡ ሥጋነ ። ወይደልወነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንጹም ፡ እምፍ ትወት" ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወመዓልተ'?፤ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ጓ ፡ መዓልት ፡ወ % ፡ ሌሊት ። ወእምጽባሕ ፡ዕለተ ፡ቀዳም ፡ዘሆሳዕና ፡ንዌጥን ፡በሐዘን ፡ወጾም ፡፣በእንተ ፣ ሕማመ” ፡ መድኅቴኒነ” ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ጾም ፡የዐቢ"' ፡ ወይከብር፡፡ ፡ እምዐቢይ ፡ጾም ። ”ወዝንቱ ፡ጾም ፡በእንተ ፣ ሕማመ” ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ ኢይደመር ፣
19 Indeed, the week of the beginning of the fast does not belong to Lent; we fast in penance because of King Heraclius, who killed the Jews and broke the pact he had stipulated with them; this is well known in the chronicle of King Heraclius. The week of Easter is not calculated within Lent, but we fast it because of the passion of our Savior. We begin it from the day of Palm Saturday until the day of the Saturday of Light. The beginning of the forty days that our Lord fasted (Mt 4:2) for us is from the Monday of the second fast, and its conclusion is the morning of the day of Palm Saturday: these will be forty days and forty nights that we fast night and day. We do not fast from food, but from animal lust, because we cannot abstain from food, except every day, until evening, due to the weakness of our body. We must fast from lust, night and day, for the duration of those forty days and forty nights. History of the Lenten fast from the apostolic era until two hundred years later 20 From the morning of Palm Saturday, we begin with contrition and fasting because of the passion of our Savior for us, because this fast is greater and more worthy of honor than Lent. This

ምስለ ፡ዐቢይ ፡ጾም፤ ፡እስመ ፡በቀዳሚ ፡ኮኑ ፡መሃይምናን ፡ሶበ ፡ያብዕሉ ፡በዓለ፣ ጥምቀት ፡ይጸውሙ፡ ፡እምቅድሜሁ! ፡ አሐተ ፡ዕለተ ። እስመ ፡እግዚእነ ፡ጾመ፡ ፡ ፡ የዕለት ፡ እምዕለተ ፣ ጥምቀት ፡ እስከ ፣ ይትፌጸም ፡ቫግ ፡ መዓልት ። ወከማሁ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ነበርነ ፡ ንጸውም ፡ በከመ ፡ ተመሀርነ' ፡ ሕገ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት፤ ፡ እምሐዋር ያት ፣ አላ ፣ ኢነበርነ ፡ ንጸውም ፡ ፅለተ'" ፣ ጥምቀት ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በዓለ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ወኢይደሉ፤፤ ፡ ቦቴ ፡ ጾም፤፡ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ ነበርነ ፡ ንጸውም ፡ አሐተ ፣፣ ፅለተ"፤ ፡፣ እምቅድመ ፡ ዕለተ" ፡ ጥምቀት ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ዕለት! ፡ ጥምቀት ። ወዓዲ ፡ ናብዕል ፡በዕለተ ፡ጥምቀት ፡፣ወንጸውም ፡በሳኒታሁ ፡እስከ ፡ይትፌጸም" ፡ቋወ፻'፣ መዓልት ። ወይትወሰክ ፡ላዕሌሃ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ዘእምቅድመ ፣ጥምቀት ። "ንጸውም ፡ እምፍትወታት” ፡ ወእመብልዕ ፡ በከመ ፡ አጠየቁከ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይትፌጸም” ፡ ዛ ፡ መዓልት ፡ ወትረ ፡ ወተፍጻሜታ” ፡ አመ'፤ ፡ ዕሥራሁ” ፣ ለወ ርኃ ፡ የካቲት ፣ ንበልዕ ፡ በሳኒታሁ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በዓል ። ወንበልዕ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እስከ ፡ ወርኀ"' ፣ ኔሳን ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ናእተ” ፡ አይሁድ ። ንጸውም” ፣ እምዕለተ ፡ ቀዳም ፡ ዘሆ ሳዕና ፣ ዘይሰመይ ፡ ቀዳም” ፡ ዘአልዐዛር ፡ በእንተ ፣ ሕማመ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እስከ ፡ ፅለተ ፡ቀዳም ፡ዘብርሃን ። ንጸውም ፡ሱባዔ ፣ባሕቲቱ”' ፡ወናብዕል ፡በዓለ ፡ፋሲካ ። ነበርነ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ንገብር ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዕርገተ” ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡እስከ ፡መጠነ ፡፪፻ ፡ዓመት ።
fast for the passion of our Lord is not added to Lent, because at the beginning, when the faithful celebrated the feast of the Baptism, they fasted one day before it. Indeed, our Lord fasted forty days, from the day of the Baptism until the end of the forty days. Similarly, we used to fast according to the Christian law that we learned from the apostles, but they did not fast on the day of the Baptism because it is the feast of our Lord, and on it, one must not fast. We used to fast only one day, before the day of the Baptism, in place of the day of the Baptism, and we celebrate on the day of the Baptism and fast the day after, until the end of the thirty-nine days, and to it is added that day which precedes the baptism. 21 We fast from lusts and from food, just as I have clarified to you. At the end of the forty days, and its conclusion is on the twentieth day of Yakkātit, we eat the day after, without a feast, and we eat like this until the month of Nīsān, in which is the unleavened bread of the Jews. We fast from the day of Palm Saturday, which is called the Saturday of Lazarus, because of the Passion of our Lord, until the day of the Saturday of Light. We fast only one week and we celebrate the feast of Easter. We used to do this, after the Ascension of our Lord Jesus Christ, until the span of two hundred years.

### Problems of the previous Lenten calendar

22 In the countryside, there were many people who did not know the calculation, and the Holy Week of our Savior escaped them, and they did not know that it was known through the calculation of the moon. The fathers, teachers of the church, saw that in this way the people of the countryside would go to ruin, and they moved the forty-day fast and made it approach the Holy Week of our Savior, so that the fast of our Savior would not be destroyed by the people of the countryside. They said: “It is not necessary that the forty days of fasting pass without the people of the countryside knowing it.” They, in fact, knew the forty-day fast of the holy Easter. For this reason, the beginning of the forty days of fasting was on the second Monday of the fast of the Egyptians and the Melkites. Its conclusion is the morning of the Saturday of Lazarus. From that day, we begin in the morning the fast of the passion of our Savior until the evening of the day of the Saturday of Light. Calendar of Lent of the various Christian communities 23 This is the fast of the Egyptians and the Melkites, while the Greeks, (that is) the people of Constantinople who are called Rūm, even if they are not Rūm, because Rūm are the Afranj (that is) the people of Rome; they fast from the beginning of the second week of the fast. They do not fast the week that is known as Heraclius, and their fast is of forty days until the day of the Saturday of Lazarus, and it is followed by the fast of our Savior
ወይተልዎ ፡ ጾመ፤ ፡ ሕማማተ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እስከ ፡ ምሴተ፡ ፡ ቀዳም ፡ ዘብርሃን ። ወሮምሰ ፡እሉ ፡እሙንቱ ፡አፍርንጊ ፡ይበልዑ ፡ዕለተ ፡ሰኑይ ፡ወዕለተ፤ ፣፡ወሠሉስ፣ ዘይወጥኑ ፣ ቦሙ ፣ ጸመ ፡ ዮናናዊያን ፡ ወአርመን ፡ ወሶርያ ፡፣ ወይጸውሙ ፡ እም ረቡዕ ፣ ወይከውኑ ፣ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ ዓ ፡ መዓልት ፡ ዘይጸውሙ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ሆሳዕና ። ወእምሳኒታሁ ፡ይወጥን' ፡ጾመ ፡ሕማማተ ፡መድኃኒነ ፡እስመ ፡እሙንቱ ፡ይቤሉ ፣ ከመ ፣ ጸዶመ ፡ ሕማማተ ፣ መድኃኒነ ፡ ጥንቱ” ፡ ዕለተ" ፣ ሰኑይ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ ፅለተ ፡ ቀዳም ፡ዘአልዓዛር ። ወኩሎሙ ፡ፅሩያን ።
until the evening of the Saturday of Light. As for the Rūm who are the Afranj, they eat on the day of Monday and the day of Tuesday, on which the Greeks and Armenians and Syrians begin the fast, and they fast from Wednesday, and it becomes the conclusion of the forty days that they fast on the day of Palms. From the day after, the fast of the Passion of our Savior begins. They (i.e., the Afranj) maintain that the fast of the Passion of our Savior is the beginning of the day of Monday and not the day of the Saturday of Lazarus, and they are all equal.

24 And I have declared this to you: that the Sabbath and the day of Sunday are among the number of the forty days of fasting. It is our duty to fast on them from lust, as on every day of fasting, because we must fast forty days and forty nights from lust and from every food that stirs up lust, that is, animal flesh. And it is not that every food that grows on the earth, even if there is heat within it, stirs up lust in their nature (i.e., of humans). The heat within them passes quickly. For this reason, they ordained that we eat the honey of the bee, and it is very hot, yet devoid of lust. The heat of that which does not contain lust passes quickly. Everything returns to its nature from which it came. Epilogue between exhortation and self-apology
ለቡ ፣ ዘንተ ፡ በእንተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአንብብ” ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘትረክብ፣ እምክርስቲያን ። ክርስቶስ” ፡ ይኩን ፡ ስምዐ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ኦእጉየ” ፣ እስመ ፡ አነ” ፡
25 Pay attention to this for God and read it to all the Christians you meet. Christ be my witness: O my brother, I have not illustrated this to you seeking
ኢከሠትኩ ፡ለከ ፡ዘንተ ፡ ኢእፈቅድ!፤ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ምክሐ ፡ ወኢውዳሴ ፣ ከንቱ ፡እስመ ፣ ዝንቱ ፣ ምግባራት ፡፣ ኢአልቦሙ፡፣ ምክሐ!፣ ወኢክብረ፣ ለዘመሀራ፤፣ አላ፣ ኃጢአት ፡ ይደልዎ ፡ ለዘ' ፡ ኢገብረ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወይደልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ፈድ ፋደ ፡ እምነ ፡ ዘይሜህር ። እስመ ፡ የብዙኀ ፣ ወሀብዎ ፣ ብዙኀ ፣ ይትኀሠሥዎ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ለዘመሀረ ፡ዘንተ ፡ምግባራተ ፡ምክሐ ፣፡ወኢክብረ ፡አላ ፡ይደልዎ ፡ኩነኔ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢገብረ ፡ ቦሙ ። አእምር ፡ ኦፍቁርየ ፡ ከመ ፡ አነ'' ፡ ኢጸሐፍኩ ፡ ለከ ዘንተ ፡እንዘ ፡እፈቅድ ፣ምክሐ ፡ወውዳሴ ፡አላ ፡ከመ ፡ታንብቦ!! ፡ወትትፈወስ' ፣ ቦቱ ፡ ወታነብቦ ፡ ለዘትረክቦ ፡ እምውሉደ ፡ ጥምቀት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትፈወሱ ፡ ቦቱ። ወእግዚአብሔር ፣ ይትኃሠሥ”፤፤ ፣ እምኔከ ፡ ወእምነ ፡ዘይከውን ፡በኀቤሁ ፡፣ዝንቱ መጽሐፍ ፡ ለእመ" ፡ ኢያንብቦሙ"፤ ፣ ‹ለኩሉ ፡ ዘይትጋብኡ ። ኀቤሁ!' ፡ እምው ሉደ ፡ ጥምቀት ። ወአነ ፡ ኢጸሐፍክዎሙ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በእንተ" ፡ ቅንአት ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ በእንተ" ፣ ኅዳጥ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ወሀኬቶሙ ፡ በመ ንግሥት ፡ዐቢይ ፡ዘለዓለም ፡እስመ ፡ወልደ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡ቤዘዎሙ ፡በርእሱ ፡ እስከ ፡ያበጽሖሙ ፣ለውእቱ ፡መንግሥት ። ሎቱ ፡ኩሉሌ” ፡ስብሐት ፡ወአኩቴት ፡ ወክብር ፡ ወዕበይ ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወመንፈሱ"' ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማሕየዊ ፡ ፅሩይ” ፣ ምስሌሁ ፡ በመለኮቱ” ፡ ይእዜኒ ፡ወዘልፈኒ ፡ወለዓለመ ፡ዓለም ፡አሜን ።”
in it boasting, nor vainglory, because these things contain no boast, nor honor for those who teach them, but it will be imputed as sin to him who does not fulfill it; rather, he must do even more than he who teaches. For to whom much has been given, much will be required (Lk 12:48). There is no boast, nor honor for those who teach these things, but he deserves condemnation if he does not fulfill them. Know, O my beloved, that I have not written this to you desiring boast and praise, but so that you may read it and be healed by it, and that you may read it to those born of baptism whom you meet, so that they may be healed by it. God will demand an account from you, and from him with whom this book is found, if he does not read it to those who gather with him among those born of baptism. I have not written them except for the great zeal for the benefit of the faithful of little understanding of this era and the neglect toward the great eternal kingdom. Indeed, the Son of God redeemed them with himself until he brought them to this kingdom. To him be all glory, praise, honor, greatness, and sanctification with his Father and his life-giving Holy Spirit, coequal with Him in his divinity, now and forever and unto the ages of ages. Amen.
